    22→def cmd_plan_describe(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    23→    """Set augmented description on findings."""
    24→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    25→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    26→        return
    27→
    28→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    29→    text: str = getattr(args, "text", "")
    30→
    31→    from desloppify.engine.plan import describe_finding
    32→
    33→    plan = load_plan()
    34→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    35→    if not finding_ids:
    36→        print_no_match_hint(patterns)
    37→        return
    38→
    39→    for fid in finding_ids:
    40→        describe_finding(plan, fid, text or None)
    41→    save_plan(plan)
    42→    print(colorize(f"  Set description on {len(finding_ids)} finding(s).", "green"))
    43→
    44→

    48→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    49→        return
    50→
    51→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])

    53→
    54→    from desloppify.engine.plan import annotate_finding
    55→
    56→    plan = load_plan()

    72→def cmd_plan_skip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    73→    """Skip findings — unified command for temporary/permanent/false-positive."""
    74→    from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.selection import (
    75→        show_attestation_requirement,
    76→        validate_attestation,
    77→    )
    78→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
    79→
    80→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    81→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    82→        return
    83→
    84→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    85→    reason: str | None = getattr(args, "reason", None)
    86→    review_after: int | None = getattr(args, "review_after", None)
    87→    permanent: bool = getattr(args, "permanent", False)
    88→    false_positive: bool = getattr(args, "false_positive", False)
    89→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
    90→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    91→
    92→    # Determine skip kind
    93→    if false_positive:
    94→        kind = "false_positive"
    95→    elif permanent:
    96→        kind = "permanent"
    97→    else:
    98→        kind = "temporary"
    99→
   100→    # Validate requirements for permanent/false_positive
   101→    if kind in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   102→        if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   103→            show_attestation_requirement(
   104→                "Permanent skip" if kind == "permanent" else "False positive",
   105→                attestation,
   106→                ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
   107→            )
   108→            return
   109→        if kind == "permanent" and not note:
   110→            print(
   111→                colorize("  --permanent requires --note to explain the decision.", "yellow"),
   112→                file=sys.stderr,
   113→            )
   114→            return
   115→
   116→    dry_run: bool = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
   117→
   118→    plan = load_plan()
   119→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   120→    if not finding_ids:
   121→        print_no_match_hint(patterns)
   122→        return
   123→
   124→    if dry_run:
   125→        print(colorize(f"  Dry run: {len(finding_ids)} item(s) would be skipped ({kind}):", "cyan"))
   126→        findings = state.get("findings", {})
   127→        for fid in finding_ids:
   128→            f = findings.get(fid, {})
   129→            det = f.get("detector", "?")
   130→            summary = f.get("summary", fid)
   131→            print(f"    {det}: {summary}")
   132→        return
   133→
   134→    # For permanent/false_positive: delegate to state layer for score impact
   135→    if kind in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   136→        from desloppify import state as state_mod
   137→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
   138→
   139→        state_file = state_path(args)
   140→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   141→        status = "wontfix" if kind == "permanent" else "false_positive"
   142→        resolved: list[str] = []
   143→        for fid in finding_ids:
   144→            resolved.extend(
   145→                state_mod.resolve_findings(
   146→                    state_data, fid, status, note or "", attestation=attestation
   147→                )
   148→            )
   149→        if resolved:
   150→            state_mod.save_state(state_data, state_file)
   151→
   152→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   153→    count = skip_items(
   154→        plan,
   155→        finding_ids,
   156→        kind=kind,
   157→        reason=reason,
   158→        note=note,
   159→        attestation=attestation,
   160→        review_after=review_after,
   161→        scan_count=scan_count,
   162→    )
   163→    save_plan(plan)
   164→
   165→    label = {"temporary": "Skipped", "permanent": "Wontfixed", "false_positive": "Marked false positive"}
   166→    print(colorize(f"  {label[kind]} {count} item(s).", "green"))
   167→    if review_after:
   168→        print(colorize(f"  Will re-surface after {review_after} scan(s).", "dim"))
   169→
   170→    # Nudge about synthesis staleness for review findings
   171→    has_review = any(
   172→        state.get("findings", {}).get(fid, {}).get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   173→        for fid in finding_ids
   174→    )
   175→    if has_review:
   176→        from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SYNTHESIS_ID
   177→        if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   178→            print(colorize(
   179→                "  Tip: review findings changed -- run "
   180→                "`desloppify plan synthesize` to update the meta-plan.",
   181→                "yellow",
   182→            ))

   185→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   186→    """Unskip findings — bring back to queue."""
   187→    state = command_runtime(args).state
   188→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   189→        return
   190→
   191→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   192→
   193→    plan = load_plan()
   194→    # For unskip we need to match against all statuses (skipped items may be wontfix/fp)
   195→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   196→    if not finding_ids:
   197→        print_no_match_hint(patterns)
   198→        return
   199→
   200→    count, need_reopen = unskip_items(plan, finding_ids)
   201→    save_plan(plan)
   202→
   203→    # Reopen permanent/false_positive items in state
   204→    if need_reopen:
   205→        from desloppify import state as state_mod
   206→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
   207→
   208→        state_file = state_path(args)
   209→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   210→        reopened: list[str] = []
   211→        for fid in need_reopen:
   212→            reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_findings(state_data, fid, "open"))
   213→        if reopened:
   214→            state_mod.save_state(state_data, state_file)
   215→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} finding(s) in state.", "dim"))
   216→
   217→    print(colorize(f"  Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue.", "green"))
   218→
   219→
   220→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   221→# Reopen
   222→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   223→
   224→def cmd_plan_reopen(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   225→    """Reopen resolved findings from plan context."""
   226→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   227→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
   228→
   229→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   230→
   231→    state_file = state_path(args)
   232→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   233→
   234→    reopened: list[str] = []
   235→    for pattern in patterns:
   236→        reopened.extend(
   237→            state_mod.resolve_findings(state_data, pattern, "open")
   238→        )
   239→
   240→    if not reopened:
   241→        print(colorize("  No resolved findings matching: " + " ".join(patterns), "yellow"))
   242→        return
   243→
   244→    state_mod.save_state(state_data, state_file)
   245→
   246→    # Remove from skipped if present, and ensure all reopened IDs are in queue
   247→    plan = load_plan()
   248→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   249→    count = 0
   250→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   251→    for fid in reopened:
   252→        if fid in skipped:
   253→            skipped.pop(fid)
   254→            count += 1
   255→        if fid not in order:
   256→            plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   257→            order.add(fid)
   258→            count += 1
   259→    if count:
   260→        save_plan(plan)
   261→
   262→    print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} finding(s).", "green"))
   263→    if count:
   264→        print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: items moved back to queue.", "dim"))
   265→
   266→
   267→def cmd_plan_done(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   268→    """Mark findings as fixed — delegates to cmd_resolve for rich UX."""
   269→    from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.selection import (
   270→        show_attestation_requirement,
   271→        validate_attestation,
   272→    )
   273→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
   274→
   275→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   276→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   277→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   278→
   279→    # Pre-validate attestation before delegating (avoids stale hint in resolve)
   280→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   281→        show_attestation_requirement("Plan done", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
   282→        return
   283→
   284→    # Build a Namespace that cmd_resolve expects
   285→    resolve_args = argparse.Namespace(
   286→        status="fixed",
   287→        patterns=patterns,
   288→        note=note,
   289→        attest=attestation,
   290→        confirm_batch_wontfix=False,
   291→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   292→        lang=getattr(args, "lang", None),
   293→        path=getattr(args, "path", None),
   294→        exclude=getattr(args, "exclude", None),
   295→    )
   296→
   297→    from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.cmd import cmd_resolve
   298→
   299→    cmd_resolve(resolve_args)
   300→
   301→
   302→def cmd_plan_focus(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   303→    """Set or clear the active cluster focus."""
   304→    clear_flag = getattr(args, "clear", False)
   305→    cluster_name: str | None = getattr(args, "cluster_name", None)
   306→
   307→    plan = load_plan()
   308→    if clear_flag:
   309→        clear_focus(plan)
   310→        save_plan(plan)
   311→        print(colorize("  Focus cleared.", "green"))
   312→        return
   313→
   314→    if not cluster_name:
   315→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   316→        if active:
   317→            print(f"  Focused on: {active}")
   318→        else:
   319→            print("  No active focus.")
   320→        return
   321→
   322→    try:
   323→        set_focus(plan, cluster_name)
   324→    except ValueError as ex:
   325→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   326→        return
   327→    save_plan(plan)
   328→    print(colorize(f"  Focused on: {cluster_name}", "green"))
   329→

1→"""Plan schema types, defaults, and validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, Required, TypedDict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
     8→
     9→PLAN_VERSION = 4
    10→
    11→VALID_SKIP_KINDS = {"temporary", "permanent", "false_positive", "synthesized_out"}
    12→
    13→VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS = frozenset({
    14→    "delete", "merge", "flatten", "enforce",
    15→    "simplify", "decompose", "extract", "inline",
    16→})
    17→
    18→EPIC_PREFIX = "epic/"
    19→
    20→
    21→class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    22→    finding_id: Required[str]
    23→    kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
    24→    reason: str | None
    25→    note: str | None  # required for permanent (wontfix note)
    26→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    27→    created_at: str
    28→    review_after: int | None  # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
    29→    skipped_at_scan: int  # state.scan_count when skipped
    30→    original_position: int | None  # queue position before skip (for restore)
    31→
    32→
    33→class ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False):
    34→    finding_id: Required[str]
    35→    description: str | None
    36→    note: str | None
    37→    cluster: str | None
    38→    created_at: str
    39→    updated_at: str
    40→
    41→
    42→class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    43→    name: Required[str]
    44→    description: str | None
    45→    finding_ids: list[str]
    46→    created_at: str
    47→    updated_at: str
    48→    auto: bool  # True for auto-generated clusters
    49→    cluster_key: str  # Deterministic grouping key (for regeneration)
    50→    action: str | None  # Primary resolution command/guidance text
    51→    user_modified: bool  # True when user manually edits membership
    52→    # Epic fields (optional — only present on synthesis-created clusters)
    53→    thesis: str | None
    54→    direction: str | None  # One of VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS
    55→    root_cause: str | None
    56→    supersedes: list[str]
    57→    dismissed: list[str]
    58→    agent_safe: bool
    59→    dependency_order: int
    60→    action_steps: list[str]
    61→    source_clusters: list[str]
    62→    status: str | None  # "pending" | "in_progress" | "completed"
    63→    synthesis_version: int
    64→
    65→
    66→class EpicSynthesisMeta(TypedDict, total=False):
    67→    synthesized_ids: list[str]
    68→    last_run: str
    69→    version: int
    70→    dismissed_ids: list[str]
    71→    finding_snapshot_hash: str
    72→    strategy_summary: str
    73→    trigger: str
    74→
    75→
    76→class SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    77→    original_id: Required[str]
    78→    original_detector: str
    79→    original_file: str
    80→    original_summary: str
    81→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
    82→    superseded_at: str
    83→    remapped_to: str | None
    84→    candidates: list[str]
    85→    note: str | None
    86→
    87→
    88→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    89→    version: Required[int]
    90→    created: Required[str]
    91→    updated: Required[str]
    92→    queue_order: list[str]
    93→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
    94→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
    95→    active_cluster: str | None
    96→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
    97→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
    98→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
    99→    epic_synthesis_meta: EpicSynthesisMeta
   100→    deleted_clusters: list[str]  # cluster_keys of deleted auto-clusters
   101→
   102→
   103→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   104→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   105→    now = utc_now()
   106→    return {
   107→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   108→        "created": now,
   109→        "updated": now,
   110→        "queue_order": [],
   111→        "deferred": [],
   112→        "skipped": {},
   113→        "active_cluster": None,
   114→        "overrides": {},
   115→        "clusters": {},
   116→        "superseded": {},
   117→        "epic_synthesis_meta": {},
   118→        "deleted_clusters": [],
   119→    }
   120→
   121→
   122→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   123→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   124→
   125→    Handles migrations:
   126→    - v1 → v2: deferred list → skipped dict
   127→    - v3 → v4: epics dict → synthesis-clusters in clusters dict
   128→    """
   129→    defaults = empty_plan()
   130→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   131→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   132→
   133→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   134→        plan["queue_order"] = []
   135→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
   136→        plan["deferred"] = []
   137→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
   138→        plan["skipped"] = {}
   139→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
   140→        plan["overrides"] = {}
   141→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
   142→        plan["clusters"] = {}
   143→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
   144→        plan["superseded"] = {}
   145→    if not isinstance(plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta"), dict):
   146→        plan["epic_synthesis_meta"] = {}
   147→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deleted_clusters"), list):
   148→        plan["deleted_clusters"] = []
   149→
   150→    # Migrate deferred → skipped (v1 → v2)
   151→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   152→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   153→    if deferred:
   154→        now = utc_now()
   155→        for fid in list(deferred):
   156→            if fid not in skipped:
   157→                skipped[fid] = {
   158→                    "finding_id": fid,
   159→                    "kind": "temporary",
   160→                    "reason": None,
   161→                    "note": None,
   162→                    "attestation": None,
   163→                    "created_at": now,
   164→                    "review_after": None,
   165→                    "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   166→                }
   167→        deferred.clear()
   168→
   169→    # Normalize cluster fields
   170→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
   171→        if isinstance(cluster, dict):
   172→            if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
   173→                cluster["finding_ids"] = []
   174→            cluster.setdefault("auto", False)
   175→            cluster.setdefault("cluster_key", "")
   176→            cluster.setdefault("action", None)
   177→            cluster.setdefault("user_modified", False)
   178→
   179→    # Migrate epics → clusters (v3 → v4)
   180→    old_epics = plan.pop("epics", None)
   181→    if old_epics and isinstance(old_epics, dict):
   182→        clusters = plan["clusters"]
   183→        now = utc_now()
   184→        for name, epic in old_epics.items():
   185→            if name not in clusters:
   186→                clusters[name] = {
   187→                    "name": name,
   188→                    "description": epic.get("thesis"),
   189→                    "finding_ids": list(epic.get("finding_ids", [])),
   190→                    "auto": True,
   191→                    "cluster_key": f"epic::{name}",
   192→                    "action": f"desloppify plan focus {name}",
   193→                    "user_modified": False,
   194→                    "created_at": epic.get("created_at", now),
   195→                    "updated_at": epic.get("updated_at", now),
   196→                    "thesis": epic.get("thesis"),
   197→                    "direction": epic.get("direction"),
   198→                    "root_cause": epic.get("root_cause"),
   199→                    "agent_safe": epic.get("agent_safe", False),
   200→                    "dependency_order": epic.get("dependency_order", 999),
   201→                    "action_steps": epic.get("action_steps", []),
   202→                    "status": epic.get("status", "pending"),
   203→                    "synthesis_version": epic.get("synthesis_version", 0),
   204→                    "supersedes": epic.get("supersedes", []),
   205→                    "dismissed": epic.get("dismissed", []),
   206→                    "source_clusters": epic.get("source_clusters", []),
   207→                }
   208→
   209→
   210→def synthesis_clusters(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   211→    """Return clusters that were created by epic synthesis (have a thesis)."""
   212→    return {
   213→        name: cluster
   214→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   215→        if cluster.get("thesis")
   216→    }
   217→
   218→
   219→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   220→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   221→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   222→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   223→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   224→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   225→
   226→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   227→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   228→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   229→    if overlap:
   230→        raise ValueError(
   231→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   232→        )
   233→
   234→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   235→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   236→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   237→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   238→            raise ValueError(
   239→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   240→            )
   241→
   242→
   243→__all__ = [
   244→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   245→    "EpicSynthesisMeta",
   246→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   247→    "Cluster",
   248→    "ItemOverride",
   249→    "PlanModel",
   250→    "SkipEntry",
   251→    "SupersededEntry",
   252→    "VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS",
   253→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   254→    "empty_plan",
   255→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   256→    "synthesis_clusters",
   257→    "validate_plan",
   258→]
   259→

1→"""Epic synthesis engine — LLM-driven meta-plan for review findings.
     2→
     3→Clusters review findings by root cause into epics, dismisses false positives,
     4→resolves contradictions, and orders by dependency. The synthesis is incremental:
     5→it updates existing epics rather than recreating from scratch.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import json
    11→import logging
    12→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    13→from typing import Any
    14→
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    16→    EPIC_PREFIX,
    17→    VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS,
    18→    Cluster,
    19→    PlanModel,
    20→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    21→    synthesis_clusters,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import review_finding_snapshot_hash
    24→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    25→
    26→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27→
    28→
    29→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    30→# Data types
    31→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→
    33→@dataclass
    34→class SynthesisInput:
    35→    """All data the LLM needs to produce/update epics."""
    36→
    37→    open_findings: dict[str, dict]       # id -> finding (review + concerns)
    38→    mechanical_findings: dict[str, dict]  # id -> finding (non-review, for context)
    39→    existing_epics: dict[str, Cluster]    # current synthesis-clusters to update
    40→    dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]      # for context
    41→    new_since_last: set[str]             # finding IDs new since last synthesis
    42→    resolved_since_last: set[str]        # finding IDs resolved since last
    43→    previously_dismissed: list[str]      # IDs dismissed in prior synthesis
    44→    synthesis_version: int               # next version number
    45→
    46→
    47→@dataclass
    48→class DismissedFinding:
    49→    """A finding the LLM says doesn't make sense."""
    50→
    51→    finding_id: str
    52→    reason: str
    53→
    54→
    55→@dataclass
    56→class ContradictionNote:
    57→    """Record of a resolved contradiction."""
    58→
    59→    kept: str
    60→    dismissed: str
    61→    reason: str
    62→
    63→
    64→@dataclass
    65→class SynthesisResult:
    66→    """Parsed and validated LLM synthesis output."""
    67→
    68→    strategy_summary: str
    69→    epics: list[dict]
    70→    dismissed_findings: list[DismissedFinding] = field(default_factory=list)
    71→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = field(default_factory=list)
    72→    priority_rationale: str = ""
    73→
    74→
    75→@dataclass
    76→class SynthesisMutationResult:
    77→    """What changed when synthesis was applied to the plan."""
    78→
    79→    epics_created: int = 0
    80→    epics_updated: int = 0
    81→    epics_completed: int = 0
    82→    findings_dismissed: int = 0
    83→    findings_reassigned: int = 0
    84→    strategy_summary: str = ""
    85→    synthesis_version: int = 0
    86→    dry_run: bool = False
    87→
    88→
    89→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    90→# Input collection
    91→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    92→
    93→def collect_synthesis_input(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> SynthesisInput:
    94→    """Gather all data needed for the synthesis LLM prompt."""
    95→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    96→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    97→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    98→    epics = synthesis_clusters(plan)
    99→
   100→    open_review: dict[str, dict] = {}
   101→    open_mechanical: dict[str, dict] = {}
   102→    for fid, f in findings.items():
   103→        if f.get("status") != "open":
   104→            continue
   105→        if f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns"):
   106→            open_review[fid] = f
   107→        else:
   108→            open_mechanical[fid] = f
   109→
   110→    synthesized_ids = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   111→    current_review_ids = set(open_review.keys())
   112→    new_since = current_review_ids - synthesized_ids
   113→    resolved_since = synthesized_ids - current_review_ids
   114→    previously_dismissed = list(meta.get("dismissed_ids", []))
   115→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
   116→
   117→    return SynthesisInput(
   118→        open_findings=open_review,
   119→        mechanical_findings=open_mechanical,
   120→        existing_epics=dict(epics),
   121→        dimension_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   122→        new_since_last=new_since,
   123→        resolved_since_last=resolved_since,
   124→        previously_dismissed=previously_dismissed,
   125→        synthesis_version=version,
   126→    )
   127→
   128→
   129→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   130→# Prompt construction
   131→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   132→
   133→_SYNTHESIS_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
   134→You are maintaining the meta-plan for this codebase. Your goal is to produce
   135→a coherent, prioritized strategy to address ALL open review findings.
   136→
   137→Your plan should:
   138→- Cluster findings by ROOT CAUSE, not by dimension or detector
   139→- Give each cluster (epic) a clear thesis: one imperative sentence
   140→- Order epics by dependency: what must be done first for later work to make sense
   141→- Dismiss findings that don't make sense, are contradictory, or are false positives
   142→- Mark which epics are agent-safe (can be executed mechanically) vs need human judgment
   143→- Avoid creating work that contradicts other work in the plan
   144→- Be ambitious but realistic — aim to resolve all findings coherently
   145→
   146→Available directions for epics: delete, merge, flatten, enforce, simplify, decompose, extract, inline.
   147→
   148→Available plan tools (the agent executing your plan has access to these):
   149→- `desloppify plan queue` — view all items in priority order
   150→- `desloppify plan focus epic/<name>` — focus the queue on one epic
   151→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --permanent --note "why" --attest "..."` — permanently dismiss
   152→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --note "revisit later"` — temporarily defer
   153→- `desloppify plan done <id> --note "what I did" --attest "..."` — mark resolved
   154→- `desloppify plan move <id> top|bottom|before|after <target>` — reorder
   155→- `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` — inspect a cluster
   156→- `desloppify scan` — re-scan after making changes to verify progress
   157→- `desloppify show review --status open` — see all open review findings
   158→
   159→Your output defines the ENTIRE work plan. Findings not assigned to any epic
   160→will remain in the queue as individual items. Dismissed findings will be
   161→removed from the queue with your stated reason.
   162→
   163→Respond with a single JSON object matching this schema:
   164→{
   165→  "strategy_summary": "2-4 sentence narrative: what the meta-plan says, top priorities, current state",
   166→  "epics": [
   167→    {
   168→      "name": "slug-name",
   169→      "thesis": "imperative one-liner",
   170→      "direction": "delete|merge|flatten|enforce|simplify|decompose|extract|inline",
   171→      "root_cause": "why this cluster exists",
   172→      "finding_ids": ["id1", "id2"],
   173→      "dismissed": ["id3"],
   174→      "agent_safe": true,
   175→      "dependency_order": 1,
   176→      "action_steps": ["step 1", "step 2"],
   177→      "status": "pending"
   178→    }
   179→  ],
   180→  "dismissed_findings": [
   181→    {"finding_id": "id", "reason": "why this finding doesn't make sense"}
   182→  ],
   183→  "contradiction_notes": [
   184→    {"kept": "finding_id", "dismissed": "finding_id", "reason": "why"}
   185→  ],
   186→  "priority_rationale": "why the dependency_order is what it is"
   187→}
   188→"""
   189→
   190→
   191→def build_synthesis_prompt(si: SynthesisInput) -> str:
   192→    """Build the user-facing prompt content with all finding data."""
   193→    parts: list[str] = []
   194→
   195→    # Section: existing epics
   196→    if si.existing_epics:
   197→        parts.append("## Existing Epics (update these, don't recreate)")
   198→        for name, epic in sorted(si.existing_epics.items()):
   199→            status = epic.get("status", "pending")
   200→            thesis = epic.get("thesis", "")
   201→            direction = epic.get("direction", "")
   202→            fids = epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   203→            parts.append(
   204→                f"- {name} [{status}] ({direction}): {thesis}"
   205→                f"\n  Findings: {', '.join(fids[:10])}"
   206→                f"{'...' if len(fids) > 10 else ''}"
   207→            )
   208→        parts.append("")
   209→
   210→    # Section: what changed
   211→    if si.new_since_last:
   212→        parts.append(f"## New findings since last synthesis ({len(si.new_since_last)})")
   213→        for fid in sorted(si.new_since_last):
   214→            f = si.open_findings.get(fid, {})
   215→            parts.append(f"- {fid}: {f.get('summary', '(no summary)')}")
   216→        parts.append("")
   217→
   218→    if si.resolved_since_last:
   219→        parts.append(f"## Resolved since last synthesis ({len(si.resolved_since_last)})")
   220→        for fid in sorted(si.resolved_since_last):
   221→            parts.append(f"- {fid}")
   222→        parts.append("")
   223→
   224→    # Section: all open review findings
   225→    parts.append(f"## All open review findings ({len(si.open_findings)})")
   226→    for fid, f in sorted(si.open_findings.items()):
   227→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   228→        suggestion = detail.get("suggestion", "")
   229→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
   230→        confidence = f.get("confidence", "medium")
   231→        file_path = f.get("file", "")
   232→        summary = f.get("summary", "")
   233→        parts.append(f"- [{confidence}] {fid}")
   234→        parts.append(f"  File: {file_path}")
   235→        if dimension:
   236→            parts.append(f"  Dimension: {dimension}")
   237→        parts.append(f"  Summary: {summary}")
   238→        if suggestion:
   239→            parts.append(f"  Suggestion: {suggestion}")
   240→    parts.append("")
   241→
   242→    # Section: dimension scores for context
   243→    if si.dimension_scores:
   244→        parts.append("## Dimension scores (context)")
   245→        for name, data in sorted(si.dimension_scores.items()):
   246→            if isinstance(data, dict):
   247→                score = data.get("score", "?")
   248→                strict = data.get("strict", score)
   249→                issues = data.get("issues", 0)
   250→                parts.append(f"- {name}: {score}% (strict: {strict}%, {issues} issues)")
   251→        parts.append("")
   252→
   253→    # Section: previously dismissed
   254→    if si.previously_dismissed:
   255→        parts.append(f"## Previously dismissed ({len(si.previously_dismissed)})")
   256→        parts.append("Maintain unless contradicted by new evidence.")
   257→        for fid in si.previously_dismissed:
   258→            parts.append(f"- {fid}")
   259→        parts.append("")
   260→
   261→    return "\n".join(parts)
   262→
   263→
   264→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   265→# Result parsing
   266→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   267→
   268→def parse_synthesis_result(raw: dict, valid_ids: set[str]) -> SynthesisResult:
   269→    """Parse and validate raw LLM output into a SynthesisResult.
   270→
   271→    Invalid finding IDs are silently dropped from epics and dismissals.
   272→    """
   273→    strategy_summary = str(raw.get("strategy_summary", ""))
   274→
   275→    epics: list[dict] = []
   276→    for raw_epic in raw.get("epics", []):
   277→        if not isinstance(raw_epic, dict):
   278→            continue
   279→        name = str(raw_epic.get("name", "")).strip()
   280→        if not name:
   281→            continue
   282→        # Validate direction
   283→        direction = str(raw_epic.get("direction", "simplify")).strip()
   284→        if direction not in VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS:
   285→            direction = "simplify"
   286→        # Filter to valid finding IDs
   287→        finding_ids = [
   288→            fid for fid in raw_epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   289→            if isinstance(fid, str) and fid in valid_ids
   290→        ]
   291→        dismissed = [
   292→            fid for fid in raw_epic.get("dismissed", [])
   293→            if isinstance(fid, str) and fid in valid_ids
   294→        ]
   295→        action_steps = [
   296→            str(s) for s in raw_epic.get("action_steps", [])
   297→            if isinstance(s, str)
   298→        ]
   299→
   300→        epics.append({
   301→            "name": name,
   302→            "thesis": str(raw_epic.get("thesis", "")),
   303→            "direction": direction,
   304→            "root_cause": str(raw_epic.get("root_cause", "")),
   305→            "finding_ids": finding_ids,
   306→            "dismissed": dismissed,
   307→            "agent_safe": bool(raw_epic.get("agent_safe", False)),
   308→            "dependency_order": int(raw_epic.get("dependency_order", 999)),
   309→            "action_steps": action_steps,
   310→            "status": str(raw_epic.get("status", "pending")),
   311→        })
   312→
   313→    dismissed_findings: list[DismissedFinding] = []
   314→    for d in raw.get("dismissed_findings", []):
   315→        if not isinstance(d, dict):
   316→            continue
   317→        fid = str(d.get("finding_id", ""))
   318→        if fid in valid_ids:
   319→            dismissed_findings.append(
   320→                DismissedFinding(finding_id=fid, reason=str(d.get("reason", "")))
   321→            )
   322→
   323→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = []
   324→    for c in raw.get("contradiction_notes", []):
   325→        if not isinstance(c, dict):
   326→            continue
   327→        contradiction_notes.append(ContradictionNote(
   328→            kept=str(c.get("kept", "")),
   329→            dismissed=str(c.get("dismissed", "")),
   330→            reason=str(c.get("reason", "")),
   331→        ))
   332→
   333→    priority_rationale = str(raw.get("priority_rationale", ""))
   334→
   335→    return SynthesisResult(
   336→        strategy_summary=strategy_summary,
   337→        epics=epics,
   338→        dismissed_findings=dismissed_findings,
   339→        contradiction_notes=contradiction_notes,
   340→        priority_rationale=priority_rationale,
   341→    )
   342→
   343→
   344→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   345→# Plan mutation
   346→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   347→
   348→def apply_synthesis_to_plan(
   349→    plan: PlanModel,
   350→    state: StateModel,
   351→    synthesis: SynthesisResult,
   352→    *,
   353→    trigger: str = "manual",
   354→) -> SynthesisMutationResult:
   355→    """Apply parsed synthesis result to the living plan.
   356→
   357→    1. Creates/updates synthesis-clusters in plan["clusters"]
   358→    2. Marks dismissed findings as synthesized_out skips
   359→    3. Reorders queue_order to group epic members by dependency_order
   360→    4. Updates epic_synthesis_meta with snapshot hash
   361→    """
   362→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   363→    now = utc_now()
   364→    result = SynthesisMutationResult()
   365→    result.strategy_summary = synthesis.strategy_summary
   366→
   367→    clusters = plan["clusters"]
   368→    skipped: dict = plan["skipped"]
   369→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   370→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   371→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
   372→    result.synthesis_version = version
   373→
   374→    # --- Update/create epics (as clusters with thesis) ----------------------
   375→    seen_epic_names: set[str] = set()
   376→    for epic_data in sorted(synthesis.epics, key=lambda e: e.get("dependency_order", 999)):
   377→        raw_name = epic_data["name"]
   378→        epic_name = f"{EPIC_PREFIX}{raw_name}" if not raw_name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX) else raw_name
   379→        seen_epic_names.add(epic_name)
   380→
   381→        existing = clusters.get(epic_name)
   382→        if existing and existing.get("thesis"):
   383→            # Update existing synthesis-cluster
   384→            existing["thesis"] = epic_data["thesis"]
   385→            existing["direction"] = epic_data["direction"]
   386→            existing["root_cause"] = epic_data.get("root_cause", "")
   387→            existing["finding_ids"] = epic_data["finding_ids"]
   388→            existing["dismissed"] = epic_data.get("dismissed", [])
   389→            existing["agent_safe"] = epic_data.get("agent_safe", False)
   390→            existing["dependency_order"] = epic_data["dependency_order"]
   391→            existing["action_steps"] = epic_data.get("action_steps", [])
   392→            existing["updated_at"] = now
   393→            existing["synthesis_version"] = version
   394→            existing["description"] = epic_data["thesis"]
   395→            # Don't overwrite in_progress status from agent
   396→            if existing.get("status") != "in_progress":
   397→                existing["status"] = epic_data.get("status", "pending")
   398→            result.epics_updated += 1
   399→        else:
   400→            # Create new synthesis-cluster
   401→            cluster: Cluster = {
   402→                "name": epic_name,
   403→                "description": epic_data["thesis"],
   404→                "finding_ids": epic_data["finding_ids"],
   405→                "auto": True,
   406→                "cluster_key": f"epic::{epic_name}",
   407→                "action": f"desloppify plan focus {epic_name}",
   408→                "user_modified": False,
   409→                "created_at": now,
   410→                "updated_at": now,
   411→                # Epic fields
   412→                "thesis": epic_data["thesis"],
   413→                "direction": epic_data["direction"],
   414→                "root_cause": epic_data.get("root_cause", ""),
   415→                "supersedes": [],
   416→                "dismissed": epic_data.get("dismissed", []),
   417→                "agent_safe": epic_data.get("agent_safe", False),
   418→                "dependency_order": epic_data["dependency_order"],
   419→                "action_steps": epic_data.get("action_steps", []),
   420→                "source_clusters": [],
   421→                "status": epic_data.get("status", "pending"),
   422→                "synthesis_version": version,
   423→            }
   424→            clusters[epic_name] = cluster
   425→            result.epics_created += 1
   426→
   427→    # --- Dismiss findings ---------------------------------------------------
   428→    dismissed_ids: list[str] = []
   429→    for df in synthesis.dismissed_findings:
   430→        fid = df.finding_id
   431→        dismissed_ids.append(fid)
   432→        # Remove from queue, add to skipped as synthesized_out
   433→        if fid in order:
   434→            order.remove(fid)
   435→        skipped[fid] = {
   436→            "finding_id": fid,
   437→            "kind": "synthesized_out",
   438→            "reason": df.reason,
   439→            "note": f"Dismissed by epic synthesis v{version}",
   440→            "attestation": None,
   441→            "created_at": now,
   442→            "review_after": None,
   443→            "skipped_at_scan": int(state.get("scan_count", 0)),
   444→        }
   445→        result.findings_dismissed += 1
   446→
   447→    # Also handle per-epic dismissed lists
   448→    for epic_data in synthesis.epics:
   449→        for fid in epic_data.get("dismissed", []):
   450→            if fid not in dismissed_ids and fid in order:
   451→                order.remove(fid)
   452→                dismissed_ids.append(fid)
   453→                skipped[fid] = {
   454→                    "finding_id": fid,
   455→                    "kind": "synthesized_out",
   456→                    "reason": f"Dismissed by epic synthesis v{version}",
   457→                    "note": None,
   458→                    "attestation": None,
   459→                    "created_at": now,
   460→                    "review_after": None,
   461→                    "skipped_at_scan": int(state.get("scan_count", 0)),
   462→                }
   463→                result.findings_dismissed += 1
   464→
   465→    # --- Reorder queue: epic findings grouped by dependency_order -----------
   466→    epic_finding_ids: set[str] = set()
   467→    epic_ordered_ids: list[str] = []
   468→    for epic_data in sorted(synthesis.epics, key=lambda e: e.get("dependency_order", 999)):
   469→        for fid in epic_data["finding_ids"]:
   470→            if fid not in epic_finding_ids and fid not in dismissed_ids:
   471→                epic_finding_ids.add(fid)
   472→                epic_ordered_ids.append(fid)
   473→
   474→    # Rebuild order: epic items first (by dependency), then non-epic items in original order
   475→    non_epic_items = [fid for fid in order if fid not in epic_finding_ids]
   476→    # Insert epic items at the front (after synthesis::pending if present)
   477→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SYNTHESIS_ID
   478→    new_order: list[str] = []
   479→    # Keep synthesis::pending at front if present (it will be pruned next sync)
   480→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in non_epic_items:
   481→        non_epic_items.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
   482→    new_order.extend(epic_ordered_ids)
   483→    new_order.extend(non_epic_items)
   484→    order.clear()
   485→    order.extend(new_order)
   486→
   487→    # --- Update synthesis meta -----------------------------------------------
   488→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   489→    open_review_ids = sorted(
   490→        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   491→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   492→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   493→    )
   494→
   495→    plan["epic_synthesis_meta"] = {
   496→        "synthesized_ids": open_review_ids,
   497→        "last_run": now,
   498→        "version": version,
   499→        "dismissed_ids": dismissed_ids,
   500→        "finding_snapshot_hash": current_hash,
   501→        "strategy_summary": synthesis.strategy_summary,
   502→        "trigger": trigger,
   503→    }
   504→    plan["updated"] = now
   505→
   506→    return result
   507→
   508→
   509→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   510→# Top-level orchestrator
   511→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   512→
   513→@dataclass
   514→class SynthesisDeps:
   515→    """Injectable dependencies for the synthesis engine."""
   516→
   517→    llm_call: Any = None  # Callable[[str, str], str] — (system, user) -> response
   518→
   519→
   520→def synthesize_epics(
   521→    plan: PlanModel,
   522→    state: StateModel,
   523→    *,
   524→    deps: SynthesisDeps | None = None,
   525→    dry_run: bool = False,
   526→    trigger: str = "manual",
   527→) -> SynthesisMutationResult:
   528→    """Run epic synthesis: collect input, call LLM, apply results.
   529→
   530→    If ``dry_run`` is True, collects input and builds prompt but does not
   531→    call the LLM or mutate the plan.
   532→
   533→    If ``deps.llm_call`` is None, returns a dry-run result with the prompt.
   534→    """
   535→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   536→    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, state)
   537→
   538→    prompt = build_synthesis_prompt(si)
   539→    valid_ids = set(si.open_findings.keys())
   540→
   541→    if dry_run or deps is None or deps.llm_call is None:
   542→        result = SynthesisMutationResult(dry_run=True)
   543→        result.strategy_summary = f"[dry-run] Prompt built with {len(si.open_findings)} findings"
   544→        return result
   545→
   546→    # Call LLM
   547→    try:
   548→        raw_response = deps.llm_call(_SYNTHESIS_SYSTEM_PROMPT, prompt)
   549→        raw_json = json.loads(raw_response)
   550→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   551→        logger.error("Epic synthesis LLM response parse error: %s", exc)
   552→        result = SynthesisMutationResult()
   553→        result.strategy_summary = f"Synthesis failed: {exc}"
   554→        return result
   555→
   556→    synthesis = parse_synthesis_result(raw_json, valid_ids)
   557→    return apply_synthesis_to_plan(plan, state, synthesis, trigger=trigger)
   558→
   559→
   560→__all__ = [
   561→    "DismissedFinding",
   562→    "SynthesisDeps",
   563→    "SynthesisInput",
   564→    "SynthesisMutationResult",
   565→    "SynthesisResult",
   566→    "apply_synthesis_to_plan",
   567→    "build_synthesis_prompt",
   568→    "collect_synthesis_input",
   569→    "parse_synthesis_result",
   570→    "synthesize_epics",
   571→]
   572→

1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    PlanModel,
     8→    SkipEntry,
     9→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    12→
    13→
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→# Helpers
    16→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17→
    18→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    19→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    20→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    21→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    22→    if finding_id in order:
    23→        order.remove(finding_id)
    24→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    25→
    26→
    27→def _resolve_position(
    28→    order: list[str],
    29→    position: str,
    30→    target: str | None = None,
    31→    offset: int | None = None,
    32→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    33→) -> int:
    34→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    35→
    36→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    37→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    38→    """
    39→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    40→
    41→    if position == "top":
    42→        return 0
    43→    if position == "bottom":
    44→        return len(order)
    45→
    46→    if position == "before" and target:
    47→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    48→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    49→                return i
    50→        return 0
    51→
    52→    if position == "after" and target:
    53→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    54→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    55→                return i + 1
    56→        return len(order)
    57→
    58→    if position == "up" and offset is not None:
    59→        if not finding_ids:
    60→            return 0
    61→        # Find current position of first item
    62→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    63→        current_idx = None
    64→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    65→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    66→            if item_id == first_id:
    67→                current_idx = i
    68→                break
    69→        if current_idx is None:
    70→            # Item not yet in order; treat as "insert at position offset from top"
    71→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
    72→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
    73→
    74→    if position == "down" and offset is not None:
    75→        if not finding_ids:
    76→            return len(order)
    77→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    78→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    79→        current_idx = None
    80→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    81→            if item_id == first_id:
    82→                current_idx = i
    83→                break
    84→        if current_idx is None:
    85→            return len(clean_order)
    86→        return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
    87→
    88→    return len(order)
    89→
    90→
    91→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    92→# Move items
    93→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    94→
    95→def move_items(
    96→    plan: PlanModel,
    97→    finding_ids: list[str],
    98→    position: str,
    99→    target: str | None = None,
   100→    offset: int | None = None,
   101→) -> int:
   102→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   103→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   104→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   105→
   106→    # Remove from skipped if present
   107→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   108→    for fid in finding_ids:
   109→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   110→
   111→    # Remove from current position in order
   112→    for fid in finding_ids:
   113→        if fid in order:
   114→            order.remove(fid)
   115→
   116→    # Resolve insertion point
   117→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   118→
   119→    # Insert in original order
   120→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   121→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   122→
   123→    return len(finding_ids)
   124→
   125→
   126→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   127→# Skip / unskip
   128→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   129→
   130→def skip_items(
   131→    plan: PlanModel,
   132→    finding_ids: list[str],
   133→    *,
   134→    kind: str = "temporary",
   135→    reason: str | None = None,
   136→    note: str | None = None,
   137→    attestation: str | None = None,
   138→    review_after: int | None = None,
   139→    scan_count: int = 0,
   140→) -> int:
   141→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   142→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   143→    now = utc_now()
   144→    count = 0
   145→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   146→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   147→    for fid in finding_ids:
   148→        # Record queue position before removal so unskip can restore it
   149→        original_pos: int | None = None
   150→        if fid in order:
   151→            original_pos = order.index(fid)
   152→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   153→        skipped[fid] = {
   154→            "finding_id": fid,
   155→            "kind": kind,
   156→            "reason": reason,
   157→            "note": note,
   158→            "attestation": attestation,
   159→            "created_at": now,
   160→            "review_after": review_after,
   161→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   162→            "original_position": original_pos,
   163→        }
   164→        count += 1
   165→    return count
   166→
   167→
   168→def unskip_items(
   169→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   170→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   171→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   172→
   173→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   174→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   175→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   176→    """
   177→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   178→    count = 0
   179→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   180→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   181→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   182→    to_restore_pos: list[tuple[str, int]] = []
   183→    to_restore_end: list[str] = []
   184→    for fid in finding_ids:
   185→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   186→        if entry is not None:
   187→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   188→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   189→            if fid not in order:
   190→                original_pos = entry.get("original_position")
   191→                if original_pos is not None:
   192→                    to_restore_pos.append((fid, original_pos))
   193→                else:
   194→                    to_restore_end.append(fid)
   195→            count += 1
   196→    # Insert position-aware items sorted by position; +i compensates for
   197→    # positions being recorded after earlier items were already removed.
   198→    to_restore_pos.sort(key=lambda x: x[1])
   199→    for i, (fid, pos) in enumerate(to_restore_pos):
   200→        order.insert(min(pos + i, len(order)), fid)
   201→    order.extend(to_restore_end)
   202→    return count, need_reopen
   203→
   204→
   205→def resurface_stale_skips(
   206→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   207→) -> list[str]:
   208→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   209→
   210→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   211→    """
   212→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   213→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   214→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   215→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   216→    for fid in list(skipped):
   217→        entry = skipped[fid]
   218→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   219→            continue
   220→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   221→        if review_after is None:
   222→            continue
   223→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   224→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   225→            skipped.pop(fid)
   226→            if fid not in order:
   227→                original_pos = entry.get("original_position")
   228→                if original_pos is not None:
   229→                    order.insert(min(original_pos, len(order)), fid)
   230→                else:
   231→                    order.append(fid)
   232→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   233→    return resurfaced
   234→

   240→def describe_finding(
   241→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   242→) -> None:
   243→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a finding."""
   244→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   245→    now = utc_now()
   246→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   247→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   248→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   249→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   250→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   251→
   252→
   253→def annotate_finding(
   254→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, note: str | None
   255→) -> None:
   256→    """Set or clear a note on a finding."""
   257→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   258→    now = utc_now()
   259→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   260→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   261→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   262→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   263→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now

   266→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   267→# Clusters
   268→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   269→
   270→def create_cluster(
   271→    plan: PlanModel,
   272→    name: str,
   273→    description: str | None = None,
   274→    action: str | None = None,
   275→) -> Cluster:
   276→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
   277→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   278→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
   279→        raise ValueError(
   280→            f"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}"
   281→        )
   282→    if name.startswith("epic/"):
   283→        raise ValueError(
   284→            f"Cluster names starting with 'epic/' are reserved for epics: {name!r}"
   285→        )
   286→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
   287→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
   288→    now = utc_now()
   289→    cluster: Cluster = {
   290→        "name": name,
   291→        "description": description,
   292→        "finding_ids": [],
   293→        "created_at": now,
   294→        "updated_at": now,
   295→        "auto": False,
   296→        "cluster_key": "",
   297→        "action": action,
   298→        "user_modified": False,
   299→    }
   300→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
   301→    return cluster
   302→
   303→
   304→def add_to_cluster(
   305→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   306→) -> int:
   307→    """Add finding IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
   308→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   309→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   310→    if cluster is None:
   311→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   312→
   313→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   314→    count = 0
   315→    now = utc_now()
   316→    for fid in finding_ids:
   317→        if fid not in member_ids:
   318→            member_ids.append(fid)
   319→            count += 1
   320→        # Update override to track cluster membership
   321→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   322→        if fid not in overrides:
   323→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   324→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
   325→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   326→
   327→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   328→    return count
   329→
   330→
   331→def remove_from_cluster(
   332→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   333→) -> int:
   334→    """Remove finding IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
   335→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   336→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   337→    if cluster is None:
   338→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   339→
   340→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   341→    now = utc_now()
   342→    count = 0
   343→    for fid in finding_ids:
   344→        if fid in member_ids:
   345→            member_ids.remove(fid)
   346→            count += 1
   347→        # Clear cluster from override
   348→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   349→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
   350→            override["cluster"] = None
   351→            override["updated_at"] = now
   352→
   353→    # Mark auto-clusters as user_modified when items are manually removed
   354→    if count > 0 and cluster.get("auto"):
   355→        cluster["user_modified"] = True
   356→
   357→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   358→    return count
   359→
   360→
   361→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
   362→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   363→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   364→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   365→    if cluster is None:
   366→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   367→
   368→    # Track deleted auto-cluster keys so auto_cluster_findings() won't regenerate them
   369→    if cluster.get("auto"):
   370→        cluster_key = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   371→        if cluster_key:
   372→            deleted_keys: list[str] = plan["deleted_clusters"]
   373→            if cluster_key not in deleted_keys:
   374→                deleted_keys.append(cluster_key)
   375→
   376→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   377→    now = utc_now()
   378→    for fid in orphaned:
   379→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   380→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   381→            override["cluster"] = None
   382→            override["updated_at"] = now
   383→
   384→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   385→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   386→
   387→    return orphaned
   388→
   389→
   390→def move_cluster(
   391→    plan: PlanModel,
   392→    cluster_name: str,
   393→    position: str,
   394→    target: str | None = None,
   395→    offset: int | None = None,
   396→) -> int:
   397→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   398→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   399→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   400→    if cluster is None:
   401→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   402→
   403→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   404→    if not member_ids:
   405→        return 0
   406→
   407→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)
   408→

   413→
   414→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
   415→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
   416→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   417→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
   418→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   419→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   420→

   428→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   429→# Reset
   430→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   431→
   432→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   433→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp."""
   434→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   435→    plan.clear()
   436→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan as _empty
   437→    for k, v in _empty().items():
   438→        plan[k] = v
   439→    plan["created"] = created
   440→
   441→
   442→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   443→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   444→
   445→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   446→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   447→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   448→    """
   449→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   450→    found = 0
   451→
   452→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   453→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   454→    for fid in finding_ids:
   455→        was_present = False
   456→        if fid in order:
   457→            order.remove(fid)
   458→            was_present = True
   459→        if fid in skipped:
   460→            skipped.pop(fid)
   461→            was_present = True
   462→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   463→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   464→            if fid in ids:
   465→                ids.remove(fid)
   466→                was_present = True
   467→        if was_present:
   468→            found += 1
   469→
   470→    return found
   471→
   472→
   473→__all__ = [
   474→    "add_to_cluster",
   475→    "annotate_finding",

## 1. How `plan done` Works

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py` (lines 267-299)

The `cmd_plan_done` function:
```python
def cmd_plan_done(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Mark findings as fixed — delegates to cmd_resolve for rich UX."""
    from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.selection import (
        show_attestation_requirement,
        validate_attestation,
    )
    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import ATTEST_EXAMPLE

    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)

    # Pre-validate attestation before delegating (avoids stale hint in resolve)
    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
        show_attestation_requirement("Plan done", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
        return

    # Build a Namespace that cmd_resolve expects
    resolve_args = argparse.Namespace(
        status="fixed",
        patterns=patterns,
        note=note,
        attest=attestation,
        confirm_batch_wontfix=False,
        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
        lang=getattr(args, "lang", None),
        path=getattr(args, "path", None),
        exclude=getattr(args, "exclude", None),
    )

    from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.cmd import cmd_resolve

    cmd_resolve(resolve_args)
```

**Key Points:**
- It is a lightweight wrapper that delegates to `cmd_resolve` from the resolve command
- It validates the attestation upfront to provide clear error messages
- It builds an `argparse.Namespace` with `status="fixed"` and passes it to `cmd_resolve`
- It requires patterns (finding IDs to mark as fixed), optional attestation, and optional note
- The actual resolution logic (moving items, state updates, plan updates) is handled by `cmd_resolve`, not by this function

## Conclusion

**The "main objective" or strategy field in the plan is:**
- **Location:** `plan["epic_synthesis_meta"]["strategy_summary"]`
- **Type:** `str`
- **Content:** A 2-4 sentence narrative produced by LLM synthesis describing what the meta-plan says, top priorities, and current state
- **Source:** Generated by the epic synthesis engine when `desloppify plan synthesize` is run (or triggered automatically)
- **Mutability:** Updated whenever synthesis runs; contains only narrative text (no structured objective list)

There is **no separate `objective` or `strategy` field** at the root of `PlanModel` — everything goes through `epic_synthesis_meta`.

> DEVELOPER

You are a systems-obsessed architect who thinks deeply about agent failure modes, state machines, and guardrails. You love formal verification, invariants, and making systems that are impossible to misuse rather than just hard to misuse. You think in terms of state transitions, preconditions, postconditions, and proof obligations.

Here is the problem you need to analyze:

## The Problem

We have a CLI tool called "desloppify" that tracks codebase health. It produces "findings" — issues an LLM-based review identified in the code. When enough findings accumulate, the tool enters a "planning phase" (synthesis::pending) where an AI agent is supposed to organize findings into coherent work arcs before starting to fix code.

The planning phase exists because review findings must be grouped by root cause into coherent work arcs before execution. Without this, agents fix individual findings in isolation, leading to contradictory changes, wasted effort, and thrashing between conflicting directions.

Currently, the planning phase is a single gate: the agent runs `plan synthesize` to see findings, organizes them into clusters using CLI commands, then marks the phase complete. The problem is that agents blow through this mechanically — they skim the findings, create superficial clusters based on keywords, and rush to the "complete" step without genuine analysis. One agent literally just cleared the gate token from the plan file directly because it couldn't figure out the completion command.

The specific failure modes we've observed:
1. Agent skims findings, clusters by surface keywords (file name, dimension name) instead of root cause
2. Agent doesn't notice contradictions between findings (two findings recommend opposite architectural directions)
3. Agent doesn't think about dependencies (fix A must happen before fix B, or they'll conflict)
4. Agent has no sequencing logic — everything gets random priority
5. Agent writes empty/generic strategies like "fix all issues" without reasoning
6. Agent treats the planning gate as bureaucracy to clear, not a genuine thinking step

The tool already has mechanics for organizing: create clusters, add findings to clusters, move clusters to set priority, focus on a cluster. What's missing is forcing the agent to actually THINK at each step.

## The Proposed Approach

Multi-stage planning with written reports at each stage. The agent must progress through stages in order, writing a mini-report at each one (minimum character count). The tool checks mechanical preconditions (clusters exist, findings are organized) AND requires the written report. Stages reset if the finding landscape changes.

## Your Task

Analyze this problem from your perspective. Consider:

1. What stages should exist? Don't just rubber-stamp the 5 proposed — think about what cognitive tasks actually need to happen for good planning, and where agents specifically fail.

2. For each stage, what should the report contain? What questions should the agent be forced to answer? What would a GOOD report look like vs. a phoned-in one?

3. What mechanical preconditions should the tool verify at each stage? What invariants should hold?

4. What are the minimum character counts? Think about what's actually needed to demonstrate genuine thought vs. what's gameable padding.

5. What state transitions matter? When should stages reset? Can stages be re-done? What happens if findings change mid-planning?

6. What are the failure modes of THIS system? How might an agent game multi-stage reports? What would you do about that?

7. Any stages you'd add or remove from the proposed 5 (assess, group, conflicts, order, strategy)?

Be specific and opinionated. Give concrete examples of good vs. bad reports. Think about edge cases.

Here is the problem you need to analyze:

## The Problem

We have a CLI tool called "desloppify" that tracks codebase health. It produces "findings" — issues an LLM-based review identified in the code. When enough findings accumulate, the tool enters a "planning phase" (synthesis::pending) where an AI agent is supposed to organize findings into coherent work arcs before starting to fix code.

The planning phase exists because review findings must be grouped by root cause into coherent work arcs before execution. Without this, agents fix individual findings in isolation, leading to contradictory changes, wasted effort, and thrashing between conflicting directions.

Currently, the planning phase is a single gate: the agent runs `plan synthesize` to see findings, organizes them into clusters using CLI commands, then marks the phase complete. The problem is that agents blow through this mechanically — they skim the findings, create superficial clusters based on keywords, and rush to the "complete" step without genuine analysis.

The deeper problem: planning is a creative, integrative cognitive task. The agent needs to see patterns, identify tensions, imagine sequences. But we're asking it to do this through a CLI interface designed for execution, not reflection. The tool speaks in commands and flags; planning requires narrative and judgment.

The specific failure modes:
1. Agent skims findings, clusters by surface keywords instead of root cause
2. Agent doesn't notice contradictions (two findings recommend opposite directions)
3. Agent doesn't think about dependencies or sequencing
4. Agent writes empty strategies without reasoning
5. Agent treats planning as bureaucracy to clear, not genuine thinking

## The Proposed Approach

Multi-stage planning with written reports at each stage. 5 stages: assess, group, conflicts, order, strategy. Each requires a mini-report with minimum character count. The tool checks mechanical preconditions and requires the written report before advancing.

## Your Task

Think about this from a perspective of what makes work genuine vs. performative. Consider:

1. Does the multi-stage approach risk turning planning into a MORE elaborate form of box-checking? 5 stages with character minimums could feel like filling out forms rather than thinking. How do you make each stage feel like it's genuinely aiding thought rather than taxing it?

2. What's the right grain size for stages? Too few and agents skip thinking. Too many and it becomes bureaucratic overhead that the agent resents and games. Where's the sweet spot?

3. For reports — what should they actually feel like? A character minimum is a blunt instrument. What would make a report genuinely useful (to the agent itself, to future agents, to human reviewers) rather than just proof-of-work?

4. Think about the NARRATIVE of planning. When a human plans well, what does that process actually look like? Is it really 5 discrete stages, or is it more iterative? How do you capture the essence of good planning without forcing an artificial structure?

5. What would make an agent WANT to plan well, rather than just comply with the structure? Can the tool make the planning phase feel rewarding — like the agent is building something it's proud of — rather than bureaucratic?

6. Consider the audience for these reports. Who reads them? If nobody reads them, they're performative. If the agent reads them back later during execution, they're functional. If a human reviews them, they're accountability. The purpose shapes what they should contain.

7. What stages would YOU design? Don't feel bound by the 5 proposed. Think about what cognitive moves actually matter for good planning, and how to structure prompts that elicit genuine reflection rather than compliance.

8. Think about the failure mode of the multi-stage system itself: an agent that writes eloquent, thoughtful-sounding reports that are actually generic and could apply to any codebase. How do you ensure specificity?

Be honest about tensions and tradeoffs. Don't just propose — also critique.

> AGENT

I've been sitting with this problem for a while, and I think the tension at its heart is more fundamental than it first appears. Let me work through it honestly.

## The Core Tension

The proposed solution — multi-stage planning with written reports — is trying to solve a problem of *cognition* with a tool designed for *process*. That's not necessarily wrong, but it's the kind of move that can easily make things worse in a specific way: it can turn shallow compliance into *elaborate* shallow compliance.

Right now the agent blows through planning in one step. The five-stage proposal means it blows through planning in five steps, each with a 500-character essay. You've traded a single rubber stamp for a form with five fields. The agent that was writing empty strategies will now write five paragraphs of plausible-sounding analysis that could apply to any codebase. Character minimums select for verbosity, not insight.

This isn't hypothetical — it's the default behavior of language models under bureaucratic prompting. We're *very good* at producing text that pattern-matches to "thoughtful analysis" without actually doing the cognitive work. The question is whether the structure can be designed to make genuine thinking the path of least resistance.

## What Actually Happens During Good Planning

When a human plans well, the process isn't five discrete stages. It's more like this:

1. **Immersion** — reading all the findings, letting patterns emerge without forcing them. This is slow and feels unproductive. It's where the agent most wants to skip ahead.

2. **Noticing** — something catches your attention. Two findings that seem unrelated but share a root cause. A tension between what one finding recommends and what another implies. A dependency nobody made explicit. These moments are specific and surprising — they couldn't be generated from a template.

3. **Structuring** — once you've noticed enough, a shape emerges. Groups form. An order suggests itself. This is the creative moment, and it's inherently non-linear.

4. **Testing** — you stress-test the structure. What happens if we do A before B? Does this cluster actually hold together, or did I group by keyword? What did I miss?

5. **Committing** — you write down the plan, and in writing it, discover the last few things you hadn't thought through.

The proposed five stages (assess, group, conflicts, order, strategy) roughly map to this, but they linearize something that's actually iterative, and they miss the crucial distinction: **the value is in the noticing, not the reporting**. A report about what you noticed is only valuable if the noticing actually happened.

## What I'd Actually Design

Let me read through the current codebase to understand what we're working with before proposing something concrete.
