```
    27→
    28→
    29→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    30→# Data types
    31→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→
    33→@dataclass
    34→class SynthesisInput:
    35→    """All data the LLM needs to produce/update epics."""
    36→
    37→    open_findings: dict[str, dict]       # id -> finding (review + concerns)
    38→    mechanical_findings: dict[str, dict]  # id -> finding (non-review, for context)
    39→    existing_epics: dict[str, Cluster]    # current synthesis-clusters to update
    40→    dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]      # for context
    41→    new_since_last: set[str]             # finding IDs new since last synthesis
    42→    resolved_since_last: set[str]        # finding IDs resolved since last
    43→    previously_dismissed: list[str]      # IDs dismissed in prior synthesis
    44→    synthesis_version: int               # next version number
    45→
    46→
    47→@dataclass
    48→class DismissedFinding:
    49→    """A finding the LLM says doesn't make sense."""
    50→
    51→    finding_id: str
    52→    reason: str
    53→
    54→
    55→@dataclass
    56→class ContradictionNote:
    57→    """Record of a resolved contradiction."""
    58→
    59→    kept: str
    60→    dismissed: str
    61→    reason: str
    62→
    63→
    64→@dataclass
    65→class SynthesisResult:
    66→    """Parsed and validated LLM synthesis output."""
    67→
    68→    strategy_summary: str
    69→    epics: list[dict]
    70→    dismissed_findings: list[DismissedFinding] = field(default_factory=list)
    71→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = field(default_factory=list)
    72→    priority_rationale: str = ""
    73→
    74→
    75→@dataclass
    76→class SynthesisMutationResult:
    77→    """What changed when synthesis was applied to the plan."""
    78→
    79→    epics_created: int = 0
    80→    epics_updated: int = 0
    81→    epics_completed: int = 0
    82→    findings_dismissed: int = 0
    83→    findings_reassigned: int = 0
    84→    strategy_summary: str = ""
    85→    synthesis_version: int = 0
    86→    dry_run: bool = False
    87→
    88→
    89→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    90→# Input collection
    91→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    92→
    93→def collect_synthesis_input(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> SynthesisInput:
    94→    """Gather all data needed for the synthesis LLM prompt."""
    95→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    96→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    97→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    98→    epics = synthesis_clusters(plan)
    99→
   100→    open_review: dict[str, dict] = {}
   101→    open_mechanical: dict[str, dict] = {}
   102→    for fid, f in findings.items():
   103→        if f.get("status") != "open":
   104→            continue
   105→        if f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns"):
   106→            open_review[fid] = f
   107→        else:
   108→            open_mechanical[fid] = f
   109→
   110→    synthesized_ids = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   111→    current_review_ids = set(open_review.keys())
   112→    new_since = current_review_ids - synthesized_ids
   113→    resolved_since = synthesized_ids - current_review_ids
   114→    previously_dismissed = list(meta.get("dismissed_ids", []))
   115→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
   116→
   117→    return SynthesisInput(
   118→        open_findings=open_review,
   119→        mechanical_findings=open_mechanical,
   120→        existing_epics=dict(epics),
   121→        dimension_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   122→        new_since_last=new_since,
   123→        resolved_since_last=resolved_since,
   124→        previously_dismissed=previously_dismissed,
   125→        synthesis_version=version,
   126→    )
   127→
   128→
   129→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   130→# Prompt construction
   131→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   132→
   133→_SYNTHESIS_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
   134→You are maintaining the meta-plan for this codebase. Your goal is to produce
   135→a coherent, prioritized strategy to address ALL open review findings.
   136→
   137→Your plan should:
   138→- Cluster findings by ROOT CAUSE, not by dimension or detector
   139→- Give each cluster (epic) a clear thesis: one imperative sentence
   140→- Order epics by dependency: what must be done first for later work to make sense
   141→- Dismiss findings that don't make sense, are contradictory, or are false positives
   142→- Mark which epics are agent-safe (can be executed mechanically) vs need human judgment
   143→- Avoid creating work that contradicts other work in the plan
   144→- Be ambitious but realistic — aim to resolve all findings coherently
   145→
   146→Available directions for epics: delete, merge, flatten, enforce, simplify, decompose, extract, inline.
   147→
   148→Available plan tools (the agent executing your plan has access to these):
   149→- `desloppify plan queue` — view all items in priority order
   150→- `desloppify plan focus epic/<name>` — focus the queue on one epic
   151→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --permanent --note "why" --attest "..."` — permanently dismiss
   152→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --note "revisit later"` — temporarily defer
   153→- `desloppify plan done <id> --note "what I did" --attest "..."` — mark resolved
   154→- `desloppify plan move <id> top|bottom|before|after <target>` — reorder
   155→- `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` — inspect a cluster
   156→- `desloppify scan` — re-scan after making changes to verify progress
   157→- `desloppify show review --status open` — see all open review findings
   158→
   159→Your output defines the ENTIRE work plan. Findings not assigned to any epic
   160→will remain in the queue as individual items. Dismissed findings will be
   161→removed from the queue with your stated reason.
   162→
   163→Respond with a single JSON object matching this schema:
   164→{
   165→  "strategy_summary": "2-4 sentence narrative: what the meta-plan says, top priorities, current state",
   166→  "epics": [
   167→    {
   168→      "name": "slug-name",
   169→      "thesis": "imperative one-liner",
   170→      "direction": "delete|merge|flatten|enforce|simplify|decompose|extract|inline",
   171→      "root_cause": "why this cluster exists",
   172→      "finding_ids": ["id1", "id2"],
   173→      "dismissed": ["id3"],
   174→      "agent_safe": true,
   175→      "dependency_order": 1,
   176→      "action_steps": ["step 1", "step 2"],
   177→      "status": "pending"
   178→    }
   179→  ],
   180→  "dismissed_findings": [
   181→    {"finding_id": "id", "reason": "why this finding doesn't make sense"}
   182→  ],
   183→  "contradiction_notes": [
   184→    {"kept": "finding_id", "dismissed": "finding_id", "reason": "why"}
   185→  ],
   186→  "priority_rationale": "why the dependency_order is what it is"
   187→}
   188→"""
   189→
   190→
   191→def build_synthesis_prompt(si: SynthesisInput) -> str:
   192→    """Build the user-facing prompt content with all finding data."""
   193→    parts: list[str] = []
   194→
   195→    # Section: existing epics
   196→    if si.existing_epics:
   197→        parts.append("## Existing Epics (update these, don't recreate)")
   198→        for name, epic in sorted(si.existing_epics.items()):
   199→            status = epic.get("status", "pending")
   200→            thesis = epic.get("thesis", "")
   201→            direction = epic.get("direction", "")
   202→            fids = epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   203→            parts.append(
   204→                f"- {name} [{status}] ({direction}): {thesis}"
   205→                f"\n  Findings: {', '.join(fids[:10])}"
   206→                f"{'...' if len(fids) > 10 else ''}"
   207→            )
   208→        parts.append("")
   209→
   210→    # Section: what changed
   211→    if si.new_since_last:
   212→        parts.append(f"## New findings since last synthesis ({len(si.new_since_last)})")
   213→        for fid in sorted(si.new_since_last):
   214→            f = si.open_findings.get(fid, {})
   215→            parts.append(f"- {fid}: {f.get('summary', '(no summary)')}")
   216→        parts.append("")
   217→
   218→    if si.resolved_since_last:
   219→        parts.append(f"## Resolved since last synthesis ({len(si.resolved_since_last)})")
   220→        for fid in sorted(si.resolved_since_last):
   221→            parts.append(f"- {fid}")
   222→        parts.append("")
   223→
   224→    # Section: all open review findings
   225→    parts.append(f"## All open review findings ({len(si.open_findings)})")
   226→    for fid, f in sorted(si.open_findings.items()):
   227→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   228→        suggestion = detail.get("suggestion", "")
   229→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
   230→        confidence = f.get("confidence", "medium")
   231→        file_path = f.get("file", "")
   232→        summary = f.get("summary", "")
   233→        parts.append(f"- [{confidence}] {fid}")
   234→        parts.append(f"  File: {file_path}")
   235→        if dimension:
   236→            parts.append(f"  Dimension: {dimension}")
   237→        parts.append(f"  Summary: {summary}")
   238→        if suggestion:
   239→            parts.append(f"  Suggestion: {suggestion}")
   240→    parts.append("")
   241→
   242→    # Section: dimension scores for context
   243→    if si.dimension_scores:
   244→        parts.append("## Dimension scores (context)")
   245→        for name, data in sorted(si.dimension_scores.items()):
   246→            if isinstance(data, dict):
   247→                score = data.get("score", "?")
   248→                strict = data.get("strict", score)
   249→                issues = data.get("issues", 0)
   250→                parts.append(f"- {name}: {score}% (strict: {strict}%, {issues} issues)")
   251→        parts.append("")
   252→
   253→    # Section: previously dismissed
   254→    if si.previously_dismissed:
   255→        parts.append(f"## Previously dismissed ({len(si.previously_dismissed)})")
   256→        parts.append("Maintain unless contradicted by new evidence.")
   257→        for fid in si.previously_dismissed:
   258→            parts.append(f"- {fid}")
   259→        parts.append("")
   260→
   261→    return "\n".join(parts)
   262→
   263→
   264→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   265→# Result parsing
   266→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   267→
   268→def parse_synthesis_result(raw: dict, valid_ids: set[str]) -> SynthesisResult:
   269→    """Parse and validate raw LLM output into a SynthesisResult.
   270→
   271→    Invalid finding IDs are silently dropped from epics and dismissals.
   272→    """
   273→    strategy_summary = str(raw.get("strategy_summary", ""))
   274→
   275→    epics: list[dict] = []
   276→    for raw_epic in raw.get("epics", []):
   277→        if not isinstance(raw_epic, dict):
   278→            continue
   279→        name = str(raw_epic.get("name", "")).strip()
   280→        if not name:
   281→            continue
   282→        # Validate direction
   283→        direction = str(raw_epic.get("direction", "simplify")).strip()
   284→        if direction not in VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS:
   285→            direction = "simplify"
   286→        # Filter to valid finding IDs
   287→        finding_ids = [
   288→            fid for fid in raw_epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   289→            if isinstance(fid, str) and fid in valid_ids
   290→        ]
   291→        dismissed = [
   292→            fid for fid in raw_epic.get("dismissed", [])
   293→            if isinstance(fid, str) and fid in valid_ids
   294→        ]
   295→        action_steps = [
   296→            str(s) for s in raw_epic.get("action_steps", [])
   297→            if isinstance(s, str)
   298→        ]
   299→
   300→        epics.append({
   301→            "name": name,
   302→            "thesis": str(raw_epic.get("thesis", "")),
   303→            "direction": direction,
   304→            "root_cause": str(raw_epic.get("root_cause", "")),
   305→            "finding_ids": finding_ids,
   306→            "dismissed": dismissed,
   307→            "agent_safe": bool(raw_epic.get("agent_safe", False)),
   308→            "dependency_order": int(raw_epic.get("dependency_order", 999)),
   309→            "action_steps": action_steps,
   310→            "status": str(raw_epic.get("status", "pending")),
   311→        })
   312→
   313→    dismissed_findings: list[DismissedFinding] = []
   314→    for d in raw.get("dismissed_findings", []):
   315→        if not isinstance(d, dict):
   316→            continue
   317→        fid = str(d.get("finding_id", ""))
   318→        if fid in valid_ids:
   319→            dismissed_findings.append(
   320→                DismissedFinding(finding_id=fid, reason=str(d.get("reason", "")))
   321→            )
   322→
   323→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = []
   324→    for c in raw.get("contradiction_notes", []):
   325→        if not isinstance(c, dict):
   326→            continue
   327→        contradiction_notes.append(ContradictionNote(
   328→            kept=str(c.get("kept", "")),
   329→            dismissed=str(c.get("dismissed", "")),
   330→            reason=str(c.get("reason", "")),
   331→        ))
   332→
   333→    priority_rationale = str(raw.get("priority_rationale", ""))
   334→
   335→    return SynthesisResult(
   336→        strategy_summary=strategy_summary,
   337→        epics=epics,
   338→        dismissed_findings=dismissed_findings,
   339→        contradiction_notes=contradiction_notes,
   340→        priority_rationale=priority_rationale,
   341→    )
   342→
   343→
   344→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   345→# Plan mutation
   346→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   347→
   348→def apply_synthesis_to_plan(
   349→    plan: PlanModel,
   350→    state: StateModel,
   351→    synthesis: SynthesisResult,
   352→    *,
   353→    trigger: str = "manual",
   354→) -> SynthesisMutationResult:
   355→    """Apply parsed synthesis result to the living plan.
   356→
   357→    1. Creates/updates synthesis-clusters in plan["clusters"]
   358→    2. Marks dismissed findings as synthesized_out skips
   359→    3. Reorders queue_order to group epic members by dependency_order
   360→    4. Updates epic_synthesis_meta with snapshot hash
   361→    """
   362→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   363→    now = utc_now()
   364→    result = SynthesisMutationResult()
   365→    result.strategy_summary = synthesis.strategy_summary
   366→
   367→    clusters = plan["clusters"]
   368→    skipped: dict = plan["skipped"]
   369→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   370→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   371→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
   372→    result.synthesis_version = version
   373→
   374→    # --- Update/create epics (as clusters with thesis) ----------------------
   375→    seen_epic_names: set[str] = set()
   376→    for epic_data in sorted(synthesis.epics, key=lambda e: e.get("dependency_order", 999)):
   377→        raw_name = epic_data["name"]
   378→        epic_name = f"{EPIC_PREFIX}{raw_name}" if not raw_name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX) else raw_name
   379→        seen_epic_names.add(epic_name)
   380→
   381→        existing = clusters.get(epic_name)
   382→        if existing and existing.get("thesis"):
   383→            # Update existing synthesis-cluster
   384→            existing["thesis"] = epic_data["thesis"]
   385→            existing["direction"] = epic_data["direction"]
   386→            existing["root_cause"] = epic_data.get("root_cause", "")
   387→            existing["finding_ids"] = epic_data["finding_ids"]
   388→            existing["dismissed"] = epic_data.get("dismissed", [])
   389→            existing["agent_safe"] = epic_data.get("agent_safe", False)
   390→            existing["dependency_order"] = epic_data["dependency_order"]
   391→            existing["action_steps"] = epic_data.get("action_steps", [])
   392→            existing["updated_at"] = now
   393→            existing["synthesis_version"] = version
   394→            existing["description"] = epic_data["thesis"]
   395→            # Don't overwrite in_progress status from agent
   396→            if existing.get("status") != "in_progress":
   397→                existing["status"] = epic_data.get("status", "pending")
   398→            result.epics_updated += 1
   399→        else:
   400→            # Create new synthesis-cluster
   401→            cluster: Cluster = {
   402→                "name": epic_name,
   403→                "description": epic_data["thesis"],
   404→                "finding_ids": epic_data["finding_ids"],
   405→                "auto": True,
   406→                "cluster_key": f"epic::{epic_name}",
   407→                "action": f"desloppify plan focus {epic_name}",
   408→                "user_modified": False,
   409→                "created_at": now,
   410→                "updated_at": now,
   411→                # Epic fields
   412→                "thesis": epic_data["thesis"],
   413→                "direction": epic_data["direction"],
   414→                "root_cause": epic_data.get("root_cause", ""),
   415→                "supersedes": [],
   416→                "dismissed": epic_data.get("dismissed", []),
   417→                "agent_safe": epic_data.get("agent_safe", False),
   418→                "dependency_order": epic_data["dependency_order"],
   419→                "action_steps": epic_data.get("action_steps", []),
   420→                "source_clusters": [],
   421→                "status": epic_data.get("status", "pending"),
   422→                "synthesis_version": version,
   423→            }
   424→            clusters[epic_name] = cluster
   425→            result.epics_created += 1
   426→
   427→    # --- Dismiss findings ---------------------------------------------------
   428→    dismissed_ids: list[str] = []
   429→    for df in synthesis.dismissed_findings:
   430→        fid = df.finding_id
   431→        dismissed_ids.append(fid)
   432→        # Remove from queue, add to skipped as synthesized_out
   433→        if fid in order:
   434→            order.remove(fid)
   435→        skipped[fid] = {
   436→            "finding_id": fid,
   437→            "kind": "synthesized_out",
   438→            "reason": df.reason,
   439→            "note": f"Dismissed by epic synthesis v{version}",
   440→            "attestation": None,
   441→            "created_at": now,
   442→            "review_after": None,
   443→            "skipped_at_scan": int(state.get("scan_count", 0)),
   444→        }
   445→        result.findings_dismissed += 1
   446→
   447→    # Also handle per-epic dismissed lists
   448→    for epic_data in synthesis.epics:
   449→        for fid in epic_data.get("dismissed", []):
   450→            if fid not in dismissed_ids and fid in order:
   451→                order.remove(fid)
   452→                dismissed_ids.append(fid)
   453→                skipped[fid] = {
   454→                    "finding_id": fid,
   455→                    "kind": "synthesized_out",
   456→                    "reason": f"Dismissed by epic synthesis v{version}",
   457→                    "note": None,
   458→                    "attestation": None,
   459→                    "created_at": now,
   460→                    "review_after": None,
   461→                    "skipped_at_scan": int(state.get("scan_count", 0)),
   462→                }
   463→                result.findings_dismissed += 1
   464→
   465→    # --- Reorder queue: epic findings grouped by dependency_order -----------
   466→    epic_finding_ids: set[str] = set()
   467→    epic_ordered_ids: list[str] = []
   468→    for epic_data in sorted(synthesis.epics, key=lambda e: e.get("dependency_order", 999)):
   469→        for fid in epic_data["finding_ids"]:
   470→            if fid not in epic_finding_ids and fid not in dismissed_ids:
   471→                epic_finding_ids.add(fid)
   472→                epic_ordered_ids.append(fid)
   473→
   474→    # Rebuild order: epic items first (by dependency), then non-epic items in original order
   475→    non_epic_items = [fid for fid in order if fid not in epic_finding_ids]
   476→    # Insert epic items at the front (after synthesis::pending if present)
   477→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SYNTHESIS_ID
   478→    new_order: list[str] = []
   479→    # Keep synthesis::pending at front if present (it will be pruned next sync)
   480→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in non_epic_items:
   481→        non_epic_items.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
   482→    new_order.extend(epic_ordered_ids)
   483→    new_order.extend(non_epic_items)
   484→    order.clear()
   485→    order.extend(new_order)
   486→
   487→    # --- Update synthesis meta -----------------------------------------------
   488→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   489→    open_review_ids = sorted(
   490→        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   491→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   492→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   493→    )
   494→
   495→    plan["epic_synthesis_meta"] = {
   496→        "synthesized_ids": open_review_ids,
   497→        "last_run": now,
   498→        "version": version,
   499→        "dismissed_ids": dismissed_ids,
   500→        "finding_snapshot_hash": current_hash,
   501→        "strategy_summary": synthesis.strategy_summary,
   502→        "trigger": trigger,
   503→    }
   504→    plan["updated"] = now
   505→
   506→    return result
   507→
   508→
   509→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   510→# Top-level orchestrator
   511→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   512→
   513→@dataclass
   514→class SynthesisDeps:
   515→    """Injectable dependencies for the synthesis engine."""
   516→
   517→    llm_call: Any = None  # Callable[[str, str], str] — (system, user) -> response
   518→
   519→
   520→def synthesize_epics(
   521→    plan: PlanModel,
   522→    state: StateModel,
   523→    *,
   524→    deps: SynthesisDeps | None = None,
   525→    dry_run: bool = False,
   526→    trigger: str = "manual",
   527→) -> SynthesisMutationResult:
   528→    """Run epic synthesis: collect input, call LLM, apply results.
   529→
   530→    If ``dry_run`` is True, collects input and builds prompt but does not
   531→    call the LLM or mutate the plan.
   532→
   533→    If ``deps.llm_call`` is None, returns a dry-run result with the prompt.
   534→    """
   535→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   536→    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, state)
   537→
   538→    prompt = build_synthesis_prompt(si)
   539→    valid_ids = set(si.open_findings.keys())
   540→
   541→    if dry_run or deps is None or deps.llm_call is None:
   542→        result = SynthesisMutationResult(dry_run=True)
   543→        result.strategy_summary = f"[dry-run] Prompt built with {len(si.open_findings)} findings"
   544→        return result
   545→
   546→    # Call LLM
   547→    try:
   548→        raw_response = deps.llm_call(_SYNTHESIS_SYSTEM_PROMPT, prompt)
   549→        raw_json = json.loads(raw_response)
   550→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   551→        logger.error("Epic synthesis LLM response parse error: %s", exc)
   552→        result = SynthesisMutationResult()
   553→        result.strategy_summary = f"Synthesis failed: {exc}"
   554→        return result
   555→
   556→    synthesis = parse_synthesis_result(raw_json, valid_ids)
   557→    return apply_synthesis_to_plan(plan, state, synthesis, trigger=trigger)
   558→
   559→
   560→__all__ = [
   561→    "DismissedFinding",
   562→    "SynthesisDeps",
   563→    "SynthesisInput",
   564→    "SynthesisMutationResult",
   565→    "SynthesisResult",
   566→    "apply_synthesis_to_plan",
   567→    "build_synthesis_prompt",
   568→    "collect_synthesis_input",
   569→    "parse_synthesis_result",
   570→    "synthesize_epics",
   571→]
   572→
```
```

1→"""Plan persistence — load/save with atomic writes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import shutil
     8→import sys
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    13→    PLAN_VERSION,
    14→    PlanModel,
    15→    empty_plan,
    16→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    17→    validate_plan,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import STATE_DIR, json_default
    20→
    21→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    22→
    23→PLAN_FILE = STATE_DIR / "plan.json"
    24→
    25→
    26→def load_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> PlanModel:
    27→    """Load plan from disk, or return empty plan on missing/corruption."""
    28→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    29→    if not plan_path.exists():
    30→        return empty_plan()
    31→
    32→    try:
    33→        data = json.loads(plan_path.read_text())
    34→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as ex:
    35→        print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    36→        return empty_plan()
    37→
    38→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    39→        print("  Warning: Plan file root must be a JSON object. Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    40→        return empty_plan()
    41→
    42→    version = data.get("version", 1)
    43→    if version > PLAN_VERSION:
    44→        print(
    45→            f"  Warning: Plan file version {version} is newer than supported "
    46→            f"({PLAN_VERSION}). Some features may not work correctly.",
    47→            file=sys.stderr,
    48→        )
    49→
    50→    ensure_plan_defaults(data)
    51→    try:
    52→        validate_plan(data)
    53→    except ValueError as ex:
    54→        print(f"  Warning: Plan invariants invalid ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    55→        return empty_plan()
    56→
    57→    return data  # type: ignore[return-value]
    58→
    59→
    60→def save_plan(plan: PlanModel | dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None:
    61→    """Validate and save plan to disk atomically."""
    62→    from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    63→
    64→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    65→    plan["updated"] = utc_now()
    66→    validate_plan(plan)
    67→
    68→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    69→    plan_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    70→
    71→    content = json.dumps(plan, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
    72→
    73→    if plan_path.exists():
    74→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    75→        try:
    76→            shutil.copy2(str(plan_path), str(backup))
    77→        except OSError as backup_ex:
    78→            logger.debug("Failed to create plan backup: %s", backup_ex)
    79→
    80→    try:
    81→        safe_write_text(plan_path, content)
    82→    except OSError as ex:
    83→        print(f"  Warning: Could not save plan: {ex}", file=sys.stderr)
    84→        raise
    85→
    86→
    87→def plan_path_for_state(state_path: Path) -> Path:
    88→    """Derive plan.json path from a state file path."""
    89→    return state_path.parent / "plan.json"
    90→
    91→
    92→def has_living_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> bool:
    93→    """Return True if a plan.json exists and has user intent."""
    94→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    95→    if not plan_path.exists():
    96→        return False
    97→    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
    98→    return bool(
    99→        plan.get("queue_order")
   100→        or plan.get("overrides")
   101→        or plan.get("clusters")
   102→    )
   103→
   104→
   105→__all__ = [
   106→    "PLAN_FILE",
   107→    "has_living_plan",
   108→    "load_plan",
   109→    "plan_path_for_state",
   110→    "save_plan",
   111→]
   112→
```

```
1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    PlanModel,
     8→    SkipEntry,
     9→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    12→
    13→
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→# Helpers
    16→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17→
    18→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    19→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    20→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    21→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    22→    if finding_id in order:
    23→        order.remove(finding_id)
    24→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    25→
    26→
    27→def _resolve_position(
    28→    order: list[str],
    29→    position: str,
    30→    target: str | None = None,
    31→    offset: int | None = None,
    32→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    33→) -> int:
    34→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    35→
    36→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    37→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    38→    """
    39→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    40→
    41→    if position == "top":
    42→        return 0
    43→    if position == "bottom":
    44→        return len(order)
    45→
    46→    if position == "before" and target:
    47→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    48→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    49→                return i
    50→        return 0
    51→
    52→    if position == "after" and target:
    53→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    54→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    55→                return i + 1
    56→        return len(order)
    57→
    58→    if position == "up" and offset is not None:
    59→        if not finding_ids:
    60→            return 0
    61→        # Find current position of first item
    62→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    63→        current_idx = None
    64→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    65→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    66→            if item_id == first_id:
    67→                current_idx = i
    68→                break
    69→        if current_idx is None:
    70→            # Item not yet in order; treat as "insert at position offset from top"
    71→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
    72→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
    73→
    74→    if position == "down" and offset is not None:
    75→        if not finding_ids:
    76→            return len(order)
    77→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    78→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    79→        current_idx = None
    80→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    81→            if item_id == first_id:
    82→                current_idx = i
    83→                break
    84→        if current_idx is None:
    85→            return len(clean_order)
    86→        return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
    87→
    88→    return len(order)
    89→
    90→
    91→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    92→# Move items
    93→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    94→
    95→def move_items(
    96→    plan: PlanModel,
    97→    finding_ids: list[str],
    98→    position: str,
    99→    target: str | None = None,
   100→    offset: int | None = None,
   101→) -> int:
   102→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   103→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   104→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   105→
   106→    # Remove from skipped if present
   107→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   108→    for fid in finding_ids:
   109→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   110→
   111→    # Remove from current position in order
   112→    for fid in finding_ids:
   113→        if fid in order:
   114→            order.remove(fid)
   115→
   116→    # Resolve insertion point
   117→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   118→
   119→    # Insert in original order
   120→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   121→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   122→
   123→    return len(finding_ids)
   124→
   125→
   126→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   127→# Skip / unskip
   128→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   129→
   130→def skip_items(
   131→    plan: PlanModel,
   132→    finding_ids: list[str],
   133→    *,
   134→    kind: str = "temporary",
   135→    reason: str | None = None,
   136→    note: str | None = None,
   137→    attestation: str | None = None,
   138→    review_after: int | None = None,
   139→    scan_count: int = 0,
   140→) -> int:
   141→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   142→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   143→    now = utc_now()
   144→    count = 0
   145→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   146→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   147→    for fid in finding_ids:
   148→        # Record queue position before removal so unskip can restore it
   149→        original_pos: int | None = None
   150→        if fid in order:
   151→            original_pos = order.index(fid)
   152→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   153→        skipped[fid] = {
   154→            "finding_id": fid,
   155→            "kind": kind,
   156→            "reason": reason,
   157→            "note": note,
   158→            "attestation": attestation,
   159→            "created_at": now,
   160→            "review_after": review_after,
   161→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   162→            "original_position": original_pos,
   163→        }
   164→        count += 1
   165→    return count
   166→
   167→
   168→def unskip_items(
   169→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   170→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   171→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   172→
   173→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   174→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   175→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   176→    """
   177→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   178→    count = 0
   179→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   180→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   181→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   182→    to_restore_pos: list[tuple[str, int]] = []
   183→    to_restore_end: list[str] = []
   184→    for fid in finding_ids:
   185→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   186→        if entry is not None:
   187→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   188→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   189→            if fid not in order:
   190→                original_pos = entry.get("original_position")
   191→                if original_pos is not None:
   192→                    to_restore_pos.append((fid, original_pos))
   193→                else:
   194→                    to_restore_end.append(fid)
   195→            count += 1
   196→    # Insert position-aware items sorted by position; +i compensates for
   197→    # positions being recorded after earlier items were already removed.
   198→    to_restore_pos.sort(key=lambda x: x[1])
   199→    for i, (fid, pos) in enumerate(to_restore_pos):
   200→        order.insert(min(pos + i, len(order)), fid)
   201→    order.extend(to_restore_end)
   202→    return count, need_reopen
   203→
   204→
   205→def resurface_stale_skips(
   206→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   207→) -> list[str]:
   208→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   209→
   210→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   211→    """
   212→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   213→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   214→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   215→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   216→    for fid in list(skipped):
   217→        entry = skipped[fid]
   218→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   219→            continue
   220→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   221→        if review_after is None:
   222→            continue
   223→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   224→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   225→            skipped.pop(fid)
   226→            if fid not in order:
   227→                original_pos = entry.get("original_position")
   228→                if original_pos is not None:
   229→                    order.insert(min(original_pos, len(order)), fid)
   230→                else:
   231→                    order.append(fid)
   232→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   233→    return resurfaced
   234→
   235→
   236→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   237→# Describe / annotate
   238→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   239→
   240→def describe_finding(
   241→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   242→) -> None:
   243→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a finding."""
   244→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   245→    now = utc_now()
   246→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   247→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   248→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   249→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   250→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   251→
   252→
   253→def annotate_finding(
   254→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, note: str | None
   255→) -> None:
   256→    """Set or clear a note on a finding."""
   257→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   258→    now = utc_now()
   259→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   260→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   261→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   262→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   263→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   264→
   265→
   266→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   267→# Clusters
   268→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   269→
   270→def create_cluster(
   271→    plan: PlanModel,
   272→    name: str,
   273→    description: str | None = None,
   274→    action: str | None = None,
   275→) -> Cluster:
   276→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
   277→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   278→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
   279→        raise ValueError(
   280→            f"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}"
   281→        )
   282→    if name.startswith("epic/"):
   283→        raise ValueError(
   284→            f"Cluster names starting with 'epic/' are reserved for epics: {name!r}"
   285→        )
   286→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
   287→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
   288→    now = utc_now()
   289→    cluster: Cluster = {
   290→        "name": name,
   291→        "description": description,
   292→        "finding_ids": [],
   293→        "created_at": now,
   294→        "updated_at": now,
   295→        "auto": False,
   296→        "cluster_key": "",
   297→        "action": action,
   298→        "user_modified": False,
   299→    }
   300→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
   301→    return cluster
   302→
   303→
   304→def add_to_cluster(
   305→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   306→) -> int:
   307→    """Add finding IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
   308→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   309→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   310→    if cluster is None:
   311→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   312→
   313→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   314→    count = 0
   315→    now = utc_now()
   316→    for fid in finding_ids:
   317→        if fid not in member_ids:
   318→            member_ids.append(fid)
   319→            count += 1
   320→        # Update override to track cluster membership
   321→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   322→        if fid not in overrides:
   323→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   324→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
   325→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   326→
   327→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   328→    return count
   329→
   330→
   331→def remove_from_cluster(
   332→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   333→) -> int:
   334→    """Remove finding IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
   335→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   336→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   337→    if cluster is None:
   338→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   339→
   340→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   341→    now = utc_now()
   342→    count = 0
   343→    for fid in finding_ids:
   344→        if fid in member_ids:
   345→            member_ids.remove(fid)
   346→            count += 1
   347→        # Clear cluster from override
   348→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   349→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
   350→            override["cluster"] = None
   351→            override["updated_at"] = now
   352→
   353→    # Mark auto-clusters as user_modified when items are manually removed
   354→    if count > 0 and cluster.get("auto"):
   355→        cluster["user_modified"] = True
   356→
   357→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   358→    return count
   359→
   360→
   361→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
   362→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   363→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   364→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   365→    if cluster is None:
   366→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   367→
   368→    # Track deleted auto-cluster keys so auto_cluster_findings() won't regenerate them
   369→    if cluster.get("auto"):
   370→        cluster_key = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   371→        if cluster_key:
   372→            deleted_keys: list[str] = plan["deleted_clusters"]
   373→            if cluster_key not in deleted_keys:
   374→                deleted_keys.append(cluster_key)
   375→
   376→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   377→    now = utc_now()
   378→    for fid in orphaned:
   379→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   380→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   381→            override["cluster"] = None
   382→            override["updated_at"] = now
   383→
   384→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   385→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   386→
   387→    return orphaned
   388→
   389→
   390→def move_cluster(
   391→    plan: PlanModel,
   392→    cluster_name: str,
   393→    position: str,
   394→    target: str | None = None,
   395→    offset: int | None = None,
   396→) -> int:
   397→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   398→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   399→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   400→    if cluster is None:
   401→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   402→
   403→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   404→    if not member_ids:
   405→        return 0
   406→
   407→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)
   408→
   409→
   410→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   411→# Focus
   412→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   413→
   414→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
   415→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
   416→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   417→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
   418→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   419→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   420→
   421→
   422→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   423→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
   424→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   425→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
   426→
   427→
   428→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   429→# Reset
   430→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   431→
   432→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   433→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp."""
   434→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   435→    plan.clear()
   436→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan as _empty
   437→    for k, v in _empty().items():
   438→        plan[k] = v
   439→    plan["created"] = created
   440→
   441→
   442→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   443→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   444→
   445→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   446→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   447→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   448→    """
   449→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   450→    found = 0
   451→
   452→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   453→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   454→    for fid in finding_ids:
   455→        was_present = False
   456→        if fid in order:
   457→            order.remove(fid)
   458→            was_present = True
   459→        if fid in skipped:
   460→            skipped.pop(fid)
   461→            was_present = True
   462→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   463→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   464→            if fid in ids:
   465→                ids.remove(fid)
   466→                was_present = True
   467→        if was_present:
   468→            found += 1
   469→
   470→    return found
   471→
   472→
   473→__all__ = [
   474→    "add_to_cluster",
   475→    "annotate_finding",
   476→    "clear_focus",
   477→    "create_cluster",
   478→    "delete_cluster",
   479→    "describe_finding",
   480→    "move_cluster",
   481→    "move_items",
   482→    "purge_ids",
   483→    "remove_from_cluster",
   484→    "reset_plan",
   485→    "resurface_stale_skips",
   486→    "set_focus",
   487→    "skip_items",
   488→    "unskip_items",
   489→]
```

```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import hashlib
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    20→SYNTHESIS_ID = "synthesis::pending"
    21→
    22→
    23→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    24→# Result dataclasses
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→
    27→@dataclass
    28→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    29→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    30→
    31→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    32→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→
    34→    @property
    35→    def changes(self) -> int:
    36→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    41→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    42→
    43→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    44→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    45→
    46→    @property
    47→    def changes(self) -> int:
    48→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    49→
    50→
    51→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52→# ID helpers
    53→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54→
    55→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    56→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    57→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    58→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    59→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    63→    if not dim_scores:
    64→        return set()
    65→
    66→    stale: set[str] = set()
    67→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    68→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    69→            continue
    70→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    71→        if dim_key:
    72→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    73→    return stale
    74→
    75→
    76→def _current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    77→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    78→
    79→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` directly because
    80→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` filters out placeholder dimensions
    81→    (they are hidden from the scorecard display).
    82→    """
    83→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    84→
    85→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    86→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    87→        return set()
    88→
    89→    unscored: set[str] = set()
    90→    for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    91→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    92→            continue
    93→        if not payload.get("placeholder"):
    94→            continue
    95→        if dim_key:
    96→            unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    97→    return unscored
    98→
    99→
   100→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   101→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   102→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   103→
   104→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   105→    plan: PlanModel,
   106→    state: StateModel,
   107→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   108→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   109→
   110→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   111→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   112→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   113→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   114→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   115→    """
   116→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   117→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   118→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   119→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   120→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   121→
   122→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   123→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   124→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   125→        fid for fid in order
   126→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   127→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   128→        and fid not in stale_ids
   129→    ]
   130→    for fid in to_remove:
   131→        order.remove(fid)
   132→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   133→
   134→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs to front ----------------------------
   135→    existing = set(order)
   136→    for uid in reversed(sorted(unscored_ids)):
   137→        if uid not in existing:
   138→            order.insert(0, uid)
   139→            result.injected.append(uid)
   140→
   141→    return result
   142→
   143→
   144→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   145→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   146→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   147→
   148→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   149→    plan: PlanModel,
   150→    state: StateModel,
   151→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   152→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
   153→
   154→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   155→       longer stale and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned by
   156→       ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   157→    2. If no objective items remain after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
   158→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
   159→    """
   160→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   161→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   162→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   163→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   164→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   165→
   166→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   167→    # Only prune IDs that are neither stale nor unscored.
   168→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   169→        fid for fid in order
   170→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   171→        and fid not in stale_ids
   172→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   173→    ]
   174→    for fid in to_remove:
   175→        order.remove(fid)
   176→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   177→
   178→    # --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------
   179→    has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
   180→    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
   181→        existing = set(order)
   182→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
   183→            if sid not in existing:
   184→                order.append(sid)
   185→                result.injected.append(sid)
   186→
   187→    return result
   188→
   189→
   190→@dataclass
   191→class SynthesisSyncResult:
   192→    """What changed during a synthesis sync."""
   193→
   194→    injected: bool = False
   195→    pruned: bool = False
   196→
   197→    @property
   198→    def changes(self) -> int:
   199→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   200→
   201→
   202→def review_finding_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   203→    """Hash of sorted open review/concerns finding IDs."""
   204→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   205→    ids = sorted(
   206→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   207→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   208→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   209→    )
   210→    if not ids:
   211→        return ""
   212→    return hashlib.md5("|".join(ids).encode()).hexdigest()
   213→
   214→
   215→def sync_synthesis_needed(
   216→    plan: PlanModel,
   217→    state: StateModel,
   218→) -> SynthesisSyncResult:
   219→    """Inject/prune synthesis::pending based on review finding drift.
   220→
   221→    Triggers when open review/concerns findings exist AND the snapshot
   222→    hash differs from the last-synthesized hash.
   223→    """
   224→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   225→    result = SynthesisSyncResult()
   226→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   227→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   228→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   229→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   230→    has_synthesis = SYNTHESIS_ID in order
   231→
   232→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   233→        # Need synthesis — inject if not present
   234→        if not has_synthesis:
   235→            order.insert(0, SYNTHESIS_ID)
   236→            result.injected = True
   237→    else:
   238→        # Synthesis up to date or no review findings — prune if present
   239→        if has_synthesis:
   240→            order.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
   241→            result.pruned = True
   242→
   243→    return result
   244→
   245→
   246→__all__ = [
   247→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   248→    "SYNTHESIS_ID",
   249→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
   250→    "SynthesisSyncResult",
   251→    "UnscoredDimensionSyncResult",
   252→    "_current_unscored_ids",
   253→    "review_finding_snapshot_hash",
   254→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   255→    "sync_synthesis_needed",
   256→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   257→]
   258→
```

```
    44→
    45→
    46→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    47→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    48→
    49→    items: list[dict]
    50→    total: int
    51→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    52→    requested_tier: int | None
    53→    selected_tier: int | None
    54→    fallback_reason: str | None
    55→    available_tiers: list[int]
    56→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    57→
    58→
    59→def _apply_plan_order(
    60→    items: list[dict],
    61→    plan: dict,
    62→    *,
    63→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    64→    cluster: str | None = None,
    65→    state: dict | None = None,
    66→) -> list[dict]:
    67→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
    68→
    69→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
    70→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
    71→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
    72→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
    73→    """
    74→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    75→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    76→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    77→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    78→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    79→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    80→
    81→    # Build lookup
    82→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    83→    for item in items:
    84→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    85→
    86→    # Build synthetic items for skipped entries missing from the work queue
    87→    if include_skipped and state:
    88→        findings = state.get("findings", {})
    89→        for fid in skipped_ids:
    90→            if fid not in by_id and fid in findings:
    91→                f = findings[fid]
    92→                by_id[fid] = {
    93→                    "id": fid,
    94→                    "kind": "finding",
    95→                    "tier": f.get("tier", 3),
    96→                    "effective_tier": f.get("tier", 3),
    97→                    "confidence": f.get("confidence", "medium"),
    98→                    "detector": f.get("detector", ""),
    99→                    "file": f.get("file", ""),
   100→                    "summary": f.get("summary", ""),
   101→                    "status": f.get("status", "open"),
   102→                }
   103→
   104→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
   105→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
   106→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
   107→        if override.get("description"):
   108→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
   109→        if override.get("note"):
   110→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
   111→        if override.get("cluster"):
   112→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
   113→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   114→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
   115→                "name": cluster_name,
   116→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
   117→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
   118→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
   119→            }
   120→
   121→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   122→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   123→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   124→    for fid in queue_order:
   125→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   126→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   127→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   128→
   129→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   130→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   131→    for item in items:
   132→        item_id = item["id"]
   133→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   134→            continue
   135→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   136→            skipped_items.append(item)
   137→        else:
   138→            remaining.append(item)
   139→
   140→    # Assign queue positions
   141→    result = ordered + remaining
   142→    if include_skipped:
   143→        result = result + skipped_items
   144→
   145→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   146→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   147→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   148→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   149→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   150→            if skip_entry:
   151→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   152→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   153→                if skip_reason:
   154→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   155→
   156→    # Filter to cluster if requested — but never filter out synthesis items;
   157→    # they are queue-level blockers that outrank cluster membership.
   158→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   159→    if effective_cluster:
   160→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   161→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   162→        result = [
   163→            item for item in result
   164→            if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids
   165→            or item.get("kind") == "synthesis_needed"
   166→        ]
   167→
   168→    return result
   169→
   170→
   171→def _item_matches_tier(item: dict, tier: int) -> bool:
   172→    """Check if an item (or any of its members for clusters) matches a tier."""
   173→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
   174→    if kind in ("synthesis_needed", "epic"):
   175→        return tier == 1  # Always T1
   176→    if kind == "cluster":
   177→        return any(
   178→            int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   179→            for m in item.get("members", [])
   180→        )
   181→    return int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   182→
   183→
   184→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   185→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   186→    try:
   187→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   188→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   189→        if meta:
   190→            return meta.action_type
   191→    except ImportError:
   192→        pass
   193→    return "manual_fix"
   194→
   195→
   196→_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   197→
   198→
   199→def _build_epic_items(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
   200→    """Build synthetic queue items for each pending/in_progress synthesis-cluster."""
   201→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import synthesis_clusters
   202→
   203→    epics = synthesis_clusters(plan)
   204→    if not epics:
   205→        return []
   206→
   207→    total_epics = len(epics)
   208→    items: list[dict] = []
   209→    for epic_name, epic in sorted(
   210→        epics.items(),
   211→        key=lambda kv: kv[1].get("dependency_order", 999),
   212→    ):
   213→        status = epic.get("status", "pending")
   214→        if status == "completed":
   215→            continue
   216→        finding_ids = epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   217→        if not finding_ids:
   218→            continue
   219→
   220→        items.append({
   221→            "id": epic_name,
   222→            "kind": "epic",
   223→            "tier": 1,
   224→            "effective_tier": 1,
   225→            "confidence": "high",
   226→            "detector": "epic",
   227→            "file": ".",
   228→            "summary": f"[{epic.get('direction', 'simplify')}] {epic.get('thesis', '')}",
   229→            "status": "open",
   230→            "primary_command": f"desloppify plan focus {epic_name}",
   231→            "detail": {
   232→                "thesis": epic.get("thesis", ""),
   233→                "direction": epic.get("direction", ""),
   234→                "root_cause": epic.get("root_cause", ""),
   235→                "epic_status": status,
   236→                "agent_safe": epic.get("agent_safe", False),
   237→                "dependency_order": epic.get("dependency_order", 999),
   238→                "total_epics": total_epics,
   239→                "finding_ids": finding_ids,
   240→                "action_steps": epic.get("action_steps", []),
   241→            },
   242→        })
   243→    return items
   244→
   245→
   246→def _inject_synthesis_and_epic_items(
   247→    *,
   248→    all_items: list[dict],
   249→    plan: dict,
   250→    state: StateModel,
   251→    scope: str | None,
   252→) -> None:
   253→    """Inject synthesis::pending and epic items into the queue."""
   254→    existing_ids = {item.get("id") for item in all_items}
   255→
   256→    synthesis_item = build_synthesis_item(plan, state)
   257→    if synthesis_item and synthesis_item["id"] not in existing_ids:
   258→        if scope_matches(synthesis_item, scope):
   259→            all_items.append(synthesis_item)
   260→            existing_ids.add(synthesis_item["id"])
   261→
   262→    epic_items = _build_epic_items(plan, state)
   263→    for item in epic_items:
   264→        if item["id"] not in existing_ids:
   265→            if scope_matches(item, scope):
   266→                all_items.append(item)
   267→                existing_ids.add(item["id"])
   268→
   269→
   270→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   271→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   272→
   273→    Synthesis-clusters (those with a thesis) are represented by epic
   274→    items, so their member findings are excluded from cluster collapsing.
   275→    """
   276→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   277→
   278→    if not clusters:
   279→        return items
   280→
   281→    # Build epic membership to exclude from cluster collapse — synthesis-clusters
   282→    # have their own epic queue items, so their findings shouldn't also collapse.
   283→    epic_finding_ids: set[str] = set()
   284→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   285→        if cluster.get("thesis") and cluster.get("status") != "completed":
   286→            epic_finding_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   287→
   288→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name (non-synthesis clusters only)
   289→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   290→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   291→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   292→            continue
   293→        # Skip synthesis-clusters — they have epic items
   294→        if cluster.get("thesis"):
   295→            continue
   296→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   297→            fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   298→
   299→    # Remove epic findings from cluster membership
   300→    for fid in epic_finding_ids:
   301→        fid_to_cluster.pop(fid, None)
   302→
   303→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   304→        return items
   305→
   306→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   307→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   308→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   309→
   310→    for item in items:
   311→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   312→        if cname:
   313→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   314→        else:
   315→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   316→
   317→    # Build cluster meta-items
   318→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   319→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   320→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   321→        if len(members) < 2:
   322→            result.extend(members)
   323→            continue
   324→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   325→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   326→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   327→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   328→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   329→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   330→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   331→            action_type = "reorganize"
   332→        else:
   333→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   334→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   335→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   336→                action_type = "refactor"
   337→
   338→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   339→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   340→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   341→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   342→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   343→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   344→        else:
   345→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   346→
   347→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   348→        if not primary_command:
   349→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
   350→
   351→        meta_item: dict = {
   352→            "id": cname,
   353→            "kind": "cluster",
   354→            "action_type": action_type,
   355→            "summary": summary,
   356→            "members": members,
   357→            "member_count": len(members),
   358→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   359→            "cluster_name": cname,
   360→            "cluster_auto": True,
   361→            "confidence": "high",
   362→            "detector": detector,
   363→            "file": "",
   364→        }
   365→        result.append(meta_item)
   366→
   367→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   368→    return result
   369→
   370→
   371→def build_work_queue(
   372→    state: StateModel,
   373→    *,
   374→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   375→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   376→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   377→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   378→
   379→    status = resolved_options.status
   380→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   381→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   382→    try:
   383→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   384→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   385→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   386→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   387→
   388→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   389→        state,
   390→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   391→        status_filter=status,
   392→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   393→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   394→    )
   395→
   396→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   397→    if (
   398→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   399→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   400→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   401→    ):
   402→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   403→            state,
   404→            state.get("findings", {}),
   405→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   406→        )
   407→        for item in subjective_items:
   408→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   409→                all_items.append(item)
   410→
   411→    # Inject synthesis::pending and epic items when plan has them
   412→    if resolved_options.plan:
   413→        _inject_synthesis_and_epic_items(
   414→            all_items=all_items,
   415→            plan=resolved_options.plan,
   416→            state=state,
   417→            scope=resolved_options.scope,
   418→        )
   419→
   420→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   421→
   422→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   423→    if resolved_options.plan:
   424→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   425→            all_items,
   426→            resolved_options.plan,
   427→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   428→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   429→            state=state if resolved_options.include_skipped else None,
   430→        )
   431→
   432→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   433→    should_collapse = (
   434→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   435→        and resolved_options.plan
   436→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   437→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   438→    )
   439→    if should_collapse:
   440→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   441→
   442→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   443→
   444→    requested_tier = (
   445→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
   446→    )
   447→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   448→    fallback_reason = None
   449→    filtered = all_items
   450→
   451→    if requested_tier is not None:
   452→        filtered = [
   453→            item
   454→            for item in all_items
   455→            if _item_matches_tier(item, requested_tier)
   456→        ]
   457→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   458→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   459→            if chosen is not None:
   460→                selected_tier = chosen
   461→                filtered = [
   462→                    item
   463→                    for item in all_items
   464→                    if _item_matches_tier(item, chosen)
   465→                ]
   466→                fallback_reason = (
   467→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   468→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   469→                )
   470→        elif not filtered:
   471→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   472→
   473→    total = len(filtered)
   474→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   475→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   476→
   477→    if resolved_options.explain:
   478→        for item in filtered:
   479→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   480→
   481→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   482→    return {
   483→        "items": filtered,
   484→        "total": total,
   485→        "tier_counts": counts,
   486→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   487→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   488→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   489→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   490→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   491→    }
   492→
   493→
   494→__all__ = [
   495→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   496→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   497→    "WorkQueueResult",
   498→    "build_work_queue",
   499→    "group_queue_items",
   500→]
   501→
```
