   117→    if members:
   118→        # File distribution
   119→        from collections import Counter
   120→        file_counts = Counter(m.get("file", "?") for m in members)
   121→        if len(file_counts) <= 5:
   122→            print(colorize("\n  Files:", "dim"))
   123→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common():
   124→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   125→        else:
   126→            print(colorize(f"\n  Spread across {len(file_counts)} files:", "dim"))
   127→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common(3):
   128→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   129→            remaining = len(file_counts) - 3
   130→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more files", "dim"))
   131→
   132→        # Sample IDs
   133→        print(colorize("\n  Sample:", "dim"))
   134→        for m in members[:3]:
   135→            print(f"    - {m.get('id', '')}")
   136→        if len(members) > 3:
   137→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(members) - 3} more", "dim"))
   138→
   139→    # Action
   140→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   141→    primary_command = item.get("primary_command")
   142→    if primary_command:
   143→        print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {primary_command}", "cyan"))
   144→
   145→    # Resolution hints
   146→    print(colorize(f'\n  Resolve all:   desloppify plan done "{cluster_name}" '
   147→                   f'--note "<what>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"', "dim"))
   148→    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10",
   149→                   "dim"))
   150→    print(colorize(f"  Skip cluster:  desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   151→
   152→
   153→def _render_item(
   154→    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, findings_scoped: dict, explain: bool,
   155→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   156→) -> None:
   157→    # Delegate to cluster renderer for cluster meta-items
   158→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   159→        _render_cluster_item(item)
   160→        return
   161→
   162→    tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   163→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   164→    print(colorize(f"  (Tier {tier}, {confidence} confidence)", "bold"))
   165→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   166→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   167→
   168→    # Effort/type label
   169→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
   170→        print(colorize("  Type: Design review (requires judgment)", "dim"))
   171→    elif is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
   172→        print(colorize("  Type: Auto-fixable", "dim"))
   173→
   174→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   175→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   176→        detail = item.get("detail", {})
   177→        subjective_score = float(
   178→            detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
   179→        )
   180→        print(f"  Dimension: {detail.get('dimension_name', 'unknown')}")
   181→        print(f"  Score: {subjective_score:.1f}% (always queued as T4)")
   182→        print(
   183→            colorize(
   184→                f"  Action: {item.get('primary_command', 'desloppify review --prepare')}",
   185→                "cyan",
   186→            )
   187→        )
   188→        print(colorize(
   189→            "  Note: re-review scores what it finds — scores can go down if issues are discovered.",
   190→            "dim",
   191→        ))
   192→        if explain:
   193→            reason = item.get("explain", {}).get(
   194→                "policy",
   195→                "subjective items are fixed at T4 and do not outrank mechanical T1/T2/T3.",
   196→            )
   197→            print(colorize(f"  explain: {reason}", "dim"))
   198→        return
   199→
   200→    # Plan overrides: description, cluster, note
   201→    if item.get("plan_description"):
   202→        print(colorize(f"  → {item['plan_description']}", "cyan"))
   203→    plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   204→    if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict):
   205→        cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "")
   206→        cluster_desc = plan_cluster.get("description") or ""
   207→        total = plan_cluster.get("total_items", 0)
   208→        desc_str = f' — "{cluster_desc}"' if cluster_desc else ""
   209→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}{desc_str} ({total} items)", "dim"))
   210→    if item.get("plan_note"):
   211→        print(colorize(f"  Note: {item['plan_note']}", "dim"))
   212→
   213→    file = item.get("file", "")
   214→    if file and file != ".":
   215→        print(f"  File: {file}")
   216→    print(colorize(f"  ID:   {item.get('id', '')}", "dim"))
   217→
   218→    detail = item.get("detail", {})
   219→    if detail.get("lines"):
   220→        print(f"  Lines: {', '.join(str(line_no) for line_no in detail['lines'][:8])}")
   221→    if detail.get("category"):
   222→        print(f"  Category: {detail['category']}")
   223→    if detail.get("importers") is not None:
   224→        print(f"  Active importers: {detail['importers']}")
   225→    if detail.get("suggestion"):
   226→        print(colorize(f"\n  Suggestion: {detail['suggestion']}", "dim"))
   227→
   228→    target_line = detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
   229→    if target_line and item.get("file") not in (".", ""):
   230→        snippet = read_code_snippet(item["file"], target_line)
   231→        if snippet:
   232→            print(colorize("\n  Code:", "dim"))
   233→            print(snippet)
   234→
   235→    if dim_scores:
   236→        detector = item.get("detector", "")
   237→        dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   238→        if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   239→            dimension_score = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   240→            strict_val = dimension_score.get("strict", dimension_score["score"])
   241→            print(
   242→                colorize(
   243→                    f"\n  Dimension: {dimension.name} — {dimension_score['score']:.1f}% "
   244→                    f"(strict: {strict_val:.1f}%) "
   245→                    f"({dimension_score['issues']} of {dimension_score['checks']:,} checks failing)",
   246→                    "dim",
   247→                )
   248→            )
   249→
   250→    # Score impact estimate
   251→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   252→    if potentials and detector and dim_scores:
   253→        try:
   254→            from desloppify.scoring import compute_score_impact
   255→            impact = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=1)
   256→            if impact > 0:
   257→                print(colorize(f"  Impact: fixing this is worth ~+{impact:.1f} pts on overall score", "cyan"))
   258→            else:
   259→                # Single-item impact rounds to 0 — try bulk impact for all issues in detector
   260→                dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   261→                if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   262→                    issues = dim_scores[dimension.name].get("issues", 0)
   263→                    if issues > 1:
   264→                        bulk = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=issues)
   265→                        if bulk > 0:
   266→                            print(colorize(
   267→                                f"  Impact: fixing all {issues} {detector} issues → ~+{bulk:.1f} pts",
   268→                                "cyan",
   269→                            ))
   270→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):
   271→            pass
   272→    elif detector == "review" and dim_scores:
   273→        # Review findings: show dimension drag from breakdown
   274→        try:
   275→            from desloppify.scoring import compute_health_breakdown
   276→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   277→            if dim_key:
   278→                breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   279→                for entry in breakdown.get("entries", []):
   280→                    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   281→                        continue
   282→                    entry_key = str(entry.get("name", "")).lower().replace(" ", "_")
   283→                    if entry_key == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   284→                        drag = float(entry.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   285→                        if drag > 0.01:
   286→                            print(colorize(
   287→                                f"  Dimension drag: {entry['name']} costs -{drag:.2f} pts on overall score",
   288→                                "cyan",
   289→                            ))
   290→                        break
   291→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):
   292→            pass
   293→
   294→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   295→    auto_fix_command = item.get("primary_command")
   296→    if is_auto_fix_command(auto_fix_command):
   297→        similar_count = sum(
   298→            1
   299→            for finding in findings_scoped.values()
   300→            if finding.get("detector") == detector_name and finding["status"] == "open"
   301→        )
   302→        if similar_count > 1:
   303→            print(
   304→                colorize(
   305→                    f"\n  Auto-fixable: {similar_count} similar findings. "
   306→                    f"Run `{auto_fix_command}` to fix all at once.",
   307→                    "cyan",
   308→                )
   309→            )
   310→    if explain:
   311→        explanation = item.get("explain", {})
   312→        count_weight = explanation.get("count", int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0))
   313→        base = (
   314→            f"ranked by tier={tier}, confidence={confidence}, "
   315→            f"count={count_weight}, id={item.get('id', '')}"
   316→        )
   317→
   318→        # Add dimension context for mechanical detectors
   319→        if dim_scores and detector:
   320→            dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   321→            if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   322→                ds = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   323→                base += (
   324→                    f". Dimension: {dimension.name} at {ds['score']:.1f}% "
   325→                    f"({ds['issues']} open issues)"
   326→                )
   327→
   328→        # For review findings, show subjective dimension context
   329→        if item.get("detector") == "review" and dim_scores:
   330→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   331→            if dim_key:
   332→                for ds_name, ds_data in dim_scores.items():
   333→                    if ds_name.lower().replace(" ", "_") == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   334→                        score_val = ds_data.get("score", "?")
   335→                        score_str = f"{score_val:.1f}" if isinstance(score_val, (int, float)) else str(score_val)
   336→                        base += f". Subjective dimension: {ds_name} at {score_str}%"
   337→                        break
   338→
   339→        policy = explanation.get("policy")
   340→        if policy:
   341→            base = f"{base}. {policy}"
   342→        print(colorize(f"  explain: {base}", "dim"))
   343→
   344→
   345→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   346→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   347→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   348→        return
   349→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   350→    if not explain:
   351→        return
   352→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   353→    if available:
   354→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   355→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   356→
   357→
   358→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   359→    if queue.get("items"):
   360→        return False
   361→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   362→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   363→    if tier is not None:
   364→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   365→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   366→        if available:
   367→            commands = " | ".join(
   368→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   369→            )
   370→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   371→    return True
   372→
   373→
   374→def _render_strategy_summary(plan: dict | None) -> None:
   375→    """Show the meta-plan strategy summary when epics exist."""
   376→    if not plan:
   377→        return
   378→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   379→    summary = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   380→    if not summary:
   381→        return
   382→    print(colorize("\n  Meta-plan:", "bold"))
   383→    for line in summary.strip().splitlines():
   384→        print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
   385→
   386→
   387→def _render_synthesis_item(item: dict) -> None:
   388→    """Render the synthesis::pending card."""
   389→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   390→    total = detail.get("total_review_findings", 0)
   391→    new = detail.get("new_since_last", 0)
   392→    resolved = detail.get("resolved_since_last", 0)
   393→    trigger = detail.get("trigger", "unknown")
   394→    epic_count = detail.get("epic_count", 0)
   395→    pending = detail.get("pending_epics", 0)
   396→    in_prog = detail.get("in_progress_epics", 0)
   397→    completed = detail.get("completed_epics", 0)
   398→
   399→    print(colorize("  PLAN — do not proceed until complete", "bold"))
   400→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   401→    print(f"  {total} review findings need integration into the meta-plan.")
   402→    print(f"  Trigger: {trigger}")
   403→    print()
   404→    if new:
   405→        print(f"  New findings since last synthesis: {new}")
   406→    if resolved:
   407→        print(f"  Resolved since last synthesis: {resolved}")
   408→    if epic_count:
   409→        print(f"  Current epics: {epic_count} ({pending} pending, {in_prog} in progress, {completed} completed)")
   410→    print()
   411→    print(colorize("  This is a planning step. Do NOT start fixing code until this is done.", "yellow"))
   412→    print()
   413→    print("  Steps:")
   414→    print("    1. `desloppify plan synthesize`  — see all findings that need organizing")
   415→    print("    2. Group by root cause into clusters (create, add, move, focus)")
   416→    print('    3. `desloppify plan synthesize --complete --strategy "..."`')
   417→    print("    4. `desloppify next`  — start implementation")
   418→    print()
   419→    print(colorize("  Forbidden during this phase:", "red"))
   420→    print("    - desloppify fix / resolve / editing code / plan skip")
   421→    print()
   422→    print(colorize("  View plan:", "dim"))
   423→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan queue   — items in priority order", "dim"))
   424→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan show    — plan status summary", "dim"))
   425→
   426→
   427→def _render_epic_item(item: dict) -> None:
   428→    """Render an epic card."""
   429→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   430→    direction = detail.get("direction", "")
   431→    thesis = detail.get("thesis", "")
   432→    root_cause = detail.get("root_cause", "")
   433→    status = detail.get("epic_status", "pending")
   434→    agent_safe = detail.get("agent_safe", False)
   435→    dep_order = detail.get("dependency_order", 999)
   436→    total_epics = detail.get("total_epics", 0)
   437→    finding_ids = detail.get("finding_ids", [])
   438→    action_steps = detail.get("action_steps", [])
   439→
   440→    safe_tag = " agent-safe" if agent_safe else ""
   441→    print(colorize(f"  [Epic — {direction}] ({len(finding_ids)} findings,{safe_tag} dependency_order: {dep_order})", "bold"))
   442→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   443→    print(f"  Thesis: {thesis}")
   444→    if root_cause:
   445→        print(f"  Root cause: {root_cause}")
   446→    print(f"  Status: {status}")
   447→    if total_epics:
   448→        print(f"  This is part of the meta-plan (epic {dep_order} of {total_epics}).")
   449→    print()
   450→    if action_steps:
   451→        print("  Steps:")
   452→        for i, step in enumerate(action_steps, 1):
   453→            print(f"    {i}. {step}")
   454→        print()
   455→    if finding_ids:
   456→        shown = finding_ids[:5]
   457→        print("  Findings in this epic:")
   458→        for fid in shown:
   459→            print(f"    - {fid}")
   460→        if len(finding_ids) > 5:
   461→            print(f"    ... and {len(finding_ids) - 5} more")
   462→        print()
   463→    epic_name = item.get("id", "")
   464→    print(f"  Work on this epic:")
   465→    print(f"    desloppify plan focus {epic_name}")
   466→    print(f"    (then work through findings with desloppify next)")
   467→
   468→
   469→def render_terminal_items(
   470→    items: list[dict],
   471→    dim_scores: dict,
   472→    findings_scoped: dict,
   473→    *,
   474→    group: str,
   475→    explain: bool,
   476→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   477→    plan: dict | None = None,
   478→) -> None:
   479→    # Show strategy summary when epics exist
   480→    _render_strategy_summary(plan)
   481→
   482→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   483→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   484→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   485→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   486→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   487→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   488→        visible = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("kind") != "synthesis_needed")
   489→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({visible} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   490→
   491→        # Exclude the synthesis item from the focus count — it's a queue-level
   492→        # blocker, not a cluster member, so don't count it in "N of M remaining".
   493→        focus_items = [it for it in items if it.get("kind") != "synthesis_needed"]
   494→        if len(focus_items) == 0:
   495→            print(colorize(
   496→                "  This cluster has no remaining items. "
   497→                "Run `desloppify plan focus --clear` to unfocus, "
   498→                "or `desloppify plan cluster show` to inspect.",
   499→                "yellow",
   500→            ))
   501→
   502→    # When synthesis is pending, only show the planning card — don't leak
   503→    # implementation tasks that would tempt agents to skip the planning step.
   504→    if items and items[0].get("kind") == "synthesis_needed":
   505→        queue_pos = items[0].get("queue_position")
   506→        pos_str = f"  (#{queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   507→        print(colorize(f"  Next item{pos_str}", "bold"))
   508→        _render_synthesis_item(items[0])
   509→        return
   510→
   511→    if group != "item":
   512→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   513→        return
   514→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   515→        if idx > 0:
   516→            print()
   517→        kind = item.get("kind", "")
   518→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   519→
   520→        # All item types get the same position label — no visual hierarchy
   521→        # that makes synthetic items look like metadata instead of tasks.
   522→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   523→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   524→        elif len(items) > 1:
   525→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   526→        else:
   527→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   528→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   529→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   530→
   531→        if kind == "epic":
   532→            _render_epic_item(item)
   533→            continue
   534→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   535→
   536→
   537→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   538→    if len(items) != 1:
   539→        return
   540→    kind = items[0].get("kind", "")
   541→    if kind in ("cluster", "synthesis_needed", "epic"):
   542→        return  # These cards include their own resolution hints
   543→    if kind != "finding":
   544→        return
   545→    item = items[0]
   546→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   547→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   548→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   549→        primary = item.get(
   550→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   551→        )
   552→        print(f"    {primary}")
   553→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   554→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   555→        return
   556→
   557→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   558→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   559→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   560→        print(f"    {primary}")
   561→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   562→    else:
   563→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   564→
   565→    print(
   566→        f'    desloppify plan done "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   567→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   568→    )
   569→    print(
   570→        f'    desloppify plan skip --permanent "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   571→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   572→    )
   573→
   574→
   575→def render_followup_nudges(
   576→    state: dict,
   577→    dim_scores: dict,
   578→    findings_scoped: dict,
   579→    *,
   580→    strict_score: float | None,
   581→    target_strict_score: float,
   582→) -> None:
   583→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   584→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   585→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   586→        state,
   587→        subjective_entries,
   588→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   589→        max_quality_items=3,
   590→        max_integrity_items=5,
   591→    )
   592→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   593→    if strict_score is not None:
   594→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   595→        if gap > 0:
   596→            print(
   597→                colorize(
   598→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   599→                    "cyan",
   600→                )
   601→            )
   602→        else:
   603→            print(
   604→                colorize(
   605→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   606→                    "green",
   607→                )
   608→            )
   609→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   610→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   611→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   612→
   613→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   614→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   615→        findings_scoped
   616→    )
   617→    parts: list[str] = []
   618→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   619→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   620→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   621→    open_review = [
   622→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   623→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   624→    ]

   626→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   627→    if stale_count:
   628→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   629→    if unassessed_count:
   630→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   631→    if len(open_review):
   632→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   633→    if coverage_open > 0:
   634→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   635→
   636→    if parts:
   637→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   638→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))

    86→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    87→    kind = item.get("kind", "")

    89→    # Synthesis and epic items sort before everything else (T0-equivalent).

   134→    effective_tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   135→
   136→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":

   155→    elif is_review:

   157→            "tier asc",
   158→            "confidence asc",
   159→            "review_weight desc",
   160→            "count desc",
   161→            "id asc",
   162→        ]
   163→    else:
   164→        ranking_factors = ["tier asc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   165→    explain = {
   166→        "kind": "finding",
   167→        "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   168→        "confidence": confidence,
   169→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   170→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   171→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   172→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   173→    }
   174→    if is_review:
   175→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   176→    if is_subjective:
   177→        explain["policy"] = (

   180→        )
   181→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)

   185→def tier_counts(items: list[dict]) -> dict[int, int]:
   186→    counts = {1: 0, 2: 0, 3: 0, 4: 0}
   187→    for item in items:
   188→        if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   189→            # Count each member at its own tier
   190→            for member in item.get("members", []):
   191→                tier = int(member.get("effective_tier", member.get("tier", 3)))
   192→                counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   193→            continue
   194→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   195→        counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   196→    return counts
   197→
   198→
   199→def choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier: int, counts: dict[int, int]) -> int | None:
   200→    available = [tier for tier, count in counts.items() if count > 0]
   201→    if not available:
   202→        return None
   203→    return min(available, key=lambda tier: (abs(tier - requested_tier), tier))
   204→
   205→
   206→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   207→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   208→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   209→    for item in items:
   210→        if group == "file":
   211→            key = item.get("file", "")
   212→        elif group == "detector":
   213→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   214→        elif group == "tier":
   215→            key = f"T{int(item.get('effective_tier', item.get('tier', 3)))}"
   216→        elif group == "cluster":
   217→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   218→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   219→        else:
   220→            key = "items"
   221→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   222→    return grouped
   223→

   229→    "item_sort_key",
   230→    "tier_counts",

   232→    "group_queue_items",
   233→]

Here is the complete picture of how plan data flows into the `next` command's rendering.

---

## Plan Data Flow into `next` Rendering

### 1. `build_work_queue` in `core.py` — how plan data is incorporated

**Entry point:** `build_work_queue` receives `QueueBuildOptions` which carries `plan: dict | None`.

**Execution order inside `build_work_queue`** (lines 371-491):

1. `build_finding_items` is called first — this is plan-agnostic. It builds raw finding items from state with no plan knowledge at all.

2. `_inject_synthesis_and_epic_items` (line 413) — if `plan` is present, injects two special synthetic item types:
   - A `synthesis::pending` item (kind=`synthesis_needed`) when unintegrated review findings exist.
   - `epic` items built from `synthesis_clusters(plan)` — one per pending/in_progress synthesis cluster.

3. `all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)` — mechanical sort before plan reordering.

4. `_apply_plan_order` (line 424) — this is where all plan metadata gets stamped onto items.

5. `_collapse_clusters` (line 440) — replaces individual member findings with cluster meta-items.

---

### How `_apply_plan_order` annotates items (lines 59-168)

This function does three distinct things:

**a) Reads from `plan` dict:**
```python
queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
```

**b) Stamps plan metadata onto items (lines 104-119):**
```python
for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    if override.get("description"):
        item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    if override.get("note"):
        item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    if override.get("cluster"):
        cluster_name = override["cluster"]
        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
        item["plan_cluster"] = {
            "name": cluster_name,
            "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
            "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
            "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
        }
```

So `plan_description`, `plan_note`, and `plan_cluster` are written here — sourced from `plan["overrides"][item_id]` for the first two, and cross-referenced with `plan["clusters"]` for the cluster dict. The `sibling_ids` field on `plan_cluster` carries the full list of co-members.

**c) Skipped items** get `plan_skipped=True`, `plan_skip_kind`, and `plan_skip_reason` stamped on (lines 147-154).

**d) Queue position** is assigned sequentially (line 146): ordered (from `queue_order`) first, then remaining, then skipped.

**e) Cluster filtering** (lines 158-166): if `active_cluster` or `--cluster` flag is set, the result is filtered to only members of that cluster (plus any synthesis_needed item, which always passes through).

---

### 2. `_render_item` in `render.py` — plan metadata display

Plan metadata appears in a dedicated block at lines 200-211, after the tier/confidence header and summary line, before file/ID/detail lines:

```python
# Plan overrides: description, cluster, note
if item.get("plan_description"):
    print(colorize(f"  → {item['plan_description']}", "cyan"))
plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict):
    cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "")
    cluster_desc = plan_cluster.get("description") or ""
    total = plan_cluster.get("total_items", 0)
    desc_str = f' — "{cluster_desc}"' if cluster_desc else ""
    print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}{desc_str} ({total} items)", "dim"))
if item.get("plan_note"):
    print(colorize(f"  Note: {item['plan_note']}", "dim"))
```

Display order on a card:
1. `plan_description` — shown in **cyan** with a `→` prefix
2. `plan_cluster` — shown in **dim** as `Cluster: <name> — "<desc>" (N items)`
3. `plan_note` — shown in **dim** as `Note: <text>`

These appear before `File:`, `ID:`, `Lines:`, code snippet, dimension score, and impact estimate.

**`render_terminal_items`** (lines 469-534) also consumes plan at the top level:
- Calls `_render_strategy_summary(plan)` to show `plan["epic_synthesis_meta"]["strategy_summary"]` as a "Meta-plan:" header block.
- Reads `plan["active_cluster"]` to show a focus header line: `Focused on: <cluster> (N of M remaining)`.
- Intercepts the first item: if `kind == "synthesis_needed"`, renders only the planning card and returns early (line 504-509), preventing any implementation tasks from leaking through.

---

### 3. `ranking.py` — plan data's role in ranking

**Plan data plays zero role in `item_sort_key`**. The sort key (lines 86-130) is entirely mechanical:

- `synthesis_needed` → sort key `(-2, 0, 0, id)` — always first
- `epic` → sort key `(-1, dependency_order, 0, id)` — second
- `cluster` → sort key `(0, action_type_priority, -member_count, id)`
- subjective → `(effective_tier, 1, subjective_score, id)`
- normal finding → `(effective_tier, 0, confidence_rank, -review_weight, -count, id)`

`plan_description`, `plan_note`, `plan_cluster` are annotation fields only — they do not influence sort position. Plan influences **order** by the explicit `queue_order` list reordering done in `_apply_plan_order` after the mechanical sort.

`group_queue_items` (lines 206-222) in `ranking.py` does read `plan_cluster` for the `--group cluster` mode: it keys items by `item["plan_cluster"]["name"]` vs `"(unclustered)"`.

---

### Injection Point Map for Synthesis Observations/Notes

| What you want to inject | Where to add it | Field name on item |
|---|---|---|
| A per-finding override note | `plan["overrides"][finding_id]["note"]` (read in `_apply_plan_order` line 109) | `plan_note` — rendered dim at line 211 |
| A per-finding description/label | `plan["overrides"][finding_id]["description"]` (line 107) | `plan_description` — rendered cyan at line 202 |
| A cluster membership + description | `plan["overrides"][finding_id]["cluster"]` + `plan["clusters"][name]` (lines 111-119) | `plan_cluster` dict — rendered dim at line 204-209 |
| A top-level strategy narrative | `plan["epic_synthesis_meta"]["strategy_summary"]` | read by `_render_strategy_summary` at line 378, rendered above all items |
| An epic-level thesis/root_cause/steps | `plan["clusters"][name]` with `thesis`, `root_cause`, `action_steps` | rendered by `_render_epic_item` lines 427-466 |

The cleanest injection point for synthesis observations is `plan["overrides"][finding_id]["note"]` for per-item notes, and `plan["epic_synthesis_meta"]["strategy_summary"]` for the global narrative that appears above the queue. Both are already wired through to rendering with no code changes needed.

## Plan Data Model and Schema — Full Reference

### File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py`

**`PLAN_VERSION = 4`**

**Constants:**
- `VALID_SKIP_KINDS = {"temporary", "permanent", "false_positive", "synthesized_out"}`
- `VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS = frozenset({"delete", "merge", "flatten", "enforce", "simplify", "decompose", "extract", "inline"})`
- `EPIC_PREFIX = "epic/"`

---

#### `SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False)`
```python
finding_id: Required[str]
kind: Required[str]           # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive" | "synthesized_out"
reason: str | None
note: str | None              # required for permanent (wontfix note)
attestation: str | None       # required for permanent/false_positive
created_at: str
review_after: int | None      # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
skipped_at_scan: int          # state.scan_count when skipped
original_position: int | None # queue position before skip (for restore)
```

---

#### `ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False)`
```python
finding_id: Required[str]
description: str | None
note: str | None
cluster: str | None
created_at: str
updated_at: str
```
Note: `add_to_cluster()` writes `cluster` and `updated_at` into this for every finding added to a cluster. It's both an annotation store and a cluster-membership index.

---

#### `Cluster(TypedDict, total=False)`
```python
name: Required[str]
description: str | None
finding_ids: list[str]
created_at: str
updated_at: str
auto: bool              # True for auto-generated clusters
cluster_key: str        # Deterministic grouping key (for regeneration)
action: str | None      # Primary resolution command/guidance text
user_modified: bool     # True when user manually edits membership

# Epic fields — only present on synthesis-created clusters (thesis is the sentinel):
thesis: str | None
direction: str | None          # One of VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS
root_cause: str | None
supersedes: list[str]
dismissed: list[str]
agent_safe: bool
dependency_order: int
action_steps: list[str]
source_clusters: list[str]
status: str | None             # "pending" | "in_progress" | "completed"
synthesis_version: int
```

Key distinction: a Cluster with a `thesis` field is a **synthesis-created epic**. The `synthesis_clusters()` helper filters on `cluster.get("thesis")` being truthy to distinguish them from ordinary user clusters. The `status` field on a synthesis-cluster is intentionally not overwritten when it is `"in_progress"` (so an agent's progress is preserved across re-synthesis runs).

---

#### `EpicSynthesisMeta(TypedDict, total=False)`
```python
synthesized_ids: list[str]          # all open review/concerns finding IDs at time of last run
last_run: str                        # ISO UTC timestamp
version: int                         # incremented on each run
dismissed_ids: list[str]             # IDs dismissed in the last run
finding_snapshot_hash: str           # md5 of sorted open review finding IDs — used for drift detection
strategy_summary: str                # LLM's 2-4 sentence narrative
trigger: str                         # "manual" or other trigger label
```
Lives at `plan["epic_synthesis_meta"]`. The `finding_snapshot_hash` is compared against a freshly-computed hash on every reconcile/scan to determine if `synthesis::pending` should be injected into the queue.

---

#### `SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False)`
```python
original_id: Required[str]
original_detector: str
original_file: str
original_summary: str
status: str            # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
superseded_at: str
remapped_to: str | None
candidates: list[str]
note: str | None
```

---

#### `PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False)` — the root type
```python
version: Required[int]
created: Required[str]
updated: Required[str]
queue_order: list[str]               # ordered finding IDs (the live work queue)
deferred: list[str]                  # kept empty — migration compat only (was v1 skip list)
skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]        # key = finding_id
active_cluster: str | None           # cluster name currently in focus
overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]   # key = finding_id
clusters: dict[str, Cluster]         # key = cluster name (includes epic/... synthesis clusters)
superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
epic_synthesis_meta: EpicSynthesisMeta
deleted_clusters: list[str]          # cluster_keys of deleted auto-clusters (prevents regeneration)
```

`empty_plan()` returns sensible zero-value defaults. `ensure_plan_defaults()` handles three migrations: v1→v2 (deferred list → skipped dict), v3→v4 (epics dict → synthesis-clusters inside clusters dict), and normalizes all cluster fields. `validate_plan()` checks that no ID appears in both `queue_order` and `skipped`, and that all skip kinds are valid.

---

### File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/epic_synthesis.py`

#### Dataclasses

**`SynthesisInput`** — everything the LLM needs:
```python
@dataclass
class SynthesisInput:
    open_findings: dict[str, dict]        # id -> Finding (detector in {"review","concerns"}, status=="open")
    mechanical_findings: dict[str, dict]  # id -> Finding (all other detectors, for context only)
    existing_epics: dict[str, Cluster]    # current synthesis-clusters (thesis-bearing clusters)
    dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]      # state["dimension_scores"]
    new_since_last: set[str]              # review finding IDs not in meta.synthesized_ids
    resolved_since_last: set[str]         # meta.synthesized_ids - current open review IDs
    previously_dismissed: list[str]       # meta.dismissed_ids
    synthesis_version: int                # meta.version + 1
```

**`SynthesisResult`** — parsed and validated LLM output:
```python
@dataclass
class SynthesisResult:
    strategy_summary: str
    epics: list[dict]                            # validated epic payloads
    dismissed_findings: list[DismissedFinding]   # top-level dismissals
    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote]
    priority_rationale: str
```

**`DismissedFinding`:**
```python
@dataclass
class DismissedFinding:
    finding_id: str
    reason: str
```

**`ContradictionNote`:**
```python
@dataclass
class ContradictionNote:
    kept: str
    dismissed: str
    reason: str
```

**`SynthesisMutationResult`** — what changed after applying to plan:
```python
@dataclass
class SynthesisMutationResult:
    epics_created: int = 0
    epics_updated: int = 0
    epics_completed: int = 0
    findings_dismissed: int = 0
    findings_reassigned: int = 0
    strategy_summary: str = ""
    synthesis_version: int = 0
    dry_run: bool = False
```

**`SynthesisDeps`** — injectable dependencies:
```python
@dataclass
class SynthesisDeps:
    llm_call: Any = None  # Callable[[str, str], str] — (system, user) -> response
```

---

#### `_SYNTHESIS_SYSTEM_PROMPT` — full text

```
You are maintaining the meta-plan for this codebase. Your goal is to produce
a coherent, prioritized strategy to address ALL open review findings.

Your plan should:
- Cluster findings by ROOT CAUSE, not by dimension or detector
- Give each cluster (epic) a clear thesis: one imperative sentence
- Order epics by dependency: what must be done first for later work to make sense
- Dismiss findings that don't make sense, are contradictory, or are false positives
- Mark which epics are agent-safe (can be executed mechanically) vs need human judgment
- Avoid creating work that contradicts other work in the plan
- Be ambitious but realistic — aim to resolve all findings coherently

Available directions for epics: delete, merge, flatten, enforce, simplify, decompose, extract, inline.

Available plan tools (the agent executing your plan has access to these):
- `desloppify plan queue` — view all items in priority order
- `desloppify plan focus epic/<name>` — focus the queue on one epic
- `desloppify plan skip <id> --permanent --note "why" --attest "..."` — permanently dismiss
- `desloppify plan skip <id> --note "revisit later"` — temporarily defer
- `desloppify plan done <id> --note "what I did" --attest "..."` — mark resolved
- `desloppify plan move <id> top|bottom|before|after <target>` — reorder
- `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` — inspect a cluster
- `desloppify scan` — re-scan after making changes to verify progress
- `desloppify show review --status open` — see all open review findings

Your output defines the ENTIRE work plan. Findings not assigned to any epic
will remain in the queue as individual items. Dismissed findings will be
removed from the queue with your stated reason.

Respond with a single JSON object matching this schema:
{
  "strategy_summary": "2-4 sentence narrative: what the meta-plan says, top priorities, current state",
  "epics": [
    {
      "name": "slug-name",
      "thesis": "imperative one-liner",
      "direction": "delete|merge|flatten|enforce|simplify|decompose|extract|inline",
      "root_cause": "why this cluster exists",
      "finding_ids": ["id1", "id2"],
      "dismissed": ["id3"],
      "agent_safe": true,
      "dependency_order": 1,
      "action_steps": ["step 1", "step 2"],
      "status": "pending"
    }
  ],
  "dismissed_findings": [
    {"finding_id": "id", "reason": "why this finding doesn't make sense"}
  ],
  "contradiction_notes": [
    {"kept": "finding_id", "dismissed": "finding_id", "reason": "why"}
  ],
  "priority_rationale": "why the dependency_order is what it is"
}
```

---

#### Key function signatures

**`collect_synthesis_input(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> SynthesisInput`**
Separates `state["findings"]` into open-review vs mechanical, computes new/resolved deltas vs `meta.synthesized_ids`, loads existing synthesis-clusters. All fields in `SynthesisInput` are directly derived from plan+state at call time.

**`build_synthesis_prompt(si: SynthesisInput) -> str`**
Builds the user-facing prompt. Sections emitted (each conditional):
1. Existing Epics (update these, don't recreate) — name, status, direction, thesis, truncated finding IDs
2. New findings since last synthesis — id + summary
3. Resolved since last synthesis — id only
4. All open review findings — confidence, id, file, dimension (from `detail.dimension`), summary, suggestion (from `detail.suggestion`)
5. Dimension scores (context) — score%, strict%, issue count
6. Previously dismissed — ids only, with note to maintain unless contradicted

**`parse_synthesis_result(raw: dict, valid_ids: set[str]) -> SynthesisResult`**
Validates directions (falls back to "simplify"), silently drops invalid finding IDs from epics/dismissals, coerces all fields to expected types.

**`apply_synthesis_to_plan(plan, state, synthesis, *, trigger="manual") -> SynthesisMutationResult`**
Sequence: (1) update/create epics as synthesis-clusters; (2) write `synthesized_out` skip entries for dismissed findings; (3) rebuild `queue_order` — epic findings ordered by `dependency_order` first, non-epic items appended after; (4) write full `epic_synthesis_meta` snapshot.

**`synthesize_epics(plan, state, *, deps=None, dry_run=False, trigger="manual") -> SynthesisMutationResult`**
Top-level orchestrator. Calls `collect_synthesis_input` → `build_synthesis_prompt` → `deps.llm_call(system, user)` → `parse_synthesis_result` → `apply_synthesis_to_plan`. If `dry_run=True` or `deps` is None or `deps.llm_call` is None, returns a dry-run result without mutating anything.

---

### File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/persistence.py`

**`PLAN_FILE = STATE_DIR / "plan.json"`** (i.e. `.desloppify/plan.json`)

**`load_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> PlanModel`**
- Returns `empty_plan()` on missing file, JSON decode error, or non-dict root
- Warns but continues if version is newer than `PLAN_VERSION`
- Calls `ensure_plan_defaults()` then `validate_plan()` — returns `empty_plan()` on validation failure
- No schema migration beyond what `ensure_plan_defaults` handles

**`save_plan(plan: PlanModel | dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None`**
- Calls `ensure_plan_defaults` and stamps `updated = utc_now()` before writing
- Calls `validate_plan` — raises `ValueError` before touching disk if invariants fail
- Creates `.json.bak` backup of old file before overwriting
- Uses `safe_write_text` (atomic temp+replace write from `core.discovery_api`)
- `json.dumps` with `indent=2` and `json_default` serializer (handles datetimes etc.)

**`plan_path_for_state(state_path: Path) -> Path`**
Derives `plan.json` sibling path from a state file path (for multi-lang scenarios).

**`has_living_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> bool`**
Returns True if `queue_order`, `overrides`, or `clusters` is non-empty — i.e., the user has done anything with the plan.

---

### File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations.py`

**`create_cluster(plan, name, description=None, action=None) -> Cluster`**
- Raises `ValueError` if name starts with `"auto/"` or `"epic/"` (reserved namespaces)
- Raises `ValueError` if cluster already exists
- Creates cluster with `auto=False`, `cluster_key=""`, `user_modified=False`
- Does NOT set any epic fields (thesis, direction, etc.)

**`add_to_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids) -> int`**
- Raises `ValueError` if cluster does not exist
- Appends IDs not already in `cluster["finding_ids"]`
- Writes `overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name` for each ID (the override dict is created if absent)
- Stamps `cluster["updated_at"]` and `override["updated_at"]`
- Returns count of actually-added IDs

**`remove_from_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids) -> int`**
- Sets `override["cluster"] = None` for each removed ID
- Sets `cluster["user_modified"] = True` when any items are removed from an auto-cluster

**`delete_cluster(plan, name) -> list[str]`**
- Adds `cluster["cluster_key"]` to `plan["deleted_clusters"]` if it was an auto-cluster (prevents auto_cluster_findings from regenerating it)
- Clears `active_cluster` if it matches
- Returns orphaned finding IDs (caller must decide what to do with them)

**`move_items(plan, finding_ids, position, target=None, offset=None) -> int`**
Removes IDs from `skipped` and relocates them in `queue_order`. Position specifiers: `"top"`, `"bottom"`, `"before"`, `"after"`, `"up"` (with offset), `"down"` (with offset).

**`skip_items(plan, finding_ids, *, kind, reason, note, attestation, review_after, scan_count) -> int`**
Records `original_position` in the skip entry before removal (used by `unskip_items` to restore position).

**`unskip_items(plan, finding_ids) -> tuple[int, list[str]]`**
Returns `(count_unskipped, ids_that_need_state_layer_reopen)` — the caller must separately reopen `permanent`/`false_positive` findings at the state layer.

**`purge_ids(plan, finding_ids) -> int`**
Hard-removes IDs from queue, skipped, and all cluster memberships. Intentionally preserves overrides (descriptions/notes kept as history).

**`reset_plan(plan) -> None`**
Clears all plan data to empty defaults, preserving only the original `created` timestamp.

---

### The `Finding` TypedDict (from `engine/_state/schema.py`)

This is what's stored in `state["findings"]` and what `collect_synthesis_input` iterates:

```python
class Finding(TypedDict):
    id: str
    detector: str              # "review" | "concerns" | other mechanical detectors
    file: str
    tier: int
    confidence: str            # "high" | "medium" | "low"
    summary: str
    detail: dict[str, Any]     # free-form; synthesis reads detail["suggestion"] and detail["dimension"]
    status: FindingStatus      # "open" | "fixed" | "auto_resolved" | "wontfix" | "false_positive"
    note: str | None
    first_seen: str
    last_seen: str
    resolved_at: str | None
    reopen_count: int
    suppressed: NotRequired[bool]
    suppressed_at: NotRequired[str | None]
    suppression_pattern: NotRequired[str | None]
    resolution_attestation: NotRequired[dict[str, str | bool | None]]
    lang: NotRequired[str]
    zone: NotRequired[str]
```

The synthesis prompt specifically reads `detail["suggestion"]` and `detail["dimension"]` from each finding. Everything else the LLM sees is `confidence`, `id`, `file`, and `summary`.

---

### Synthesis stage data — what currently exists

There is **no per-epic synthesis-stage tracking** beyond the global `EpicSynthesisMeta.version` and `Cluster.synthesis_version`. The `Cluster` type already has the following fields that could carry stage data:
- `status: str | None` — `"pending"` | `"in_progress"` | `"completed"` (respected during re-synthesis: `"in_progress"` is never overwritten)
- `synthesis_version: int` — which synthesis run created/last-updated this epic
- `action_steps: list[str]` — the ordered action plan for the epic
- `source_clusters: list[str]` — populated empty at creation, not yet used

The global `EpicSynthesisMeta` carries a `finding_snapshot_hash` and the full list of `synthesized_ids`/`dismissed_ids` as of the last run. There is no per-epic "last updated" hash, no per-epic input snapshot, and no staging/draft state for in-flight synthesis.

> DEVELOPER

I need you to design the implementation plan for a multi-stage planning workflow in a Python CLI tool called desloppify. Here's the full context:

## The Problem
When the tool enters a "planning phase" (synthesis::pending), an AI agent should organize review findings into coherent work arcs before fixing code. Currently agents blow through this mechanically — skimming findings, creating superficial clusters, and rushing to completion.

## Two Expert Perspectives Synthesized

**Systems architect** recommended 4 stages (SURVEY → GROUP → ORDER → COMMIT), each with mechanical preconditions, written reports, and a hash chain linking stages. Key insights:
- Each stage blocks a specific failure mode (skimming, shallow grouping, no dependency thinking, empty strategy)
- Token-overlap checking prevents copy-paste of finding descriptions
- Scale requirements by finding count (3-5 findings: abbreviated process)
- Landscape change detection should have a dampening mechanism to prevent reset loops
- A "findings I'm deliberately deferring" mechanism prevents junk clusters created just to meet coverage requirements

**Design-sensitive perspective** recommended 2 phases (observe → structure), with the key innovation being **structured observations with finding-ID citations** instead of prose with character minimums. Key insights:
- 5 stages risks becoming bureaucratic box-checking worse than the original problem
- The best guardrails make the right thing feel natural, not forced
- Specificity (finding ID references) is a better guard against skimming than character count
- Reports should be functional artifacts surfaced during execution, not just compliance documents
- Invest in enriched display (group findings by file, highlight contradictions) to make observation productive
- Store observations as structured data in the plan so they're queryable during execution

## My Synthesis: 3 Stages

**Stage 1: OBSERVE** — "Read everything"
- Agent reads all findings, writes per-finding notes and identifies tensions
- Tool validates: ≥80% of findings cited by ID, minimum total length (300 chars)
- Per-finding observations get written to `plan["overrides"][fid]["note"]` so they surface in `next` cards during execution

**Stage 2: ORGANIZE** — "Decide everything"  
- Agent creates clusters, assigns findings, explains grouping/ordering/conflicts in one report
- Tool validates: ≥1 cluster exists with findings, every finding either clustered or explicitly deferred, queue reordered, first arc focused
- Cluster root-cause descriptions go into `plan["clusters"][name]["description"]`

**Stage 3: COMMIT** — "Own it"
- Agent writes the strategy + per-cluster execution plan
- Tool validates: every cluster name mentioned, minimum length (200 chars)
- Strategy goes into `plan["epic_synthesis_meta"]["strategy_summary"]`
- This step removes synthesis::pending from the queue

## Existing Data Model (key parts)

The plan is stored in `.desloppify/plan.json` as a `PlanModel` TypedDict:
```python
class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    queue_order: list[str]
    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]  # Cluster has: name, description, finding_ids, root_cause, etc.
    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]  # ItemOverride has: finding_id, description, note
    epic_synthesis_meta: EpicSynthesisMeta  # Has: strategy_summary, finding_snapshot_hash, etc.
```

Per-finding notes (`overrides[fid]["note"]`) already flow through to `next` card rendering with zero code changes. Cluster descriptions already render on cluster cards.

Stage data would be stored in `epic_synthesis_meta.synthesis_stages`:
```python
class SynthesisStageReport(TypedDict, total=False):
    report: str
    completed_at: str
    finding_refs: list[str]  # finding IDs referenced in the report
```
```
epic_synthesis_meta["synthesis_stages"] = {
    "observe": SynthesisStageReport,
    "organize": SynthesisStageReport, 
    "commit": SynthesisStageReport,
}
```

## CLI Interface

Reports are submitted via `--report` flag (text string) or `--report-file` (path to file for longer reports):
```bash
desloppify plan synthesize                                    # shows findings + stage status
desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "..."     # submit observation
desloppify plan synthesize --stage organize --report "..."    # submit organization rationale
desloppify plan synthesize --stage commit --report "..."      # submit strategy, completes synthesis
```

The tool extracts finding IDs from the report text using pattern matching against known IDs (full ID or 8-char hash suffix). This is the primary mechanical check.

## Files to Modify

1. `desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py` — add `SynthesisStageReport` TypedDict, add `synthesis_stages` to `EpicSynthesisMeta`
2. `desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups_admin.py` — update `plan synthesize` args: add `--stage`, `--report`, `--report-file`
3. `desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesize_handlers.py` — major rewrite: stage routing, validation, enriched display
4. `desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py` — reset stages when finding hash changes in `sync_synthesis_needed`
5. `desloppify/engine/plan.py` — export new types, update banner
6. `desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py` — update synthesis card to show stage progress
7. `desloppify/app/commands/plan/cluster_handlers.py` — update progress hint
8. `docs/SKILL.md` — update planning section

## Your Task

Design a detailed, file-by-file implementation plan. For each file:
- What exactly changes (function signatures, new functions, modified logic)
- Specific validation logic for each stage
- Edge cases: what happens with 0-2 findings? What about landscape changes mid-stage? What about voluntary stage reset?
- The enriched display for `plan synthesize` (grouping by file, highlighting tensions)

Be concrete — give pseudocode for the validation functions and the stage submission handler. Think about the implementation complexity and where to keep it simple vs. where to be thorough.

   122→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   123→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   124→
   125→    Handles migrations:
   126→    - v1 → v2: deferred list → skipped dict
   127→    - v3 → v4: epics dict → synthesis-clusters in clusters dict
   128→    """
   129→    defaults = empty_plan()
   130→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   131→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   132→
   133→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   134→        plan["queue_order"] = []
   135→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
   136→        plan["deferred"] = []
   137→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
   138→        plan["skipped"] = {}
   139→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
   140→        plan["overrides"] = {}
   141→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
   142→        plan["clusters"] = {}
   143→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
   144→        plan["superseded"] = {}
   145→    if not isinstance(plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta"), dict):
   146→        plan["epic_synthesis_meta"] = {}
   147→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deleted_clusters"), list):
   148→        plan["deleted_clusters"] = []
   149→
   150→    # Migrate deferred → skipped (v1 → v2)
   151→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   152→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   153→    if deferred:
   154→        now = utc_now()
   155→        for fid in list(deferred):
   156→            if fid not in skipped:
   157→                skipped[fid] = {
   158→                    "finding_id": fid,
   159→                    "kind": "temporary",
   160→                    "reason": None,
   161→                    "note": None,
   162→                    "attestation": None,
   163→                    "created_at": now,
   164→                    "review_after": None,
   165→                    "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   166→                }
   167→        deferred.clear()
   168→
   169→    # Normalize cluster fields
   170→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
   171→        if isinstance(cluster, dict):
   172→            if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
   173→                cluster["finding_ids"] = []
   174→            cluster.setdefault("auto", False)
   175→            cluster.setdefault("cluster_key", "")
   176→            cluster.setdefault("action", None)
   177→            cluster.setdefault("user_modified", False)

   180→    old_epics = plan.pop("epics", None)
   181→    if old_epics and isinstance(old_epics, dict):
   182→        clusters = plan["clusters"]
   183→        now = utc_now()
   184→        for name, epic in old_epics.items():
   185→            if name not in clusters:
   186→                clusters[name] = {

   200→                    "dependency_order": epic.get("dependency_order", 999),
   201→                    "action_steps": epic.get("action_steps", []),
   202→                    "status": epic.get("status", "pending"),
   203→                    "synthesis_version": epic.get("synthesis_version", 0),
   204→                    "supersedes": epic.get("supersedes", []),

   209→
   210→def synthesis_clusters(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   211→    """Return clusters that were created by epic synthesis (have a thesis)."""
   212→    return {
   213→        name: cluster
   214→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   215→        if cluster.get("thesis")
   216→    }
   217→
   218→
   219→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   220→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   221→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   222→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   223→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   224→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   225→
   226→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   227→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   228→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   229→    if overlap:
   230→        raise ValueError(
   231→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   232→        )
   233→
   234→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   235→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   236→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   237→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   238→            raise ValueError(
   239→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   240→            )
   241→
   242→
   243→__all__ = [
   244→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   245→    "EpicSynthesisMeta",
   246→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   247→    "Cluster",
   248→    "ItemOverride",
   249→    "PlanModel",
   250→    "SkipEntry",
   251→    "SupersededEntry",
   252→    "VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS",
   253→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   254→    "empty_plan",
   255→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   256→    "synthesis_clusters",
   257→    "validate_plan",
   258→]
   259→

1→"""Epic synthesis engine — LLM-driven meta-plan for review findings.
     2→
     3→Clusters review findings by root cause, dismisses false positives,
     4→resolves contradictions, and orders by dependency. The synthesis is incremental:
     5→it updates existing synthesis-clusters rather than recreating from scratch.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import json
    11→import logging

    13→from typing import Any
    14→

    28→
    29→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    30→# Data types
    31→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→
    33→@dataclass
    34→class SynthesisInput:
    35→    """All data the LLM needs to produce/update epics."""
    36→
    37→    open_findings: dict[str, dict]       # id -> finding (review + concerns)
    38→    mechanical_findings: dict[str, dict]  # id -> finding (non-review, for context)
    39→    existing_epics: dict[str, Cluster]    # current synthesis-clusters to update
    40→    dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]      # for context
    41→    new_since_last: set[str]             # finding IDs new since last synthesis
    42→    resolved_since_last: set[str]        # finding IDs resolved since last
    43→    previously_dismissed: list[str]      # IDs dismissed in prior synthesis
    44→    synthesis_version: int               # next version number
    45→
    46→
    47→@dataclass
    48→class DismissedFinding:
    49→    """A finding the LLM says doesn't make sense."""
    50→
    51→    finding_id: str
    52→    reason: str
    53→
    54→
    55→@dataclass
    56→class ContradictionNote:
    57→    """Record of a resolved contradiction."""
    58→
    59→    kept: str
    60→    dismissed: str
    61→    reason: str
    62→
    63→
    64→@dataclass
    65→class SynthesisResult:
    66→    """Parsed and validated LLM synthesis output."""
    67→
    68→    strategy_summary: str
    69→    epics: list[dict]
    70→    dismissed_findings: list[DismissedFinding] = field(default_factory=list)
    71→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = field(default_factory=list)
    72→    priority_rationale: str = ""
    73→
    74→
    75→@dataclass
    76→class SynthesisMutationResult:
    77→    """What changed when synthesis was applied to the plan."""
    78→
    79→    epics_created: int = 0
    80→    epics_updated: int = 0
    81→    epics_completed: int = 0
    82→    findings_dismissed: int = 0
    83→    findings_reassigned: int = 0
    84→    strategy_summary: str = ""

    86→    dry_run: bool = False

    89→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    90→# Input collection
    91→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    92→
    93→def collect_synthesis_input(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> SynthesisInput:
    94→    """Gather all data needed for the synthesis LLM prompt."""
    95→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    96→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    97→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    98→    epics = synthesis_clusters(plan)
    99→
   100→    open_review: dict[str, dict] = {}
   101→    open_mechanical: dict[str, dict] = {}
   102→    for fid, f in findings.items():
   103→        if f.get("status") != "open":
   104→            continue
   105→        if f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns"):
   106→            open_review[fid] = f
   107→        else:
   108→            open_mechanical[fid] = f
   109→
   110→    synthesized_ids = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   111→    current_review_ids = set(open_review.keys())
   112→    new_since = current_review_ids - synthesized_ids
   113→    resolved_since = synthesized_ids - current_review_ids
   114→    previously_dismissed = list(meta.get("dismissed_ids", []))
   115→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
   116→
   117→    return SynthesisInput(
   118→        open_findings=open_review,
   119→        mechanical_findings=open_mechanical,
   120→        existing_epics=dict(epics),
   121→        dimension_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   122→        new_since_last=new_since,
   123→        resolved_since_last=resolved_since,
   124→        previously_dismissed=previously_dismissed,
   125→        synthesis_version=version,
   126→    )
