```
   128→
   129→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   130→# Prompt construction
   131→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   132→
   133→_SYNTHESIS_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
   134→You are maintaining the meta-plan for this codebase. Your goal is to produce
   135→a coherent, prioritized strategy to address ALL open review findings.
   136→
   137→Your plan should:
   138→- Cluster findings by ROOT CAUSE, not by dimension or detector
   139→- Give each cluster (epic) a clear thesis: one imperative sentence
   140→- Order epics by dependency: what must be done first for later work to make sense
   141→- Dismiss findings that don't make sense, are contradictory, or are false positives
   142→- Mark which epics are agent-safe (can be executed mechanically) vs need human judgment
   143→- Avoid creating work that contradicts other work in the plan
   144→- Be ambitious but realistic — aim to resolve all findings coherently
   145→
   146→Available directions for epics: delete, merge, flatten, enforce, simplify, decompose, extract, inline.
   147→
   148→Available plan tools (the agent executing your plan has access to these):
   149→- `desloppify plan queue` — view all items in priority order
   150→- `desloppify plan focus epic/<name>` — focus the queue on one epic
   151→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --permanent --note "why" --attest "..."` — permanently dismiss
   152→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --note "revisit later"` — temporarily defer
   153→- `desloppify plan done <id> --note "what I did" --attest "..."` — mark resolved
   154→- `desloppify plan move <id> top|bottom|before|after <target>` — reorder
   155→- `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` — inspect a cluster
   156→- `desloppify scan` — re-scan after making changes to verify progress
   157→- `desloppify show review --status open` — see all open review findings
   158→
   159→Your output defines the ENTIRE work plan. Findings not assigned to any epic
   160→will remain in the queue as individual items. Dismissed findings will be
   161→removed from the queue with your stated reason.
   162→
   163→Respond with a single JSON object matching this schema:
   164→{
   165→  "strategy_summary": "2-4 sentence narrative: what the meta-plan says, top priorities, current state",
   166→  "epics": [
   167→    {
   168→      "name": "slug-name",
   169→      "thesis": "imperative one-liner",
   170→      "direction": "delete|merge|flatten|enforce|simplify|decompose|extract|inline",
   171→      "root_cause": "why this cluster exists",
   172→      "finding_ids": ["id1", "id2"],
   173→      "dismissed": ["id3"],
   174→      "agent_safe": true,
   175→      "dependency_order": 1,
   176→      "action_steps": ["step 1", "step 2"],
   177→      "status": "pending"
   178→    }
   179→  ],
   180→  "dismissed_findings": [
   181→    {"finding_id": "id", "reason": "why this finding doesn't make sense"}
   182→  ],
   183→  "contradiction_notes": [
   184→    {"kept": "finding_id", "dismissed": "finding_id", "reason": "why"}
   185→  ],
   186→  "priority_rationale": "why the dependency_order is what it is"
   187→}
   188→"""
   189→
   190→
   191→def build_synthesis_prompt(si: SynthesisInput) -> str:
   192→    """Build the user-facing prompt content with all finding data."""
   193→    parts: list[str] = []
   194→
   195→    # Section: existing epics
   196→    if si.existing_epics:
   197→        parts.append("## Existing Epics (update these, don't recreate)")
   198→        for name, epic in sorted(si.existing_epics.items()):
   199→            status = epic.get("status", "pending")
   200→            thesis = epic.get("thesis", "")
   201→            direction = epic.get("direction", "")
   202→            fids = epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   203→            parts.append(
   204→                f"- {name} [{status}] ({direction}): {thesis}"
   205→                f"\n  Findings: {', '.join(fids[:10])}"
   206→                f"{'...' if len(fids) > 10 else ''}"
   207→            )
   208→        parts.append("")
   209→
   210→    # Section: what changed
   211→    if si.new_since_last:
   212→        parts.append(f"## New findings since last synthesis ({len(si.new_since_last)})")
   213→        for fid in sorted(si.new_since_last):
   214→            f = si.open_findings.get(fid, {})
   215→            parts.append(f"- {fid}: {f.get('summary', '(no summary)')}")
   216→        parts.append("")
   217→
   218→    if si.resolved_since_last:
   219→        parts.append(f"## Resolved since last synthesis ({len(si.resolved_since_last)})")
   220→        for fid in sorted(si.resolved_since_last):
   221→            parts.append(f"- {fid}")
   222→        parts.append("")
   223→
   224→    # Section: all open review findings
   225→    parts.append(f"## All open review findings ({len(si.open_findings)})")
   226→    for fid, f in sorted(si.open_findings.items()):
   227→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   228→        suggestion = detail.get("suggestion", "")
   229→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
   230→        confidence = f.get("confidence", "medium")
   231→        file_path = f.get("file", "")
   232→        summary = f.get("summary", "")
   233→        parts.append(f"- [{confidence}] {fid}")
   234→        parts.append(f"  File: {file_path}")
   235→        if dimension:
   236→            parts.append(f"  Dimension: {dimension}")
   237→        parts.append(f"  Summary: {summary}")
   238→        if suggestion:
   239→            parts.append(f"  Suggestion: {suggestion}")
   240→    parts.append("")
   241→
   242→    # Section: dimension scores for context
   243→    if si.dimension_scores:
   244→        parts.append("## Dimension scores (context)")
   245→        for name, data in sorted(si.dimension_scores.items()):
   246→            if isinstance(data, dict):
   247→                score = data.get("score", "?")
   248→                strict = data.get("strict", score)
   249→                issues = data.get("issues", 0)
   250→                parts.append(f"- {name}: {score}% (strict: {strict}%, {issues} issues)")
   251→        parts.append("")
   252→
   253→    # Section: previously dismissed
   254→    if si.previously_dismissed:
   255→        parts.append(f"## Previously dismissed ({len(si.previously_dismissed)})")
   256→        parts.append("Maintain unless contradicted by new evidence.")
   257→        for fid in si.previously_dismissed:
   258→            parts.append(f"- {fid}")
   259→        parts.append("")
   260→
   261→    return "\n".join(parts)
   262→
   263→
   264→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   265→# Result parsing
   266→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   267→
   268→def parse_synthesis_result(raw: dict, valid_ids: set[str]) -> SynthesisResult:
   269→    """Parse and validate raw LLM output into a SynthesisResult.
   270→
   271→    Invalid finding IDs are silently dropped from epics and dismissals.
   272→    """
   273→    strategy_summary = str(raw.get("strategy_summary", ""))
   274→
   275→    epics: list[dict] = []
   276→    for raw_epic in raw.get("epics", []):
   277→        if not isinstance(raw_epic, dict):
   278→            continue
   279→        name = str(raw_epic.get("name", "")).strip()
   280→        if not name:
   281→            continue
   282→        # Validate direction
   283→        direction = str(raw_epic.get("direction", "simplify")).strip()
   284→        if direction not in VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS:
   285→            direction = "simplify"
   286→        # Filter to valid finding IDs
   287→        finding_ids = [
   288→            fid for fid in raw_epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   289→            if isinstance(fid, str) and fid in valid_ids
   290→        ]
   291→        dismissed = [
   292→            fid for fid in raw_epic.get("dismissed", [])
   293→            if isinstance(fid, str) and fid in valid_ids
   294→        ]
   295→        action_steps = [
   296→            str(s) for s in raw_epic.get("action_steps", [])
   297→            if isinstance(s, str)
   298→        ]
   299→
   300→        epics.append({
   301→            "name": name,
   302→            "thesis": str(raw_epic.get("thesis", "")),
   303→            "direction": direction,
   304→            "root_cause": str(raw_epic.get("root_cause", "")),
   305→            "finding_ids": finding_ids,
   306→            "dismissed": dismissed,
   307→            "agent_safe": bool(raw_epic.get("agent_safe", False)),
   308→            "dependency_order": int(raw_epic.get("dependency_order", 999)),
   309→            "action_steps": action_steps,
   310→            "status": str(raw_epic.get("status", "pending")),
   311→        })
   312→
   313→    dismissed_findings: list[DismissedFinding] = []
   314→    for d in raw.get("dismissed_findings", []):
   315→        if not isinstance(d, dict):
   316→            continue
   317→        fid = str(d.get("finding_id", ""))
   318→        if fid in valid_ids:
   319→            dismissed_findings.append(
   320→                DismissedFinding(finding_id=fid, reason=str(d.get("reason", "")))
   321→            )
   322→
   323→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = []
   324→    for c in raw.get("contradiction_notes", []):
   325→        if not isinstance(c, dict):
   326→            continue
   327→        contradiction_notes.append(ContradictionNote(
   328→            kept=str(c.get("kept", "")),
   329→            dismissed=str(c.get("dismissed", "")),
   330→            reason=str(c.get("reason", "")),
   331→        ))
   332→
   333→    priority_rationale = str(raw.get("priority_rationale", ""))
   334→
   335→    return SynthesisResult(
   336→        strategy_summary=strategy_summary,
   337→        epics=epics,
   338→        dismissed_findings=dismissed_findings,
   339→        contradiction_notes=contradiction_notes,
   340→        priority_rationale=priority_rationale,
   341→    )
   342→
   343→
   344→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   345→# Plan mutation
   346→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   347→
   348→def apply_synthesis_to_plan(
   349→    plan: PlanModel,
   350→    state: StateModel,
   351→    synthesis: SynthesisResult,
   352→    *,
   353→    trigger: str = "manual",
   354→) -> SynthesisMutationResult:
   355→    """Apply parsed synthesis result to the living plan.
   356→
   357→    1. Creates/updates synthesis-clusters in plan["clusters"]
   358→    2. Marks dismissed findings as synthesized_out skips
   359→    3. Reorders queue_order to group epic members by dependency_order
   360→    4. Updates epic_synthesis_meta with snapshot hash
   361→    """
   362→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   363→    now = utc_now()
   364→    result = SynthesisMutationResult()
   365→    result.strategy_summary = synthesis.strategy_summary
   366→
   367→    clusters = plan["clusters"]
   368→    skipped: dict = plan["skipped"]
   369→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   370→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   371→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
   372→    result.synthesis_version = version
   373→
   374→    # --- Update/create synthesis-clusters ------------------------------------
   375→    for epic_data in sorted(synthesis.epics, key=lambda e: e.get("dependency_order", 999)):
   376→        raw_name = epic_data["name"]
   377→        epic_name = f"{EPIC_PREFIX}{raw_name}" if not raw_name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX) else raw_name
   378→
   379→        existing = clusters.get(epic_name)
   380→        if existing and existing.get("thesis"):
   381→            # Update existing synthesis-cluster
   382→            existing["thesis"] = epic_data["thesis"]
   383→            existing["direction"] = epic_data["direction"]
   384→            existing["root_cause"] = epic_data.get("root_cause", "")
   385→            existing["finding_ids"] = epic_data["finding_ids"]
   386→            existing["dismissed"] = epic_data.get("dismissed", [])
   387→            existing["agent_safe"] = epic_data.get("agent_safe", False)
   388→            existing["dependency_order"] = epic_data["dependency_order"]
   389→            existing["action_steps"] = epic_data.get("action_steps", [])
   390→            existing["updated_at"] = now
   391→            existing["synthesis_version"] = version
   392→            existing["description"] = epic_data["thesis"]
   393→            # Don't overwrite in_progress status from agent
   394→            if existing.get("status") != "in_progress":
   395→                existing["status"] = epic_data.get("status", "pending")
   396→            result.epics_updated += 1
   397→        else:
   398→            # Create new synthesis-cluster
   399→            cluster: Cluster = {
   400→                "name": epic_name,
   401→                "description": epic_data["thesis"],
   402→                "finding_ids": epic_data["finding_ids"],
   403→                "auto": True,
   404→                "cluster_key": f"epic::{epic_name}",
   405→                "action": f"desloppify plan focus {epic_name}",
   406→                "user_modified": False,
   407→                "created_at": now,
   408→                "updated_at": now,
   409→                # Epic fields
   410→                "thesis": epic_data["thesis"],
   411→                "direction": epic_data["direction"],
   412→                "root_cause": epic_data.get("root_cause", ""),
   413→                "supersedes": [],
   414→                "dismissed": epic_data.get("dismissed", []),
   415→                "agent_safe": epic_data.get("agent_safe", False),
   416→                "dependency_order": epic_data["dependency_order"],
   417→                "action_steps": epic_data.get("action_steps", []),
   418→                "source_clusters": [],
   419→                "status": epic_data.get("status", "pending"),
   420→                "synthesis_version": version,
   421→            }
   422→            clusters[epic_name] = cluster
   423→            result.epics_created += 1
   424→
   425→    # --- Dismiss findings ---------------------------------------------------
   426→    dismissed_ids: list[str] = []
   427→    for df in synthesis.dismissed_findings:
   428→        fid = df.finding_id
   429→        dismissed_ids.append(fid)
   430→        # Remove from queue, add to skipped as synthesized_out
   431→        if fid in order:
   432→            order.remove(fid)
   433→        skipped[fid] = {
   434→            "finding_id": fid,
   435→            "kind": "synthesized_out",
   436→            "reason": df.reason,
   437→            "note": f"Dismissed by epic synthesis v{version}",
   438→            "attestation": None,
   439→            "created_at": now,
   440→            "review_after": None,
   441→            "skipped_at_scan": int(state.get("scan_count", 0)),
   442→        }
   443→        result.findings_dismissed += 1
   444→
   445→    # Also handle per-epic dismissed lists
   446→    for epic_data in synthesis.epics:
   447→        for fid in epic_data.get("dismissed", []):
   448→            if fid not in dismissed_ids and fid in order:
   449→                order.remove(fid)
   450→                dismissed_ids.append(fid)
   451→                skipped[fid] = {
   452→                    "finding_id": fid,
   453→                    "kind": "synthesized_out",
   454→                    "reason": f"Dismissed by epic synthesis v{version}",
   455→                    "note": None,
   456→                    "attestation": None,
   457→                    "created_at": now,
   458→                    "review_after": None,
   459→                    "skipped_at_scan": int(state.get("scan_count", 0)),
   460→                }
   461→                result.findings_dismissed += 1
   462→
   463→    # --- Reorder queue: epic findings grouped by dependency_order -----------
   464→    epic_finding_ids: set[str] = set()
   465→    epic_ordered_ids: list[str] = []
   466→    for epic_data in sorted(synthesis.epics, key=lambda e: e.get("dependency_order", 999)):
   467→        for fid in epic_data["finding_ids"]:
   468→            if fid not in epic_finding_ids and fid not in dismissed_ids:
   469→                epic_finding_ids.add(fid)
   470→                epic_ordered_ids.append(fid)
   471→
   472→    # Rebuild order: epic items first (by dependency), then non-epic items in original order
   473→    non_epic_items = [fid for fid in order if fid not in epic_finding_ids]
   474→    # Insert epic items at the front (after synthesis::pending if present)
   475→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SYNTHESIS_ID
   476→    new_order: list[str] = []
   477→    # Keep synthesis::pending at front if present (it will be pruned next sync)
   478→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in non_epic_items:
   479→        non_epic_items.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
   480→    new_order.extend(epic_ordered_ids)
   481→    new_order.extend(non_epic_items)
   482→    order.clear()
   483→    order.extend(new_order)
   484→
   485→    # --- Update synthesis meta -----------------------------------------------
   486→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   487→    open_review_ids = sorted(
   488→        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   489→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   490→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   491→    )
   492→
   493→    plan["epic_synthesis_meta"] = {
   494→        "synthesized_ids": open_review_ids,
   495→        "last_run": now,
   496→        "version": version,
   497→        "dismissed_ids": dismissed_ids,
   498→        "finding_snapshot_hash": current_hash,
   499→        "strategy_summary": synthesis.strategy_summary,
   500→        "trigger": trigger,
   501→    }
   502→    plan["updated"] = now
   503→
   504→    return result
   505→
   506→
   507→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   508→# Top-level orchestrator
   509→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   510→
   511→@dataclass
   512→class SynthesisDeps:
   513→    """Injectable dependencies for the synthesis engine."""
   514→
   515→    llm_call: Any = None  # Callable[[str, str], str] — (system, user) -> response
   516→
   517→
   518→def synthesize_epics(
   519→    plan: PlanModel,
   520→    state: StateModel,
   521→    *,
   522→    deps: SynthesisDeps | None = None,
   523→    dry_run: bool = False,
   524→    trigger: str = "manual",
   525→) -> SynthesisMutationResult:
   526→    """Run epic synthesis: collect input, call LLM, apply results.
   527→
   528→    If ``dry_run`` is True, collects input and builds prompt but does not
   529→    call the LLM or mutate the plan.
   530→
   531→    If ``deps.llm_call`` is None, returns a dry-run result with the prompt.
   532→    """
   533→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   534→    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, state)
   535→
   536→    prompt = build_synthesis_prompt(si)
   537→    valid_ids = set(si.open_findings.keys())
   538→
   539→    if dry_run or deps is None or deps.llm_call is None:
   540→        result = SynthesisMutationResult(dry_run=True)
   541→        result.strategy_summary = f"[dry-run] Prompt built with {len(si.open_findings)} findings"
   542→        return result
   543→
   544→    # Call LLM
   545→    try:
   546→        raw_response = deps.llm_call(_SYNTHESIS_SYSTEM_PROMPT, prompt)
   547→        raw_json = json.loads(raw_response)
   548→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   549→        logger.error("Epic synthesis LLM response parse error: %s", exc)
   550→        result = SynthesisMutationResult()
   551→        result.strategy_summary = f"Synthesis failed: {exc}"
   552→        return result
   553→
   554→    synthesis = parse_synthesis_result(raw_json, valid_ids)
   555→    return apply_synthesis_to_plan(plan, state, synthesis, trigger=trigger)
   556→
   557→
   558→__all__ = [
   559→    "DismissedFinding",
   560→    "SynthesisDeps",
   561→    "SynthesisInput",
   562→    "SynthesisMutationResult",
   563→    "SynthesisResult",
   564→    "apply_synthesis_to_plan",
   565→    "build_synthesis_prompt",
   566→    "collect_synthesis_input",
   567→    "parse_synthesis_result",
   568→    "synthesize_epics",
   569→]
   570→
```

```
    19→def _synthesis_coverage(plan: dict) -> tuple[int, int, dict]:
    20→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for synthesis progress."""
    21→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    22→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
    23→    for c in clusters.values():
    24→        all_cluster_ids.update(c.get("finding_ids", []))
    25→    queue_ids = [fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", []) if fid != SYNTHESIS_ID]
    26→    organized = sum(1 for fid in queue_ids if fid in all_cluster_ids)
    27→    return organized, len(queue_ids), clusters

    30→def _print_progress(plan: dict, open_findings: dict) -> None:
    31→    """Show cluster state and unclustered findings."""

    34→        print(colorize("\n  Current clusters:", "cyan"))
    35→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    36→            count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    37→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    38→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    39→            print(f"    {name}: {count} items{desc_str}")
    40→
    41→    all_clustered: set[str] = set()
    42→    for c in clusters.values():
    43→        all_clustered.update(c.get("finding_ids", []))
    44→    unclustered = [fid for fid in open_findings if fid not in all_clustered]
    45→    if unclustered:
    46→        print(colorize(f"\n  {len(unclustered)} findings not yet in a cluster:", "yellow"))
    47→        for fid in unclustered[:10]:
    48→            f = open_findings[fid]

    50→            print(f"    [{dim}] {f.get('summary', '')}")

    52→        if len(unclustered) > 10:

    54→    elif open_findings:
    55→        organized, total, _ = _synthesis_coverage(plan)
    56→        print(colorize(f"\n  All {organized}/{total} findings are in clusters.", "green"))
    57→        print(colorize(

    63→def _cmd_synthesize_complete(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    77→    if total > 0 and organized == 0:

    98→        if existing:
    99→            print(colorize(f"  Current strategy: {existing}", "dim"))
   100→            print(colorize('  Use --strategy "same" to keep it, or provide a new summary.', "dim"))
   101→        else:

   145→def cmd_plan_synthesize(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   146→    """Run LLM epic synthesis to create/update the meta-plan."""
```

```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import hashlib
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    20→SYNTHESIS_ID = "synthesis::pending"
    21→
    22→
    23→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    24→# Result dataclasses
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→
    27→@dataclass
    28→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    29→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    30→
    31→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    32→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→
    34→    @property
    35→    def changes(self) -> int:
    36→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    41→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    42→
    43→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    44→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    45→
    46→    @property
    47→    def changes(self) -> int:
    48→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    49→
    50→
    51→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52→# ID helpers
    53→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54→
    55→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    56→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    57→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    58→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    59→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    63→    if not dim_scores:
    64→        return set()
    65→
    66→    stale: set[str] = set()
    67→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    68→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    69→            continue
    70→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    71→        if dim_key:
    72→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    73→    return stale
    74→
    75→
    76→def _current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    77→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    78→
    79→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` directly because
    80→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` filters out placeholder dimensions
    81→    (they are hidden from the scorecard display).
    82→    """
    83→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    84→
    85→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    86→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    87→        return set()
    88→
    89→    unscored: set[str] = set()
    90→    for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    91→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    92→            continue
    93→        if not payload.get("placeholder"):
    94→            continue
    95→        if dim_key:
    96→            unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    97→    return unscored
    98→
    99→
   100→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   101→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   102→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   103→
   104→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   105→    plan: PlanModel,
   106→    state: StateModel,
   107→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   108→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   109→
   110→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   111→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   112→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   113→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   114→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   115→    """
   116→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   117→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   118→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   119→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   120→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   121→
   122→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   123→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   124→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   125→        fid for fid in order
   126→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   127→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   128→        and fid not in stale_ids
   129→    ]
   130→    for fid in to_remove:
   131→        order.remove(fid)
   132→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   133→
   134→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs to front ----------------------------
   135→    existing = set(order)
   136→    for uid in reversed(sorted(unscored_ids)):
   137→        if uid not in existing:
   138→            order.insert(0, uid)
   139→            result.injected.append(uid)
   140→
   141→    return result
   142→
   143→
   144→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   145→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   146→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   147→
   148→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   149→    plan: PlanModel,
   150→    state: StateModel,
   151→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   152→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
   153→
   154→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   155→       longer stale and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned by
   156→       ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   157→    2. If no objective items remain after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
   158→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
   159→    """
   160→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   161→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   162→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   163→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   164→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   165→
   166→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   167→    # Only prune IDs that are neither stale nor unscored.
   168→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   169→        fid for fid in order
   170→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   171→        and fid not in stale_ids
   172→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   173→    ]
   174→    for fid in to_remove:
   175→        order.remove(fid)
   176→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   177→
   178→    # --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------
   179→    has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
   180→    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
   181→        existing = set(order)
   182→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
   183→            if sid not in existing:
   184→                order.append(sid)
   185→                result.injected.append(sid)
   186→
   187→    return result
   188→
   189→
   190→@dataclass
   191→class SynthesisSyncResult:
   192→    """What changed during a synthesis sync."""
   193→
   194→    injected: bool = False
   195→    pruned: bool = False
   196→
   197→    @property
   198→    def changes(self) -> int:
   199→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   200→
   201→
   202→def review_finding_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   203→    """Hash of sorted open review/concerns finding IDs."""
   204→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   205→    ids = sorted(
   206→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   207→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   208→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   209→    )
   210→    if not ids:
   211→        return ""
   212→    return hashlib.md5("|".join(ids).encode()).hexdigest()
   213→
   214→
   215→def sync_synthesis_needed(
   216→    plan: PlanModel,
   217→    state: StateModel,
   218→) -> SynthesisSyncResult:
   219→    """Inject/prune synthesis::pending based on review finding drift.
   220→
   221→    Triggers when open review/concerns findings exist AND the snapshot
   222→    hash differs from the last-synthesized hash.
   223→    """
   224→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   225→    result = SynthesisSyncResult()
   226→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   227→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   228→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   229→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   230→    has_synthesis = SYNTHESIS_ID in order
   231→
   232→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   233→        # Need synthesis — inject if not present
   234→        if not has_synthesis:
   235→            order.insert(0, SYNTHESIS_ID)
   236→            result.injected = True
   237→    else:
   238→        # Synthesis up to date or no review findings — prune if present
   239→        if has_synthesis:
   240→            order.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
   241→            result.pruned = True
   242→
   243→    return result
   244→
   245→
   246→__all__ = [
   247→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   248→    "SYNTHESIS_ID",
   249→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
   250→    "SynthesisSyncResult",
   251→    "UnscoredDimensionSyncResult",
   252→    "_current_unscored_ids",
   253→    "review_finding_snapshot_hash",
   254→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   255→    "sync_synthesis_needed",
   256→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   257→]
   258→
```

```
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import sys
     7→
     8→from desloppify.languages import get_lang
     9→
    10→
    11→class _DeprecatedAction(argparse.Action):
    12→    """Argparse action that prints a deprecation warning and stores the value."""
    13→
    14→    def __call__(self, parser, namespace, values, option_string=None):
    15→        print(
    16→            f"Warning: {option_string} is deprecated and will be removed in a future version.",
    17→            file=sys.stderr,
    18→        )
    19→        setattr(namespace, self.dest, values)
    20→
    21→
    22→class _DeprecatedBoolAction(argparse.Action):
    23→    """Argparse action for deprecated boolean flags (store_true equivalent)."""
    24→
    25→    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
    26→        kwargs.setdefault("nargs", 0)
    27→        kwargs.setdefault("const", True)
    28→        kwargs.setdefault("default", False)
    29→        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
    30→
    31→    def __call__(self, parser, namespace, values, option_string=None):
    32→        print(
    33→            f"Warning: {option_string} is deprecated and will be removed in a future version.",
    34→            file=sys.stderr,
    35→        )
    36→        setattr(namespace, self.dest, True)
    37→
    38→
    39→def _add_detect_parser(sub, detector_names: list[str]) -> None:
    40→    p_detect = sub.add_parser(
    41→        "detect",
    42→        help="Run a single detector directly (bypass state)",
    43→        epilog=f"detectors: {', '.join(detector_names)}",
    44→    )
    45→    p_detect.add_argument("detector", type=str, help="Detector to run")
    46→    p_detect.add_argument("--top", type=int, default=20, help="Max items to show (default: 20)")
    47→    p_detect.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
    48→    p_detect.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="Output as JSON")
    49→    p_detect.add_argument(
    50→        "--fix",
    51→        action="store_true",
    52→        help="Auto-fix detected issues (logs detector only)",
    53→    )
    54→    p_detect.add_argument(
    55→        "--category",
    56→        choices=["imports", "vars", "params", "all"],
    57→        default="all",
    58→        help="Filter unused by category",
    59→    )
    60→    p_detect.add_argument(
    61→        "--threshold",
    62→        type=float,
    63→        default=None,
    64→        help="LOC threshold (large) or similarity (dupes)",
    65→    )
    66→    p_detect.add_argument(
    67→        "--file", type=str, default=None, help="Show deps for specific file"
    68→    )
    69→    p_detect.add_argument(
    70→        "--lang-opt",
    71→        action="append",
    72→        default=None,
    73→        metavar="KEY=VALUE",
    74→        help="Language runtime option override (repeatable)",
    75→    )

    91→def _add_review_parser(sub) -> None:
    92→    p_review = sub.add_parser(
    93→        "review",
    94→        help="Prepare or import holistic subjective review",
    95→        description="Run holistic subjective reviews using LLM-based analysis.",
    96→        epilog="""\
    97→examples:
    98→  desloppify review --prepare
    99→  desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import
   100→  desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude
   101→  desloppify review --external-submit --session-id <id> --import findings.json
   102→  desloppify review --merge --similarity 0.8""",
   103→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   104→    )
   105→
   106→    # -- core options --
   107→    g_core = p_review.add_argument_group("core options")
   108→    g_core.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
   109→    g_core.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   110→    g_core.add_argument(
   111→        "--prepare",
   112→        action="store_true",
   113→        help="Prepare review data (output to query.json)",
   114→    )
   115→    g_core.add_argument(
   116→        "--import",
   117→        dest="import_file",
   118→        type=str,
   119→        metavar="FILE",
   120→        help="Import review findings from JSON file",
   121→    )
   122→    g_core.add_argument(
   123→        "--validate-import",
   124→        dest="validate_import_file",
   125→        type=str,
   126→        metavar="FILE",
   127→        help="Validate review import payload and selected trust mode without mutating state",
   128→    )
   129→    g_core.add_argument(
   130→        "--allow-partial",
   131→        action="store_true",
   132→        help=(
   133→            "Allow partial review import when invalid findings are skipped "
   134→            "(default: fail on any skipped finding)"
   135→        ),
   136→    )
   137→    g_core.add_argument(
   138→        "--dimensions",
   139→        type=str,
   140→        default=None,
   141→        help="Comma-separated dimensions to evaluate",
   142→    )
   143→    g_core.add_argument(
   144→        "--retrospective",
   145→        action="store_true",
   146→        help=(
   147→            "Include historical review issue status/note context in the packet "
   148→            "to support root-cause vs symptom analysis during review"
   149→        ),
   150→    )
   151→    g_core.add_argument(
   152→        "--retrospective-max-issues",
   153→        type=int,
   154→        default=30,
   155→        help="Max recent historical issues to include in review context (default: 30)",
   156→    )
   157→    g_core.add_argument(
   158→        "--retrospective-max-batch-items",
   159→        type=int,
   160→        default=20,
   161→        help="Max history items included per batch focus slice (default: 20)",
   162→    )
   163→    g_core.add_argument(
   164→        "--force-review-rerun",
   165→        action="store_true",
   166→        help="Bypass the objective-plan-drained gate for review reruns",
   167→    )
   168→
   169→    # -- external review --
   170→    g_external = p_review.add_argument_group("external review")
   171→    g_external.add_argument(
   172→        "--external-start",
   173→        action="store_true",
   174→        help=(
   175→            "Start a cloud external review session (generates blind packet, "
   176→            "session id/token, and reviewer template)"
   177→        ),
   178→    )
   179→    g_external.add_argument(
   180→        "--external-submit",
   181→        action="store_true",
   182→        help=(
   183→            "Submit external reviewer JSON via a started session; "
   184→            "CLI injects canonical provenance before import"
   185→        ),
   186→    )
   187→    g_external.add_argument(
   188→        "--session-id",
   189→        type=str,
   190→        default=None,
   191→        help="External review session id for --external-submit",
   192→    )
   193→    g_external.add_argument(
   194→        "--external-runner",
   195→        choices=["claude"],
   196→        default="claude",
   197→        help="External reviewer runner for --external-start (default: claude)",
   198→    )
   199→    g_external.add_argument(
   200→        "--session-ttl-hours",
   201→        type=int,
   202→        default=24,
   203→        help="External review session expiration in hours (default: 24)",
   204→    )
   205→
   206→    # -- batch execution --
   207→    g_batch = p_review.add_argument_group("batch execution")
   208→    g_batch.add_argument(
   209→        "--run-batches",
   210→        action="store_true",
   211→        help="Run holistic investigation batches with subagents and merge/import output",
   212→    )
   213→    g_batch.add_argument(
   214→        "--runner",
   215→        choices=["codex"],
   216→        default="codex",
   217→        help="Subagent runner backend (default: codex)",
   218→    )
   219→    g_batch.add_argument(
   220→        "--parallel", action="store_true", help="Run selected batches in parallel"
   221→    )
   222→    g_batch.add_argument(
   223→        "--max-parallel-batches",
   224→        type=int,
   225→        default=3,
   226→        help=(
   227→            "Max concurrent subagent batches when --parallel is enabled "
   228→            "(default: 3)"
   229→        ),
   230→    )
   231→    g_batch.add_argument(
   232→        "--batch-timeout-seconds",
   233→        type=int,
   234→        default=20 * 60,
   235→        help="Per-batch runner timeout in seconds (default: 1200)",
   236→    )
   237→    g_batch.add_argument(
   238→        "--batch-max-retries",
   239→        type=int,
   240→        default=1,
   241→        help=(
   242→            "Retries per failed batch for transient runner/network errors "
   243→            "(default: 1)"
   244→        ),
   245→    )
   246→    g_batch.add_argument(
   247→        "--batch-retry-backoff-seconds",
   248→        type=float,
   249→        default=2.0,
   250→        help=(
   251→            "Base backoff delay for transient batch retries in seconds "
   252→            "(default: 2.0)"
   253→        ),
   254→    )
   255→    g_batch.add_argument(
   256→        "--batch-heartbeat-seconds",
   257→        type=float,
   258→        default=15.0,
   259→        help=(
   260→            "Progress heartbeat interval during parallel batch runs in seconds "
   261→            "(default: 15.0)"
   262→        ),
   263→    )
   264→    g_batch.add_argument(
   265→        "--batch-stall-warning-seconds",
   266→        type=int,
   267→        default=0,
   268→        help=(
   269→            "Emit warning when a running batch exceeds this elapsed time "
   270→            "(0 disables warnings; does not terminate the batch)"
   271→        ),
   272→    )
   273→    g_batch.add_argument(
   274→        "--batch-stall-kill-seconds",
   275→        type=int,
   276→        default=120,
   277→        help=(
   278→            "Terminate a batch when output state is unchanged and runner streams are idle "
   279→            "for this many seconds (default: 120; 0 disables kill recovery)"
   280→        ),
   281→    )
   282→    g_batch.add_argument(
   283→        "--dry-run",
   284→        action="store_true",
   285→        help="Generate packet/prompts only (skip runner/import)",
   286→    )
   287→    g_batch.add_argument(
   288→        "--run-log-file",
   289→        type=str,
   290→        default=None,
   291→        help=(
   292→            "Optional explicit path for live run log output "
   293→            "(overrides default run artifacts path)"
   294→        ),
   295→    )
   296→    g_batch.add_argument(
   297→        "--packet",
   298→        type=str,
   299→        default=None,
   300→        help="Use an existing immutable packet JSON instead of preparing a new one",
   301→    )
   302→    g_batch.add_argument(
   303→        "--only-batches",
   304→        type=str,
   305→        default=None,
   306→        help="Comma-separated 1-based batch indexes to run (e.g. 1,3,5)",
   307→    )
   308→    g_batch.add_argument(
   309→        "--scan-after-import",
   310→        action="store_true",
   311→        help="Run `scan` after successful merged import",
   312→    )
   313→
   314→    # -- trust & attestation --
   315→    g_trust = p_review.add_argument_group("trust & attestation")
   316→    g_trust.add_argument(
   317→        "--manual-override",
   318→        action="store_true",
   319→        help=(
   320→            "Allow untrusted assessment score imports. Findings always import; "
   321→            "scores require trusted blind provenance unless this override is set."
   322→        ),
   323→    )
   324→    g_trust.add_argument(
   325→        "--attested-external",
   326→        action="store_true",
   327→        help=(
   328→            "Accept external blind-run assessments as durable scores when "
   329→            "paired with --attest and valid blind packet provenance "
   330→            "(intended for cloud Claude subagent workflows)."
   331→        ),
   332→    )
   333→    g_trust.add_argument(
   334→        "--attest",
   335→        type=str,
   336→        default=None,
   337→        help=(
   338→            "Required with --manual-override or --attested-external. "
   339→            "For attested external imports include both phrases "
   340→            "'without awareness' and 'unbiased'."
   341→        ),
   342→    )
   343→
   344→    # -- post-processing --
   345→    g_post = p_review.add_argument_group("post-processing")
   346→    g_post.add_argument(
   347→        "--merge",
   348→        action="store_true",
   349→        help="Merge conceptually duplicate open review findings",
   350→    )
   351→    g_post.add_argument(
   352→        "--similarity",
   353→        type=float,
   354→        default=0.8,
   355→        help="Summary similarity threshold for merge (0-1, default: 0.8)",
   356→    )
   357→
   358→    # -- deprecated --
   359→    g_deprecated = p_review.add_argument_group("deprecated")
   360→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   361→        "--max-age",
   362→        type=int,
   363→        default=None,
   364→        action=_DeprecatedAction,
   365→        help="Deprecated in holistic-only mode (ignored)",
   366→    )
   367→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   368→        "--max-files",
   369→        type=int,
   370→        default=None,
   371→        action=_DeprecatedAction,
   372→        help="Deprecated in holistic-only mode (ignored)",
   373→    )
   374→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   375→        "--refresh",
   376→        action=_DeprecatedBoolAction,
   377→        help="Deprecated in holistic-only mode (ignored)",
   378→    )
   379→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   380→        "--holistic",
   381→        action=_DeprecatedBoolAction,
   382→        help="Deprecated: holistic is now the only review mode",
   383→    )
   384→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   385→        "--save-run-log",
   386→        action="store_true",
   387→        help=(
   388→            "Deprecated no-op: run logs are now always saved while running "
   389→            "(default location: run artifacts dir)"
   390→        ),
   391→    )

   394→def _add_zone_parser(sub) -> None:
   395→    p_zone = sub.add_parser("zone", help="Show/set/clear zone classifications")
   396→    p_zone.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
   397→    p_zone.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   398→    zone_sub = p_zone.add_subparsers(dest="zone_action")
   399→    zone_sub.add_parser("show", help="Show zone classifications for all files")
   400→    z_set = zone_sub.add_parser("set", help="Override zone for a file")
   401→    z_set.add_argument("zone_path", type=str, help="Relative file path")
   402→    z_set.add_argument(
   403→        "zone_value",
   404→        type=str,
   405→        help="Zone (production, test, config, generated, script, vendor)",
   406→    )
   407→    z_clear = zone_sub.add_parser("clear", help="Remove zone override for a file")
   408→    z_clear.add_argument("zone_path", type=str, help="Relative file path")

   422→def _fixer_help_lines(langs: list[str]) -> list[str]:
   423→    fixer_help_lines: list[str] = []
   424→    for lang_name in langs:
   425→        try:
   426→            fixer_names = sorted(get_lang(lang_name).fixers.keys())
   427→        except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError):
   428→            fixer_names = []
   429→        fixer_list = ", ".join(fixer_names) if fixer_names else "none yet"
   430→        fixer_help_lines.append(f"fixers ({lang_name}): {fixer_list}")
   431→    fixer_help_lines.append("special: review — prepare structured review data")
   432→    return fixer_help_lines
   433→
   434→
   435→def _add_fix_parser(sub, langs: list[str]) -> None:
   436→    p_fix = sub.add_parser(
   437→        "fix",
   438→        help="Auto-fix mechanical issues",
   439→        epilog="\n".join(_fixer_help_lines(langs)),
   440→    )
   441→    p_fix.add_argument("fixer", type=str, help="What to fix")
   442→    p_fix.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
   443→    p_fix.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   444→    p_fix.add_argument(
   445→        "--dry-run",
   446→        action="store_true",
   447→        help="Show what would change without modifying files",
   448→    )
   449→
   450→
   451→def _add_plan_parser(sub) -> None:
   452→    p_plan = sub.add_parser(
   453→        "plan",
   454→        help="Living plan: generate, reorder, cluster, skip, note",
   455→        description="""\
   456→Manage the living plan — a persistent layer on top of the work queue.
   457→Track custom ordering, clusters, skips, and per-finding annotations.
   458→Run with no subcommand to generate a full prioritized markdown plan.""",
   459→        epilog="""\
   460→typical workflow:
   461→  desloppify scan                       # detect findings
   462→  desloppify plan                       # full prioritized markdown
   463→  desloppify plan queue                 # compact table of all items
   464→  desloppify plan cluster create ...    # group related findings
   465→  desloppify plan focus <cluster>       # narrow scope
   466→  desloppify next                       # work on the next item
   467→  desloppify plan done <id> --attest .. # mark as fixed
   468→
   469→subcommands:
   470→  show       Show plan metadata summary
   471→  queue      Compact table of upcoming queue items
   472→  reset      Reset plan to empty
   473→  move       Move findings in the queue
   474→  done       Mark findings as fixed (score movement + next-step)
   475→  describe   Set augmented description
   476→  note       Set note on findings
   477→  skip       Skip findings (temporary/permanent/false_positive)
   478→  unskip     Bring skipped findings back to queue
   479→  reopen     Reopen resolved findings
   480→  focus      Set or clear active cluster focus
   481→  cluster    Manage finding clusters""",
   482→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   483→    )
   484→    p_plan.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   485→    p_plan.add_argument(
   486→        "--output", type=str, metavar="FILE", help="Write to file instead of stdout"
   487→    )
   488→
   489→    plan_sub = p_plan.add_subparsers(dest="plan_action")
   490→
   491→    # plan show
   492→    plan_sub.add_parser("show", help="Show plan metadata summary")
   493→
   494→    # plan queue
   495→    p_queue = plan_sub.add_parser("queue", help="Compact table of upcoming queue items")
   496→    p_queue.add_argument("--top", type=int, default=30, help="Max items (default: 30, 0=all)")
   497→    p_queue.add_argument("--tier", type=int, choices=[1, 2, 3, 4], default=None,
   498→                         help="Show only items in this tier (T1=critical .. T4=cosmetic)")
   499→    p_queue.add_argument("--cluster", type=str, default=None, metavar="NAME",
   500→                         help="Filter to a specific cluster")
   501→    p_queue.add_argument("--include-skipped", action="store_true",
   502→                         help="Include skipped items at end")
   503→
   504→    # plan reset
   505→    plan_sub.add_parser("reset", help="Reset plan to empty")
   506→
   507→    # plan move <patterns> <position> [target|N]
   508→    p_move = plan_sub.add_parser(
   509→        "move",
   510→        help="Move findings in the queue",
   511→        epilog="""\
   512→examples:
   513→  desloppify plan move security top                    # prioritize a detector
   514→  desloppify plan move "unused::src/foo.ts::*" top     # prioritize by pattern
   515→  desloppify plan move smells bottom                   # deprioritize smells
   516→  desloppify plan move unused before security          # move before another""",
   517→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   518→    )
   519→    p_move.add_argument(
   520→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   521→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   522→    )
   523→    p_move.add_argument(
   524→        "position", choices=["top", "bottom", "before", "after", "up", "down"],
   525→        help="Where to move (top, bottom, before, after, up N, down N)",
   526→    )
   527→    p_move.add_argument(
   528→        "target", nargs="?", default=None,
   529→        help="Target finding ID (for before/after) or offset (for up/down)",
   530→    )
   531→
   532→    # plan describe <patterns> "<text>"
   533→    p_describe = plan_sub.add_parser("describe", help="Set augmented description")
   534→    p_describe.add_argument(
   535→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   536→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   537→    )
   538→    p_describe.add_argument("text", type=str, help="Description text")
   539→
   540→    # plan note <patterns> "<text>"
   541→    p_note = plan_sub.add_parser("note", help="Set note on findings")
   542→    p_note.add_argument(
   543→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   544→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   545→    )
   546→    p_note.add_argument("text", type=str, help="Note text")
   547→
   548→    # plan skip <patterns> [--reason] [--review-after N] [--permanent] [--false-positive] [--note] [--attest]
   549→    p_skip = plan_sub.add_parser(
   550→        "skip",
   551→        help="Skip findings: temporary (default), --permanent (wontfix), or --false-positive",
   552→    )
   553→    p_skip.add_argument(
   554→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   555→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   556→    )
   557→    p_skip.add_argument("--reason", type=str, default=None, help="Why this is being skipped")
   558→    p_skip.add_argument(
   559→        "--review-after", type=int, default=None, metavar="N",
   560→        help="Re-surface after N scans (temporary only)",
   561→    )
   562→    p_skip.add_argument(
   563→        "--permanent", action="store_true",
   564→        help="Mark as wontfix (score-affecting, requires --note and --attest)",
   565→    )
   566→    p_skip.add_argument(
   567→        "--false-positive", action="store_true",
   568→        help="Mark as false positive (requires --attest)",
   569→    )
   570→    p_skip.add_argument("--note", type=str, default=None, help="Explanation (required for --permanent)")
   571→    p_skip.add_argument(
   572→        "--attest", type=str, default=None,
   573→        help="Attestation (required for --permanent and --false-positive)",
   574→    )
   575→    p_skip.add_argument(
   576→        "--dry-run", action="store_true",
```

```
    65→def _render_grouped(items: list[dict], group: str) -> None:
    66→    grouped = group_queue_items(items, group)
    67→    for key, grouped_items in grouped.items():

    78→def is_auto_fix_command(command: str | None) -> bool:
    79→    cmd = (command or "").strip()
    80→    return cmd.startswith("desloppify fix ") and "--dry-run" in cmd

   112→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   113→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")

   121→        if len(file_counts) <= 5:

   322→                ds = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   323→                base += (
   324→                    f". Dimension: {dimension.name} at {ds['score']:.1f}% "
   325→                    f"({ds['issues']} open issues)"
   326→                )
   327→
   328→        # For review findings, show subjective dimension context
   329→        if item.get("detector") == "review" and dim_scores:
   330→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   331→            if dim_key:
   332→                for ds_name, ds_data in dim_scores.items():
   333→                    if ds_name.lower().replace(" ", "_") == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   334→                        score_val = ds_data.get("score", "?")
   335→                        score_str = f"{score_val:.1f}" if isinstance(score_val, (int, float)) else str(score_val)
   336→                        base += f". Subjective dimension: {ds_name} at {score_str}%"
   337→                        break
   338→
   339→        policy = explanation.get("policy")
   340→        if policy:
   341→            base = f"{base}. {policy}"
   342→        print(colorize(f"  explain: {base}", "dim"))
   343→
   344→
   345→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   346→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   347→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   348→        return
   349→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   350→    if not explain:
   351→        return
   352→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   353→    if available:
   354→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   355→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   356→
   357→
   358→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   359→    if queue.get("items"):
   360→        return False
   361→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   362→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   363→    if tier is not None:
   364→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   365→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   366→        if available:
   367→            commands = " | ".join(
   368→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   369→            )
   370→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   371→    return True
   372→
   373→
   374→def _render_strategy_summary(plan: dict | None) -> None:
   375→    """Show the meta-plan strategy summary when epics exist."""
   376→    if not plan:
   377→        return
   378→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   379→    summary = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   380→    if not summary:
   381→        return
   382→    print(colorize("\n  Meta-plan:", "bold"))
   383→    for line in summary.strip().splitlines():
   384→        print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
   385→
   386→
   387→def _render_synthesis_item(item: dict) -> None:
   388→    """Render the synthesis::pending card."""
   389→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   390→    total = detail.get("total_review_findings", 0)
   391→    new = detail.get("new_since_last", 0)
   392→    resolved = detail.get("resolved_since_last", 0)
   393→    trigger = detail.get("trigger", "unknown")
   394→    epic_count = detail.get("epic_count", 0)
   395→    pending = detail.get("pending_epics", 0)
   396→    in_prog = detail.get("in_progress_epics", 0)
   397→    completed = detail.get("completed_epics", 0)
   398→
   399→    print(colorize("  PLAN — do not proceed until complete", "bold"))
   400→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   401→    print(f"  {total} review findings need integration into the meta-plan.")
   402→    print(f"  Trigger: {trigger}")
   403→    print()
   404→    if new:
   405→        print(f"  New findings since last synthesis: {new}")
   406→    if resolved:
   407→        print(f"  Resolved since last synthesis: {resolved}")
   408→    if epic_count:
   409→        print(f"  Current epics: {epic_count} ({pending} pending, {in_prog} in progress, {completed} completed)")
   410→    print()
   411→    print(colorize("  This is a planning step. Do NOT start fixing code until this is done.", "yellow"))
   412→    print()
   413→    print("  Steps:")
   414→    print("    1. `desloppify plan synthesize`  — see all findings that need organizing")
   415→    print("    2. Group by root cause into clusters (create, add, move, focus)")
   416→    print('    3. `desloppify plan synthesize --complete --strategy "..."`')
   417→    print("    4. `desloppify next`  — start implementation")
   418→    print()
   419→    print(colorize("  Forbidden during this phase:", "red"))
   420→    print("    - desloppify fix / resolve / editing code / plan skip")
   421→    print()
   422→    print(colorize("  View plan:", "dim"))
   423→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan queue   — items in priority order", "dim"))
   424→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan show    — plan status summary", "dim"))
   425→
   426→
   427→def _render_epic_item(item: dict) -> None:
   428→    """Render an epic card."""
   429→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   430→    direction = detail.get("direction", "")
   431→    thesis = detail.get("thesis", "")
   432→    root_cause = detail.get("root_cause", "")
   433→    status = detail.get("epic_status", "pending")
   434→    agent_safe = detail.get("agent_safe", False)
   435→    dep_order = detail.get("dependency_order", 999)
   436→    total_epics = detail.get("total_epics", 0)
   437→    finding_ids = detail.get("finding_ids", [])
   438→    action_steps = detail.get("action_steps", [])
   439→
   440→    safe_tag = " agent-safe" if agent_safe else ""
   441→    print(colorize(f"  [Epic — {direction}] ({len(finding_ids)} findings,{safe_tag} dependency_order: {dep_order})", "bold"))
   442→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   443→    print(f"  Thesis: {thesis}")
   444→    if root_cause:
   445→        print(f"  Root cause: {root_cause}")
   446→    print(f"  Status: {status}")
   447→    if total_epics:
   448→        print(f"  This is part of the meta-plan (epic {dep_order} of {total_epics}).")
   449→    print()
   450→    if action_steps:
   451→        print("  Steps:")
   452→        for i, step in enumerate(action_steps, 1):
   453→            print(f"    {i}. {step}")
   454→        print()
   455→    if finding_ids:
   456→        shown = finding_ids[:5]
   457→        print("  Findings in this epic:")
   458→        for fid in shown:
   459→            print(f"    - {fid}")
   460→        if len(finding_ids) > 5:
   461→            print(f"    ... and {len(finding_ids) - 5} more")
   462→        print()
   463→    epic_name = item.get("id", "")
   464→    print(f"  Work on this epic:")
   465→    print(f"    desloppify plan focus {epic_name}")
   466→    print(f"    (then work through findings with desloppify next)")
   467→
   468→
   469→def render_terminal_items(
   470→    items: list[dict],
   471→    dim_scores: dict,
   472→    findings_scoped: dict,
   473→    *,
   474→    group: str,
   475→    explain: bool,
   476→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   477→    plan: dict | None = None,
   478→) -> None:
   479→    # Show strategy summary when epics exist
   480→    _render_strategy_summary(plan)
   481→
   482→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   483→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   484→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   485→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   486→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   487→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   488→        visible = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("kind") != "synthesis_needed")
   489→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({visible} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   490→
   491→        # Exclude the synthesis item from the focus count — it's a queue-level
   492→        # blocker, not a cluster member, so don't count it in "N of M remaining".
   493→        focus_items = [it for it in items if it.get("kind") != "synthesis_needed"]
   494→        if len(focus_items) == 0:
   495→            print(colorize(
   496→                "  This cluster has no remaining items. "
   497→                "Run `desloppify plan focus --clear` to unfocus, "
   498→                "or `desloppify plan cluster show` to inspect.",
   499→                "yellow",
   500→            ))
   501→
   502→    # When synthesis is pending, only show the planning card — don't leak
   503→    # implementation tasks that would tempt agents to skip the planning step.
   504→    if items and items[0].get("kind") == "synthesis_needed":
   505→        queue_pos = items[0].get("queue_position")
   506→        pos_str = f"  (#{queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   507→        print(colorize(f"  Next item{pos_str}", "bold"))
   508→        _render_synthesis_item(items[0])
   509→        return
   510→
   511→    if group != "item":
   512→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   513→        return
   514→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   515→        if idx > 0:
   516→            print()
   517→        kind = item.get("kind", "")
   518→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   519→
   520→        # All item types get the same position label — no visual hierarchy
   521→        # that makes synthetic items look like metadata instead of tasks.
   522→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   523→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   524→        elif len(items) > 1:
   525→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   526→        else:
   527→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   528→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   529→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   530→
   531→        if kind == "epic":
   532→            _render_epic_item(item)
   533→            continue
   534→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   535→
   536→
   537→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   538→    if len(items) != 1:
   539→        return
   540→    kind = items[0].get("kind", "")
   541→    if kind in ("cluster", "synthesis_needed", "epic"):
   542→        return  # These cards include their own resolution hints
   543→    if kind != "finding":
   544→        return
   545→    item = items[0]
   546→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   547→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   548→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   549→        primary = item.get(
   550→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   551→        )
   552→        print(f"    {primary}")
   553→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   554→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   555→        return
   556→
   557→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   558→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   559→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   560→        print(f"    {primary}")
   561→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   562→    else:
   563→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   564→
   565→    print(
   566→        f'    desloppify plan done "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   567→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   568→    )
   569→    print(
   570→        f'    desloppify plan skip --permanent "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   571→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   572→    )
   573→
   574→
   575→def render_followup_nudges(
   576→    state: dict,
   577→    dim_scores: dict,
   578→    findings_scoped: dict,
   579→    *,
   580→    strict_score: float | None,
   581→    target_strict_score: float,
   582→) -> None:
   583→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   584→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   585→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   586→        state,
   587→        subjective_entries,
   588→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   589→        max_quality_items=3,
   590→        max_integrity_items=5,
   591→    )
   592→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   593→    if strict_score is not None:
   594→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   595→        if gap > 0:
   596→            print(
   597→                colorize(
   598→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   599→                    "cyan",
   600→                )
   601→            )
   602→        else:
   603→            print(
   604→                colorize(
   605→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   606→                    "green",
   607→                )
   608→            )
   609→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   610→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   611→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   612→
   613→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   614→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   615→        findings_scoped
   616→    )
   617→    parts: list[str] = []
   618→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   619→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   620→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   621→    open_review = [
   622→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   623→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   624→    ]
   625→    if low_dims:
   626→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   627→    if stale_count:
   628→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   629→    if unassessed_count:
   630→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   631→    if len(open_review):
   632→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   633→    if coverage_open > 0:
   634→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   635→
   636→    if parts:
   637→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   638→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   639→
   640→
   641→__all__ = [
   642→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   643→    "render_followup_nudges",
   644→    "render_queue_header",
   645→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   646→    "render_terminal_items",
   647→    "scorecard_subjective",
   648→    "show_empty_queue",
   649→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   650→]
   651→
```

```
1→"""Plan cluster subcommand handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.plan._resolve import print_no_match_hint, resolve_ids_from_patterns
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    12→    SYNTHESIS_ID,
    13→    add_to_cluster,
    14→    create_cluster,
```
