```
    22→
    23→def _print_synthesis_progress(plan: dict) -> None:
    24→    """If synthesis is pending, print how many findings are organized into clusters."""
    25→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    26→        return
    27→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
    28→    for c in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    29→        all_cluster_ids.update(c.get("finding_ids", []))
    30→    queue_ids = [fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", []) if fid != SYNTHESIS_ID]
    31→    organized = sum(1 for fid in queue_ids if fid in all_cluster_ids)
    32→    total = len(queue_ids)
    33→    print(colorize(
    34→        f"  Synthesis progress: {organized}/{total} findings organized into clusters.",
    35→        "cyan",
    36→    ))
    37→    if organized < total:
    38→        print(colorize(
    39→            "  Continue organizing, or run `desloppify plan synthesize` to see remaining.",
    40→            "dim",
    41→        ))
    42→    else:
    43→        print(colorize(
    44→            '  All findings organized! Complete: `desloppify plan synthesize --complete --strategy "..."`',
    45→            "green",
    46→        ))
    47→
    48→
    49→def _cmd_cluster_create(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    50→    name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    51→    description: str | None = getattr(args, "description", None)
    52→    action: str | None = getattr(args, "action", None)
    53→    plan = load_plan()
    54→    try:
    55→        create_cluster(plan, name, description, action=action)
    56→    except ValueError as ex:
    57→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    58→        return
    59→    save_plan(plan)
    60→    print(colorize(f"  Created cluster: {name}", "green"))
    61→    _print_synthesis_progress(plan)
    62→
    63→
    64→def _cmd_cluster_add(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    65→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    66→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    67→        return
    68→
    69→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    70→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    71→
    72→    plan = load_plan()
    73→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    74→    if not finding_ids:
    75→        print_no_match_hint(patterns)
    76→        return
    77→    try:
    78→        count = add_to_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids)
    79→    except ValueError as ex:
    80→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    81→        return
    82→    save_plan(plan)
    83→    print(colorize(f"  Added {count} item(s) to cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
    84→    _print_synthesis_progress(plan)
    85→
    86→
    87→def _cmd_cluster_remove(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    88→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    89→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    90→        return
    91→
    92→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    93→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    94→
    95→    plan = load_plan()
    96→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    97→    if not finding_ids:
    98→        print_no_match_hint(patterns)
    99→        return
   100→    try:
   101→        count = remove_from_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids)
   102→    except ValueError as ex:
   103→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   104→        return
   105→    save_plan(plan)
   106→    print(colorize(f"  Removed {count} item(s) from cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
   107→
   108→
   109→def _cmd_cluster_delete(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   110→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   111→    plan = load_plan()
   112→    try:
   113→        orphaned = delete_cluster(plan, cluster_name)
   114→    except ValueError as ex:
   115→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   116→        return
   117→    save_plan(plan)
   118→    print(colorize(f"  Deleted cluster {cluster_name} ({len(orphaned)} items orphaned).", "green"))
   119→
   120→
   121→def _cmd_cluster_move(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   122→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   123→    position: str = getattr(args, "position", "top")
   124→    target: str | None = getattr(args, "target", None)
   125→
   126→    plan = load_plan()
   127→
   128→    offset: int | None = None
   129→    if position in ("up", "down") and target is not None:
   130→        try:
   131→            offset = int(target)
   132→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   133→            print(colorize(f"  Invalid offset: {target}", "red"))
   134→            return
   135→        target = None
   136→
   137→    try:
   138→        count = move_cluster(plan, cluster_name, position, target=target, offset=offset)
   139→    except ValueError as ex:
   140→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   141→        return
   142→    save_plan(plan)
   143→    if position in ("before", "after") and target:
   144→        print(colorize(f"  Moved cluster {cluster_name} ({count} items) to {position} {target}.", "green"))
   145→    elif position in ("up", "down") and offset is not None:
   146→        print(colorize(f"  Moved cluster {cluster_name} ({count} items) {position} {offset} position(s).", "green"))
   147→    else:
   148→        print(colorize(f"  Moved cluster {cluster_name} ({count} items) to {position}.", "green"))
   149→    _print_synthesis_progress(plan)
   150→
   151→
   152→def _print_cluster_member(
   153→    idx: int, fid: str, finding: dict | None, override: dict | None = None,
   154→) -> None:
   155→    """Print a single cluster member line with optional finding details."""
   156→    print(f"    {idx}. {fid}")
   157→    if not finding:
   158→        return
   159→    file = finding.get("file", "")
   160→    lines = finding.get("detail", {}).get("lines", [])
   161→    line_str = f" at lines: {', '.join(str(ln) for ln in lines)}" if lines else ""
   162→    if file and file != ".":
   163→        print(colorize(f"       File: {file}{line_str}", "dim"))
   164→    summary = finding.get("summary", "")
   165→    if summary:
   166→        print(colorize(f"       {summary}", "dim"))
   167→    if override:
   168→        if override.get("description"):
   169→            print(colorize(f"       Description: {override['description']}", "cyan"))
   170→        if override.get("note"):
   171→            print(colorize(f"       Note: {override['note']}", "dim"))
   172→
   173→
   174→def _load_findings_best_effort(args: argparse.Namespace) -> dict:
   175→    """Load findings from state, returning empty dict on failure."""
   176→    rt = command_runtime(args)
   177→    return rt.state.get("findings", {})
   178→
   179→
   180→def _cmd_cluster_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   181→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   182→    plan = load_plan()
   183→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name)
   184→    if cluster is None:
   185→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist.", "red"))
   186→        return
   187→
   188→    # Header
   189→    auto_tag = "Auto-generated" if cluster.get("auto") else "Manual"
   190→    cluster_key = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   191→    key_type = f" ({cluster_key.split('::', 1)[0]})" if cluster_key else ""
   192→    print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}", "bold"))
   193→    print(colorize(f"  Type: {auto_tag}{key_type}", "dim"))
   194→    desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   195→    if desc:
   196→        print(colorize(f"  Description: {desc}", "dim"))
   197→    action = cluster.get("action") or ""
   198→    if action:
   199→        print(colorize(f"  Action: {action}", "dim"))
   200→
   201→    # Members
   202→    finding_ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   203→    if not finding_ids:
   204→        print(colorize("  Members: (none)", "dim"))
   205→    else:
   206→        findings = _load_findings_best_effort(args)
   207→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   208→        print(colorize(f"  Members ({len(finding_ids)}):", "dim"))
   209→        for idx, fid in enumerate(finding_ids, 1):
   210→            _print_cluster_member(idx, fid, findings.get(fid), overrides.get(fid))
   211→
   212→    # Commands
   213→    print()
   214→    print(colorize("  Commands:", "dim"))
   215→    print(colorize(f'    Resolve all:  desloppify plan done "{cluster_name}" --note "<what>" --attest "..."', "dim"))
   216→    print(colorize(f"    Drill in:     desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10", "dim"))
   217→    print(colorize(f"    Skip:         desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   218→
   219→
   220→def _cmd_cluster_list(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   221→    plan = load_plan()
   222→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   223→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   224→    if not clusters:
   225→        print("  No clusters defined.")
   226→        return
   227→    print(colorize("  Clusters:", "bold"))
   228→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   229→        member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   230→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   231→        marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   232→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   233→        auto_tag = " [auto]" if cluster.get("auto") else ""
   234→        print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{auto_tag}{desc_str}{marker}")
   235→
   236→
   237→def cmd_cluster_dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   238→    """Route cluster subcommands."""
   239→    cluster_action = getattr(args, "cluster_action", None)
   240→    dispatch = {
   241→        "create": _cmd_cluster_create,
   242→        "add": _cmd_cluster_add,
   243→        "remove": _cmd_cluster_remove,
   244→        "delete": _cmd_cluster_delete,
   245→        "move": _cmd_cluster_move,
   246→        "show": _cmd_cluster_show,
   247→        "list": _cmd_cluster_list,
   248→    }
   249→    handler = dispatch.get(cluster_action)
   250→    if handler is None:
   251→        _cmd_cluster_list(args)
   252→        return
   253→    handler(args)
   254→
   255→
   256→__all__ = ["cmd_cluster_dispatch"]
   257→
```

```
    34→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))

   101→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   102→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
   103→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
   104→
   105→    if plan_action is None:
   106→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
   107→        return
   108→
   109→    if plan_action == "show":
   110→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
   111→        return

   115→

   117→        return
   118→
   119→    if plan_action == "reset":

   122→

   124→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.move_handlers import cmd_plan_move
   125→
   126→        cmd_plan_move(args)
   127→        return
   128→
   129→    if plan_action in (
   130→        "describe", "note", "focus",
   131→        "skip", "unskip", "reopen", "done",
   132→    ):
   133→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.override_handlers import (
   134→            cmd_plan_describe,
   135→            cmd_plan_done,
   136→            cmd_plan_focus,
   137→            cmd_plan_note,
   138→            cmd_plan_reopen,
   139→            cmd_plan_skip,
   140→            cmd_plan_unskip,
   141→        )

   143→        dispatch = {

   145→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   146→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   147→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   148→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   149→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   150→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   151→        }
   152→        dispatch[plan_action](args)

   154→
   155→    if plan_action == "cluster":

   157→

   159→        return
   160→
   161→    if plan_action == "synthesize":

   163→

   167→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
```

```
1→"""Pattern → finding-ID resolution for plan commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.resolution import match_findings
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     9→
    10→
    11→def resolve_ids_from_patterns(
    12→    state: StateModel,
    13→    patterns: list[str],
    14→    *,
    15→    plan: PlanModel | None = None,
    16→    status_filter: str = "open",
    17→) -> list[str]:
    18→    """Resolve one or more patterns to a deduplicated list of finding IDs.
    19→
    20→    When *plan* is provided, literal IDs that exist only in the plan
    21→    (e.g. ``subjective::*`` synthetic items) are included even if they
    22→    have no corresponding entry in ``state["findings"]``.
    23→    """
    24→    seen: set[str] = set()
    25→    result: list[str] = []
    26→
    27→    # Collect all plan IDs for literal-match fallback
    28→    plan_ids: set[str] = set()
    29→    if plan is not None:
    30→        plan_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    31→        plan_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    32→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    33→            plan_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    34→
    35→    for pattern in patterns:
    36→        matches = match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter)
    37→        if matches:
    38→            for finding in matches:
    39→                fid = finding["id"]
    40→                if fid not in seen:
    41→                    seen.add(fid)
    42→                    result.append(fid)
    43→        elif pattern in plan_ids and pattern not in seen:
    44→            # Literal plan ID (e.g. subjective::foo) not in state findings
    45→            seen.add(pattern)
    46→            result.append(pattern)
    47→    return result
    48→
    49→
    50→def print_no_match_hint(patterns: list[str]) -> None:
    51→    """Print a helpful message when no findings match the given patterns."""
    52→    print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    53→    tried = ", ".join(repr(p) for p in patterns[:5])
    54→    if len(patterns) > 5:
    55→        tried += f", ... ({len(patterns)} total)"
    56→    print(colorize(f"  Tried: {tried}", "dim"))
    57→    print(colorize("  Valid pattern formats:", "dim"))
    58→    print(colorize("    - Exact finding ID      detector::file/path::symbol::hash", "dim"))
    59→    print(colorize("    - Detector prefix        detector::                       ", "dim"))
    60→    print(colorize("    - Detector name          unused, security, review, ...    ", "dim"))
    61→    print(colorize("    - File path              src/foo.ts                       ", "dim"))
    62→    print(colorize("    - 8-char hash suffix     abc12345                         ", "dim"))
    63→    print(colorize("    - Glob                   *pattern*                        ", "dim"))
    64→
    65→
    66→__all__ = ["print_no_match_hint", "resolve_ids_from_patterns"]
    67→
```

```
1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   105→
   106→
   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   119→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   138→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   147→        return None
   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   149→
   150→
   151→def primary_command_for_finding(
   152→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   153→) -> str:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   156→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   157→        available_fixers = [
   158→            fixer
   159→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   160→            if supported_fixers is None or fixer in supported_fixers
   161→        ]
   162→        if available_fixers:
   163→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   164→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   165→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   166→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   167→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   168→    return f'desloppify plan done "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   169→
   170→
   171→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   172→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   173→    if not dim_scores:
   174→        return {}
   175→
   176→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   177→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   178→    for entry in entries:
   179→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   180→        if not name:
   181→            continue
   182→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   183→        [REDACTED](name)
   184→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   185→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   186→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   187→            if not key:
   188→                continue
   189→            aliases.add(key)
   190→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   191→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   192→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   193→        for alias in aliases:
   194→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   195→    return scores
   196→
   197→
   198→def build_subjective_items(
   199→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   200→) -> list[dict]:
   201→    """Create synthetic subjective work items (always tier 4)."""
   202→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   203→    if not dim_scores:
   204→        return []
   205→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   206→
   207→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   208→    if not subjective_entries:
   209→        return []
   210→    unassessed_dims = {
   211→        str(name).strip()
   212→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   213→            dim_scores
   214→        )
   215→    }
   216→
   217→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   218→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   219→    for finding in findings.values():
   220→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   221→            continue
   222→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   223→        if not dim_key:
   224→            continue
   225→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   226→
   227→    items: list[dict] = []
   228→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   229→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   230→        if not name:
   231→            continue
   232→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   233→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   234→            continue
   235→
   236→        [REDACTED](name)
   237→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   238→        cli_keys = [
   239→            str(key).strip().lower()
   240→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   241→            if str(key).strip()
   242→        ]
   243→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   244→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   245→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   246→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   247→            name in unassessed_dims
   248→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   249→        )
   250→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   251→        if is_unassessed:
   252→            primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   253→        elif is_stale:
   254→            if cli_keys:
   255→                primary_command = (
   256→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   257→                )
   258→            else:
   259→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   260→        else:
   261→            if open_review > 0:
   262→                primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   263→            elif cli_keys:
   264→                primary_command = (
   265→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   266→                )
   267→            else:
   268→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   269→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   270→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   271→        items.append(
   272→            {
   273→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   274→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   275→                "file": ".",
   276→                "tier": 4,
   277→                "effective_tier": 4,
   278→                "confidence": "medium",
   279→                "summary": summary,
   280→                "detail": {
   281→                    "dimension_name": name,
   282→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   283→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   284→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   285→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   286→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   287→                },
   288→                "status": "open",
   289→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   290→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   291→            }
   292→        )
   293→    return items
   294→
   295→
   296→def build_synthesis_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> dict | None:
   297→    """Build a synthetic T1 queue item for pending epic synthesis.
   298→
   299→    Returns None if synthesis is not needed (no synthesis::pending in queue).
   300→    """
   301→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   302→        SYNTHESIS_ID,
   303→        review_finding_snapshot_hash,
   304→    )
   305→
   306→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   307→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in order:
   308→        return None
   309→
   310→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   311→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   312→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   313→
   314→    # Count review findings
   315→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   316→    review_ids = [
   317→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   318→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   319→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   320→    ]
   321→    total = len(review_ids)
   322→    synthesized = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   323→    new_since_last = sum(1 for fid in review_ids if fid not in synthesized)
   324→    resolved_since_last = sum(1 for fid in synthesized if fid not in {r for r in review_ids})
   325→
   326→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import synthesis_clusters
   327→
   328→    epics = synthesis_clusters(plan)
   329→    pending_epics = sum(1 for e in epics.values() if e.get("status", "pending") == "pending")
   330→    in_progress_epics = sum(1 for e in epics.values() if e.get("status") == "in_progress")
   331→    completed_epics = sum(1 for e in epics.values() if e.get("status") == "completed")
   332→
   333→    trigger = "initial synthesis needed"
   334→    if last_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   335→        if new_since_last > resolved_since_last:
   336→            trigger = "new findings imported"
   337→        elif resolved_since_last > 0:
   338→            trigger = "findings resolved"
   339→        else:
   340→            trigger = "findings changed"
   341→
   342→    return {
   343→        "id": SYNTHESIS_ID,
   344→        "kind": "synthesis_needed",
   345→        "tier": 1,
   346→        "effective_tier": 1,
   347→        "confidence": "high",
   348→        "detector": "synthesis",
   349→        "file": ".",
   350→        "summary": f"Meta-plan update: {total} review findings need integration into epics",
   351→        "status": "open",
   352→        "primary_command": "desloppify plan synthesize",
   353→        "detail": {
   354→            "total_review_findings": total,
   355→            "new_since_last": new_since_last,
   356→            "resolved_since_last": resolved_since_last,
   357→            "trigger": trigger,
   358→            "epic_count": len(epics),
   359→            "pending_epics": pending_epics,
   360→            "in_progress_epics": in_progress_epics,
   361→            "completed_epics": completed_epics,
   362→        },
   363→    }
   364→
   365→
   366→__all__ = [
   367→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   368→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   369→    "build_subjective_items",
   370→    "build_synthesis_item",
   371→    "detail_dict",
   372→    "is_review_finding",
   373→    "is_subjective_finding",
   374→    "primary_command_for_finding",
   375→    "review_finding_weight",
   376→    "scope_matches",
   377→    "slugify",
   378→    "status_matches",
   379→    "subjective_strict_scores",
   380→    "supported_fixers_for_item",
   381→]
   382→
```

```
1→"""State resolution operations (match + resolve)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import copy
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "coerce_assessment_score",
     9→    "match_findings",
    10→    "resolve_findings",
    11→]
    12→
    13→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import _matches_pattern
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    16→    StateModel,
    17→    ensure_state_defaults,
    18→    utc_now,
    19→    validate_state_invariants,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
    22→
    23→
    24→def coerce_assessment_score(value: object) -> float | None:
    25→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float.
    26→
    27→    Returns ``None`` when the value cannot be interpreted as a numeric score
    28→    (e.g. bools, non-numeric strings, missing keys).
    29→    """
    30→    if is_numeric(value):
    31→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
    32→    if isinstance(value, dict):
    33→        raw = value.get("score")
    34→        if not is_numeric(raw):
    35→            return None
    36→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw))), 1)
    37→    return None
    38→
    39→
    40→def _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
    41→    state: StateModel,
    42→    *,
    43→    status: str,
    44→    resolved_findings: list[dict],
    45→    now: str,
    46→) -> None:
    47→    """Mark subjective assessments as stale when review findings are resolved.
    48→
    49→    The assessment score is preserved (not zeroed) — only a fresh review import
    50→    should change dimension scores.  The stale marker tells the UI to prompt
    51→    for a re-review.
    52→    """
    53→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    54→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    55→        return
    56→
    57→    touched_dimensions: set[str] = set()
    58→    for finding in resolved_findings:
    59→        if finding.get("detector") != "review":
    60→            continue
    61→        dimension = str(finding.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).strip()
    62→        if dimension:
    63→            touched_dimensions.add(dimension)
    64→
    65→    for dimension in sorted(touched_dimensions):
    66→        if dimension not in assessments:
    67→            continue
    68→
    69→        payload = assessments.get(dimension)
    70→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
    71→            payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    72→            payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_finding_{status}"
    73→            payload["stale_since"] = now
    74→        else:
    75→            assessments[dimension] = {
    76→                "score": coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0,
    77→                "needs_review_refresh": True,
    78→                "refresh_reason": f"review_finding_{status}",
    79→                "stale_since": now,
    80→            }
    81→
    82→
    83→def match_findings(
    84→    state: StateModel, pattern: str, status_filter: str = "open"
    85→) -> list[dict]:
    86→    """Return findings matching *pattern* with the given status."""
    87→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
    88→    return [
    89→        finding
    90→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
    91→        if not finding.get("suppressed")
    92→        if (status_filter == "all" or finding["status"] == status_filter)
    93→        and _matches_pattern(finding_id, finding, pattern)
    94→    ]
    95→
    96→
    97→def resolve_findings(
    98→    state: StateModel,
    99→    pattern: str,
   100→    status: str,
   101→    note: str | None = None,
   102→    attestation: str | None = None,
   103→) -> list[str]:
   104→    """Set finding status for matches and return affected finding IDs."""
   105→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   106→    now = utc_now()
   107→    resolved: list[str] = []
   108→    resolved_findings: list[dict] = []
   109→    status_filter = "all" if status == "open" else "open"
   110→    for finding in match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter):
   111→        previous_status = str(finding.get("status", "open")).strip() or "open"
   112→        if status == "open" and previous_status == "open":
   113→            continue
   114→
   115→        extra_updates: dict[str, object] = {}
   116→        if status == "wontfix":
   117→            snapshot_scan_count = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   118→            extra_updates["wontfix_scan_count"] = snapshot_scan_count
   119→            extra_updates["wontfix_snapshot"] = {
   120→                "captured_at": now,
   121→                "scan_count": snapshot_scan_count,
   122→                "tier": finding.get("tier"),
   123→                "confidence": finding.get("confidence"),
   124→                "detail": copy.deepcopy(finding.get("detail", {})),
   125→            }
   126→        if status == "open":
   127→            finding["reopen_count"] = int(finding.get("reopen_count", 0) or 0) + 1
   128→            finding.pop("wontfix_scan_count", None)
   129→            finding.pop("wontfix_snapshot", None)
   130→            previous_note = finding.get("note")
   131→            next_note = note if note is not None else previous_note
   132→            extra_updates["resolved_at"] = None
   133→            extra_updates["note"] = next_note
   134→            extra_updates["resolution_attestation"] = {
   135→                "kind": "manual_reopen",
   136→                "text": attestation or note,
   137→                "attested_at": now,
   138→                "scan_verified": False,
   139→                "previous_status": previous_status,
   140→            }
   141→
   142→        updates: dict[str, object] = {
   143→            "status": status,
   144→            "note": note,
   145→            "resolved_at": now,
   146→            "suppressed": False,
   147→            "suppressed_at": None,
   148→            "suppression_pattern": None,
   149→            "resolution_attestation": {
   150→                "kind": "manual",
   151→                "text": attestation,
   152→                "attested_at": now,
   153→                "scan_verified": False,
   154→            },
   155→        }
   156→        updates.update(extra_updates)
   157→        finding.update(updates)
   158→        resolved.append(finding["id"])
   159→        resolved_findings.append(finding)
   160→
   161→    _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
   162→        state,
   163→        status=status,
   164→        resolved_findings=resolved_findings,
   165→        now=now,
   166→    )
   167→
   168→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   169→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   170→    return resolved
   171→
```

1→<!-- desloppify-begin -->
     2→<!-- desloppify-skill-version: 3 -->
     3→---
     4→name: desloppify
     5→description: >
     6→  Codebase health scanner and technical debt tracker. Use when the user asks
     7→  about code quality, technical debt, dead code, large files, god classes,
     8→  duplicate functions, code smells, naming issues, import cycles, or coupling
     9→  problems. Also use when asked for a health score, what to fix next, or to
    10→  create a cleanup plan. Supports 28 languages.
    11→allowed-tools: Bash(desloppify *)
    12→---
    13→
    14→# Desloppify
    15→
    16→## 1. Your Job
    17→
    18→Improve code quality by maximising the **strict score** honestly.
    19→
    20→**The main thing you do is run `desloppify next`** — it tells you exactly what to fix and how. Fix it, resolve it, run `next` again. Keep going.
    21→
    22→**Read the output of every command** — it prints guidance, warnings, and next steps. Follow what it says rather than substituting your own analysis.
    23→
    24→## 2. The Workflow
    25→
    26→Two loops. The **outer loop** rescans periodically to measure progress.
    27→The **inner loop** is where you spend most of your time: fixing issues one by one.
    28→
    29→### Outer loop — scan and check
    30→
    31→```bash
    32→desloppify scan --path .       # analyse the codebase
    33→desloppify status              # check scores — are we at target?
    34→```
    35→If not at target, work the inner loop. Rescan periodically — especially after clearing a cluster or batch of related fixes. Issues cascade-resolve and new ones may surface.
    36→
    37→### Inner loop — fix issues
    38→
    39→Repeat until the queue is clear:
    40→
    41→```
    42→1. desloppify next              ← tells you exactly what to fix next
    43→2. Fix the issue in code
    44→3. Mark it done with `plan done` (next shows the exact command including required attestation)
    45→```
    46→
    47→Score may temporarily drop after fixes — cascade effects are normal, keep going.
    48→If `next` suggests an auto-fixer, run `desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run` to preview, then apply.
    49→
    50→### Planning phase — synthesis
    51→
    52→When `desloppify next` shows **"PLAN — do not proceed until complete"**, you are in
    53→the **planning phase**. This blocks all other work until complete.
    54→
    55→**Do this:**
    56→1. `desloppify plan synthesize` — see all findings that need organizing
    57→2. Group by root cause into clusters:
    58→   - `desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."`
    59→   - `desloppify plan cluster add <name> <finding-pattern> [...]`
    60→3. Prioritize: `desloppify plan cluster move <name> top`
    61→4. Focus: `desloppify plan focus <name>`
    62→5. **Complete:** `desloppify plan synthesize --complete --strategy "2-4 sentence plan summary"`
    63→   - Use `--strategy "same"` to keep an existing strategy unchanged
    64→   - The tool verifies clusters exist with findings before allowing completion
    65→
    66→**Do NOT** edit code, run `fix`, run `resolve`, or skip findings during this phase.
    67→Planning exists because review findings must be grouped by root cause into coherent
    68→work arcs before execution — fixing individual findings without this leads to
    69→contradictory changes and wasted effort.
    70→
    71→**To be strategic**, use `plan` to shape what `next` gives you:
    72→```bash
    73→desloppify plan                        # see the full ordered queue
    74→desloppify plan move <pat> top         # reorder — what unblocks the most?
    75→desloppify plan cluster create <name>  # group related issues to batch-fix
    76→desloppify plan focus <cluster>        # scope next to one cluster
    77→desloppify plan defer <pat>            # push low-value items aside
    78→desloppify plan skip <pat>             # hide from next
    79→desloppify plan done <pat>             # mark complete
    80→desloppify plan synthesize             # group review findings into strategic epics
    81→desloppify plan reopen <pat>           # reopen
    82→```
    83→
    84→### Subjective reviews
    85→
    86→The scan will prompt you when a subjective review is needed — just follow its instructions.
    87→If you need to trigger one manually:
    88→```bash
    89→desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import
    90→```
    91→
    92→### Other useful commands
    93→
    94→```bash
    95→desloppify next --count 5                         # top 5 priorities

```
    28→def path_scoped_findings(
    29→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    30→    scan_path: str | None,
    31→) -> dict[str, Finding]:
    32→    """Filter findings to those within the given scan path."""
    33→    return {
    34→        finding_id: finding
    35→        for finding_id, finding in findings.items()
    36→        if finding_in_scan_scope(str(finding.get("file", "")), scan_path)
    37→    }
    38→
    39→
    40→def finding_in_scan_scope(file_path: str, scan_path: str | None) -> bool:
    41→    """Return True when a file path belongs to the active scan scope."""
    42→    if not scan_path or scan_path == ".":
    43→        return True
    44→    prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
    45→    return (
    46→        file_path.startswith(prefix)
    47→        or file_path == scan_path
    48→        or file_path == "."
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def open_scope_breakdown(
    53→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    54→    scan_path: str | None,
    55→    *,
    56→    detector: str | None = None,
    57→) -> dict[str, int]:
    58→    """Return open-finding counts split by in-scope vs out-of-scope carryover."""
    59→    in_scope = 0
    60→    out_of_scope = 0
    61→
    62→    for finding in findings.values():
    63→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    64→            continue
    65→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    66→            continue
    67→        if detector is not None and finding.get("detector") != detector:
    68→            continue
    69→        file_path = str(finding.get("file", ""))
    70→        if finding_in_scan_scope(file_path, scan_path):
    71→            in_scope += 1
    72→        else:
    73→            out_of_scope += 1
    74→
    75→    return {
    76→        "in_scope": in_scope,
    77→        "out_of_scope": out_of_scope,
    78→        "global": in_scope + out_of_scope,
    79→    }
    80→
    81→
    82→def is_ignored(finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]) -> bool:
    83→    """Check if a finding matches any ignore pattern (glob, ID prefix, or file path)."""
    84→    return matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, file, ignore_patterns) is not None
    85→
    86→
    87→def matched_ignore_pattern(
    88→    finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]
    89→) -> str | None:
    90→    """Return the ignore pattern that matched, if any."""
    91→    for pattern in ignore_patterns:
    92→        if "*" in pattern:
    93→            target = finding_id if "::" in pattern else file
    94→            if fnmatch.fnmatch(target, pattern):
    95→                return pattern
    96→            continue
    97→
    98→        if "::" in pattern:
    99→            if finding_id.startswith(pattern):
   100→                return pattern
   101→            continue
   102→
   103→        if file == pattern or file == rel(pattern):
   104→            return pattern
   105→
   106→    return None
   107→
   108→
   109→def remove_ignored_findings(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   110→    """Suppress findings matching an ignore pattern. Returns count affected."""
   111→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   112→    matched_ids = [
   113→        finding_id
   114→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
   115→        if is_ignored(finding_id, finding["file"], [pattern])
   116→    ]
   117→    now = utc_now()
   118→    for finding_id in matched_ids:
   119→        finding = state["findings"][finding_id]
   120→        finding["suppressed"] = True
   121→        finding["suppressed_at"] = now
   122→        finding["suppression_pattern"] = pattern
   123→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with engine._state.scoring
   124→    from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
   125→
   126→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   127→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   128→    return len(matched_ids)
   129→
   130→
   131→def add_ignore(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   132→    """Add an ignore pattern and remove existing matching findings."""
   133→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   134→    config = state.setdefault("config", {})
   135→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   136→    if pattern not in ignores:
   137→        ignores.append(pattern)
   138→    return remove_ignored_findings(state, pattern)
   139→
   140→
   141→def make_finding(
   142→    detector: str,
   143→    file: str,
   144→    name: str,
   145→    *,
   146→    tier: int,
   147→    confidence: str,
   148→    summary: str,
   149→    detail: dict | None = None,
   150→) -> Finding:
   151→    """Create a normalized finding dict with a stable ID."""
   152→    rfile = rel(file)
   153→    finding_id = f"{detector}::{rfile}::{name}" if name else f"{detector}::{rfile}"
   154→    now = utc_now()
   155→    return {
   156→        "id": finding_id,
   157→        "detector": detector,
   158→        "file": rfile,
   159→        "tier": tier,
   160→        "confidence": confidence,
   161→        "summary": summary,
   162→        "detail": detail or {},
   163→        "status": "open",
   164→        "note": None,
   165→        "first_seen": now,
   166→        "last_seen": now,
   167→        "resolved_at": None,
   168→        "reopen_count": 0,
   169→    }
   170→
   171→
   172→def _matches_pattern(finding_id: str, finding: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
   173→    """Check if a finding matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, path, or hash suffix."""
   174→    if finding_id == pattern:
   175→        return True
   176→    if "*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern):
   177→        return True
   178→    if "::" in pattern and finding_id.startswith(pattern):
   179→        return True
   180→    if finding.get("detector") == pattern:
   181→        return True
   182→    file = finding.get("file", "")
   183→    if file == pattern or file.startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/"):
   184→        return True
   185→    # 8-char hash suffix: match against the last :: segment of the finding ID
   186→    if len(pattern) >= 8 and "::" not in pattern and "*" not in pattern and "/" not in pattern:
   187→        last_seg = finding_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] if "::" in finding_id else ""
   188→        if last_seg and last_seg.startswith(pattern[:8]):
   189→            return True
   190→    return False
   191→
```

```
    14→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    15→class CommandRuntime:
    16→    """Explicit runtime dependencies shared by command handlers."""
    17→
    18→    config: dict[str, Any]
    19→    state: dict[str, Any]
    20→    state_path: Path | None
    21→
    22→
    23→def command_runtime(args) -> CommandRuntime:
    24→    """Return runtime context from explicit args.runtime or construct one."""
    25→    runtime = getattr(args, "runtime", None)
    26→    if isinstance(runtime, CommandRuntime):
    27→        return runtime
    28→
    29→    config = load_config()
    30→    state_file = state_path(args)
    31→    if isinstance(state_file, str):
    32→        state_file = Path(state_file)
    33→
    34→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    35→
    36→    return CommandRuntime(config=config, state=state, state_path=state_file)
    37→
    38→
    39→__all__ = ["CommandRuntime", "command_runtime"]
```

   101→```
   102→
   103→## 3. Reference
   104→
   105→### How scoring works
   106→
   107→Overall score = **40% mechanical** + **60% subjective**.
   108→
   109→- **Mechanical (40%)**: auto-detected issues — duplication, dead code, smells, unused imports, security. Fixed by changing code and rescanning.
   110→- **Subjective (60%)**: design quality review — naming, error handling, abstractions, clarity. Starts at **0%** until reviewed. The scan will prompt you when a review is needed.
   111→- **Strict score** is the north star: wontfix items count as open. The gap between overall and strict is your wontfix debt.
   112→- **Score types**: overall (lenient), strict (wontfix counts), objective (mechanical only), verified (confirmed fixes only).
   113→
   114→### Subjective reviews in detail
   115→
   116→- **Preferred**: `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` — does everything in one command.
   117→- **Manual path**: `desloppify review --prepare` → review per dimension → `desloppify review --import file.json`.
   118→- Import first, fix after — import creates tracked state entries for correlation.
   119→- Integrity: reviewers score from evidence only. Scores hitting exact targets trigger auto-reset.
   120→- Even moderate scores (60-80) dramatically improve overall health.
   121→- Stale dimensions auto-surface in `next` — just follow the queue.
   122→
   123→### Key concepts
   124→
   125→- **Tiers**: T1 auto-fix → T2 quick manual → T3 judgment call → T4 major refactor.
   126→- **Auto-clusters**: related findings are auto-grouped in `next`. Drill in with `next --cluster <name>`.
   127→- **Zones**: production/script (scored), test/config/generated/vendor (not scored). Fix with `zone set`.
   128→- **Epics**: `plan synthesize` groups review findings by root cause into strategic refactoring arcs. Run it when `next` shows "Meta-plan update needed".
   129→- **Wontfix cost**: widens the lenient↔strict gap. Challenge past decisions when the gap grows.
   130→- Score can temporarily drop after fixes (cascade effects are normal).
   131→
   132→## 4. Escalate Tool Issues Upstream
   133→
   134→When desloppify itself appears wrong or inconsistent:
   135→
   136→1. Capture a minimal repro (`command`, `path`, `expected`, `actual`).
   137→2. Open a GitHub issue in `peteromallet/desloppify`.
   138→3. If you can fix it safely, open a PR linked to that issue.
   139→4. If unsure whether it is tool bug vs user workflow, issue first, PR second.
   140→
   141→## Prerequisite
   142→
   143→`command -v desloppify >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo "desloppify: installed" || echo "NOT INSTALLED — run: pip install --upgrade git+https://github.com/peteromallet/desloppify.git"`
   144→
   145→<!-- desloppify-end -->
   146→

Good -- `synthesis_stages` does not exist anywhere yet; this is entirely new. Now I have complete understanding of the codebase. Let me produce the full implementation plan.

---

## Implementation Plan: Multi-Stage Synthesis Workflow

### Overview

This plan replaces the current flat `plan synthesize` / `plan synthesize --complete` workflow with a 3-stage pipeline: OBSERVE, ORGANIZE, COMMIT. Each stage has mechanical validation, writes structured data to the plan, and must be completed in order.

The key design principle: stages are sequential gates, not bureaucratic hoops. Each stage's validation enforces a specific anti-pattern guard (skimming, shallow grouping, empty strategy), and the data written at each stage is functional -- it surfaces during execution via `next` cards and cluster displays.

---

### File 1: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py`

**What changes**: Add `SynthesisStageReport` TypedDict, add `synthesis_stages` field to `EpicSynthesisMeta`, update `__all__`.

**New type**:
```python
SYNTHESIS_STAGES = ("observe", "organize", "commit")

class SynthesisStageReport(TypedDict, total=False):
    stage: Required[str]           # "observe" | "organize" | "commit"
    report: str                    # The agent's report text
    completed_at: str              # ISO timestamp
    finding_refs: list[str]        # Finding IDs cited in the report
    finding_snapshot_hash: str     # Hash at time of stage completion
```

**Modified type** -- `EpicSynthesisMeta` gets one new optional field:
```python

    # ... existing fields unchanged ...
    synthesis_stages: dict[str, SynthesisStageReport]  # NEW
```

**`ensure_plan_defaults`** -- add normalization for the new field:

```python
if not isinstance(meta.get("synthesis_stages"), dict):
```

**Complexity**: Low. This is purely additive -- a new TypedDict and one dict field. No existing data is altered. Old plans without `synthesis_stages` get an empty dict via `ensure_plan_defaults`.

### File 2: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups_admin.py`

**What changes**: Replace current `p_synthesize` arguments with stage-aware arguments. The `--complete` and `--strategy` flags become deprecated (mapped internally to `--stage commit`).

**Modified section** -- `_add_plan_parser`, the `# plan synthesize` block:

```python
# plan synthesize [--stage STAGE] [--report TEXT] [--report-file FILE] [--dry-run] [--reset-stages]
p_synthesize = plan_sub.add_parser(
    "synthesize",
    help="Multi-stage planning workflow for review findings",
    epilog="""\
stages:
  observe    Read all findings, write per-finding notes, identify tensions
  organize   Create clusters, assign findings, explain grouping rationale
  commit     Write strategy and per-cluster execution plan; completes synthesis

examples:
  desloppify plan synthesize                                # show findings + stage status
  desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "..." # submit observation report
  desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report-file obs.md
  desloppify plan synthesize --stage organize --report "..."
  desloppify plan synthesize --stage commit --report "..."  # completes synthesis
  desloppify plan synthesize --reset-stages                 # voluntary stage reset""",
    formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
)
p_synthesize.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Show synthesis prompt without running")
p_synthesize.add_argument("--timeout", type=int, default=600, help="LLM call timeout in seconds")
p_synthesize.add_argument(
    "--stage",
    choices=["observe", "organize", "commit"],
    default=None,
    help="Stage to submit a report for",
)
p_synthesize.add_argument(
    "--report",
    type=str,
    default=None,
    help="Report text for the specified stage",
)
p_synthesize.add_argument(
    "--report-file",
    type=str,
    default=None,
    metavar="FILE",
    help="Path to a file containing the report (for longer reports)",
)
p_synthesize.add_argument(
    "--reset-stages",
    action="store_true",
    help="Voluntarily reset all completed stages to re-do planning",
)
# Backwards compat: --complete is mapped to --stage commit in the handler
p_synthesize.add_argument(
    "--complete", action="store_true",
    help=argparse.SUPPRESS,  # hidden, backwards compat
)
p_synthesize.add_argument(
    "--strategy", type=str, default=None,
    help=argparse.SUPPRESS,  # hidden, backwards compat
)
```

**Complexity**: Low. Purely parser definition changes. The `--complete`/`--strategy` flags are kept as hidden (`SUPPRESS`) for backwards compatibility.

### File 3: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesize_handlers.py`

**What changes**: Major rewrite. The file grows from ~213 lines to roughly ~450 lines. Split into clear sections: display, validation, stage submission, backwards-compat bridge.

#### Constants and helpers at module top:

```python
from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import SYNTHESIS_STAGES, SynthesisStageReport

_STAGE_ORDER = {name: idx for idx, name in enumerate(SYNTHESIS_STAGES)}
# "observe" -> 0, "organize" -> 1, "commit" -> 2

# Minimum total characters for each stage's report
_MIN_REPORT_LENGTH = {
    "observe": 300,
    "organize": 200,
    "commit": 200,
}

# Minimum fraction of finding IDs that must be cited in the observe report
_MIN_OBSERVE_COVERAGE = 0.80

# For very small finding counts (<=3), use abbreviated requirements
_ABBREVIATED_THRESHOLD = 3
_ABBREVIATED_MIN_LENGTH = {"observe": 100, "organize": 80, "commit": 80}
_ABBREVIATED_OBSERVE_COVERAGE = 0.5  # at least half for tiny sets
```

#### Finding ID extraction:

```python
def _extract_finding_refs(report: str, known_ids: set[str]) -> list[str]:
    """Extract finding IDs mentioned in report text.
    
    Matches against:
    1. Full finding IDs (detector::file::name::hash)
    2. 8+ char hash suffixes (the last :: segment of IDs)
    3. Detector prefix matches (e.g. "review::" matches all review findings)
    
    Returns deduplicated list of full finding IDs that were referenced.
    """
    referenced: list[str] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    
    # Build lookup: hash_suffix -> full_id
    suffix_map: dict[str, str] = {}
    for fid in known_ids:
        if "::" in fid:
            last_seg = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
            if len(last_seg) >= 8:
                suffix_map[last_seg[:8]] = fid
    
    # Check full IDs first (longest match wins)
    for fid in sorted(known_ids, key=len, reverse=True):
        if fid in report and fid not in seen:
            seen.add(fid)
            referenced.append(fid)
    
    # Check 8-char hash suffixes
    import re
    for match in re.finditer(r'\b([0-9a-f]{8,})\b', report):
        token = match.group(1)[:8]
        full_id = suffix_map.get(token)
        if full_id and full_id not in seen:
            seen.add(full_id)
            referenced.append(full_id)
    
    return referenced
```

#### Validation functions (one per stage):

```python
def _validate_observe(
    report: str,
    finding_refs: list[str],
    total_findings: int,
    abbreviated: bool,
) -> list[str]:
    """Validate observe stage report. Returns list of error messages (empty = valid)."""
    errors: list[str] = []
    
    min_len = (_ABBREVIATED_MIN_LENGTH if abbreviated else _MIN_REPORT_LENGTH)["observe"]
    if len(report.strip()) < min_len:
        errors.append(
            f"Report too short ({len(report.strip())} chars). "
            f"Minimum: {min_len} chars."
        )
    
    if total_findings > 0:
        coverage = len(finding_refs) / total_findings
        min_coverage = _ABBREVIATED_OBSERVE_COVERAGE if abbreviated else _MIN_OBSERVE_COVERAGE
        if coverage < min_coverage:
            errors.append(
                f"Only {len(finding_refs)}/{total_findings} findings cited "
                f"({coverage:.0%}). Minimum: {min_coverage:.0%}. "
                f"Reference findings by their full ID or 8-char hash suffix."
            )
    
    return errors


def _validate_organize(
    report: str,
    plan: dict,
    queue_ids: list[str],
    abbreviated: bool,
) -> list[str]:
    """Validate organize stage report. Returns list of error messages."""
    errors: list[str] = []
    
    min_len = (_ABBREVIATED_MIN_LENGTH if abbreviated else _MIN_REPORT_LENGTH)["organize"]
    if len(report.strip()) < min_len:
        errors.append(
            f"Report too short ({len(report.strip())} chars). "
            f"Minimum: {min_len} chars."
        )
    
    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    non_empty_clusters = {
        name: c for name, c in clusters.items()
        if c.get("finding_ids")
    }
    
    if not non_empty_clusters:
        errors.append(
            "No clusters with findings exist. "
            "Create clusters first: `desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description \"...\"`"
        )
    
    # Check coverage: every finding should be clustered or explicitly skipped
    all_clustered: set[str] = set()
    for c in clusters.values():
        all_clustered.update(c.get("finding_ids", []))
    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    
    uncovered = [
        fid for fid in queue_ids
        if fid != SYNTHESIS_ID
        and fid not in all_clustered
        and fid not in skipped_ids
        and not fid.startswith("subjective::")
    ]
    
    # Warning, not hard error -- uncovered findings remain as individual queue items
    # But we require at least SOME organization happened
    if uncovered and not non_empty_clusters:
        errors.append(
            f"{len(uncovered)} findings are neither clustered nor skipped."
        )
    
    return errors


def _validate_commit(
    report: str,
    plan: dict,
    abbreviated: bool,
) -> list[str]:
    """Validate commit stage report. Returns list of error messages."""
    errors: list[str] = []
    
    min_len = (_ABBREVIATED_MIN_LENGTH if abbreviated else _MIN_REPORT_LENGTH)["commit"]
    if len(report.strip()) < min_len:
        errors.append(
            f"Report too short ({len(report.strip())} chars). "
            f"Minimum: {min_len} chars."
        )
    
    # Every non-empty cluster name should be mentioned in the strategy
    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    non_empty = {name for name, c in clusters.items() if c.get("finding_ids")}
    report_lower = report.lower()
    unmentioned = [
        name for name in sorted(non_empty)
        if name.lower() not in report_lower
    ]
    if unmentioned:
        errors.append(
            f"Strategy does not mention cluster(s): {', '.join(unmentioned)}. "
            f"Each cluster should be addressed in the execution plan."
        )
    
    return errors
```

#### Stage submission handler:

```python
def _current_stage(plan: dict) -> str:
    """Return the next stage that needs completion."""
    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
    for stage_name in SYNTHESIS_STAGES:
        if stage_name not in stages or not stages[stage_name].get("completed_at"):
            return stage_name
    return "done"  # all stages complete


def _read_report(args: argparse.Namespace) -> str | None:
    """Read report from --report or --report-file."""
    report = getattr(args, "report", None)
    report_file = getattr(args, "report_file", None)
    
    if report and report_file:
        print(colorize("  Cannot use both --report and --report-file.", "red"))
        return None
    
    if report_file:
        import pathlib
        path = pathlib.Path(report_file)
        if not path.exists():
            print(colorize(f"  File not found: {report_file}", "red"))
            return None
        report = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    
    return report


def _apply_observe_side_effects(
    plan: dict,
    report: str,
    finding_refs: list[str],
    open_findings: dict,
) -> None:
    """Write per-finding notes extracted from observe report into overrides."""
    # For each finding referenced, store a note if the report contains 
    # meaningful text near the finding reference.
    # Simple approach: the whole observe report is the agent's analysis.
    # We don't try to slice it per-finding -- the report itself is stored
    # in synthesis_stages and the per-finding notes are set by the agent
    # using `plan note` during the observe stage.
    # 
    # No automatic slicing -- that would be fragile. The observe stage
    # encourages the agent to use `plan note` for per-finding observations.
    pass


def _apply_commit_completion(plan: dict, report: str, state: dict) -> None:
    """Remove synthesis::pending from queue and finalize meta."""
    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import review_finding_snapshot_hash
    
    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    if SYNTHESIS_ID in order:
        order.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
    
    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    meta["finding_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
    meta["strategy_summary"] = report  # The commit report IS the strategy
    meta["trigger"] = "staged_synthesis"


def _cmd_submit_stage(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Submit a report for a specific synthesis stage."""
    stage: str = getattr(args, "stage")
    report = _read_report(args)
    if report is None:
        return
    if not report or not report.strip():
        print(colorize(f"  --report is required for --stage {stage}.", "red"))
        return
    
    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    state = runtime.state
    if not require_completed_scan(state):
        return
    
    plan = load_plan()
    
    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
        print(colorize("  synthesis::pending is not in the queue — nothing to synthesize.", "yellow"))
        return
    
    # Check stage ordering
    current = _current_stage(plan)
    if current == "done":
        print(colorize("  All stages are already complete.", "yellow"))
        print(colorize("  To re-do: `desloppify plan synthesize --reset-stages`", "dim"))
        return
    
    if _STAGE_ORDER.get(stage, 0) < _STAGE_ORDER.get(current, 0):
        print(colorize(f"  Stage '{stage}' is already complete.", "yellow"))
        return
    
    if _STAGE_ORDER.get(stage, 0) > _STAGE_ORDER.get(current, 0):
        print(colorize(
            f"  Cannot submit '{stage}' — complete '{current}' first.",
            "red",
        ))
        return
    
    # Collect open review findings for validation
    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, state)
    known_ids = set(si.open_findings.keys())
    total_findings = len(known_ids)
    abbreviated = total_findings <= _ABBREVIATED_THRESHOLD
    
    # Extract finding references from report
    finding_refs = _extract_finding_refs(report, known_ids)
    
    # Stage-specific validation
    queue_ids = [fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", []) if fid != SYNTHESIS_ID]
    
    if stage == "observe":
        errors = _validate_observe(report, finding_refs, total_findings, abbreviated)
    elif stage == "organize":
        errors = _validate_organize(report, plan, queue_ids, abbreviated)
    elif stage == "commit":
        errors = _validate_commit(report, plan, abbreviated)
    else:
        errors = [f"Unknown stage: {stage}"]
    
    if errors:
        print(colorize(f"  Stage '{stage}' validation failed:", "red"))
        for err in errors:
            print(colorize(f"    - {err}", "yellow"))
        return
    
    # Record the stage
    from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import review_finding_snapshot_hash
    
    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    stages_dict = meta.setdefault("synthesis_stages", {})
    stages_dict[stage] = SynthesisStageReport(
        stage=stage,
        report=report,
        completed_at=utc_now(),
        finding_refs=finding_refs,
        finding_snapshot_hash=review_finding_snapshot_hash(state),
    )
    
    # Stage-specific side effects
    if stage == "observe":
        _apply_observe_side_effects(plan, report, finding_refs, si.open_findings)
    elif stage == "commit":
        _apply_commit_completion(plan, report, state)
    
    save_plan(plan)
    
    # Success output
    if stage == "commit":
        organized, total, clusters = _synthesis_coverage(plan)
        cluster_count = len([c for c in clusters.values() if c.get("finding_ids")])
        print(colorize(
            f"  Synthesis complete: {organized}/{total} findings in {cluster_count} cluster(s).",
            "green",
        ))
        print(colorize(f"  Strategy recorded ({len(report)} chars).", "cyan"))
        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify next` to start implementation.", "green"))
    else:
        next_stage_idx = _STAGE_ORDER[stage] + 1
        next_stage = SYNTHESIS_STAGES[next_stage_idx]
        print(colorize(f"  Stage '{stage}' complete.", "green"))
        print(colorize(
            f"  Next: `desloppify plan synthesize --stage {next_stage} --report \"...\"`",
            "cyan",
        ))
        _print_stage_progress(plan)
```

#### Enriched display (the default `plan synthesize` with no flags):

```python
def _print_stage_progress(plan: dict) -> None:
    """Print the current stage progress bar."""
    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
    current = _current_stage(plan)
    
    parts: list[str] = []
    for stage_name in SYNTHESIS_STAGES:
        if stage_name in stages and stages[stage_name].get("completed_at"):
            parts.append(f"[x] {stage_name}")
        elif stage_name == current:
            parts.append(f"[>] {stage_name}")
        else:
            parts.append(f"[ ] {stage_name}")
    
    print(colorize(f"\n  Stages: {' → '.join(parts)}", "cyan"))


def _render_findings_by_file(open_findings: dict) -> None:
    """Group findings by file and render with dimension/tension annotations."""
    from collections import defaultdict
    
    by_file: dict[str, list[tuple[str, dict]]] = defaultdict(list)
    for fid, f in sorted(open_findings.items()):
        file_path = f.get("file", ".")
        by_file[file_path].append((fid, f))
    
    # Detect cross-file tensions: findings in the same dimension that
    # suggest different directions
    dimension_directions: dict[str, list[tuple[str, str]]] = defaultdict(list)
    for fid, f in open_findings.items():
        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
        dim = detail.get("dimension", "")
        suggestion = detail.get("suggestion", "")
        if dim and suggestion:
            dimension_directions[dim].append((fid, suggestion))
    
    tensions: list[tuple[str, list[tuple[str, str]]]] = [
        (dim, items) for dim, items in dimension_directions.items()
        if len(items) > 1
    ]
    
    # Render by file
    for file_path in sorted(by_file.keys()):
        items = by_file[file_path]
        print(colorize(f"\n  {file_path} ({len(items)} finding{'s' if len(items) != 1 else ''}):", "bold"))
        for fid, f in items:
            detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
            dim = detail.get("dimension", "")
            confidence = f.get("confidence", "medium")
            summary = f.get("summary", "")
            dim_tag = f"[{dim}]" if dim else ""
            print(f"    {dim_tag} [{confidence}] {summary}")
            print(colorize(f"      {fid}", "dim"))
    
    # Render tensions
    if tensions:
        print(colorize("\n  Potential tensions:", "yellow"))
        for dim, items in tensions:
            print(colorize(f"    {dim}: {len(items)} findings may pull in different directions", "yellow"))
            for fid, suggestion in items[:3]:
                short_id = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1][:8] if "::" in fid else fid[:12]
                print(colorize(f"      {short_id}: {suggestion[:80]}", "dim"))


def cmd_plan_synthesize(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Multi-stage synthesis workflow for review findings."""
    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    state = runtime.state
    if not require_completed_scan(state):
        return
    
    # --- Backwards compat: --complete maps to --stage commit ---
    if getattr(args, "complete", False):
        strategy = getattr(args, "strategy", None)
        if strategy:
            # Map old interface to new
            args.stage = "commit"
            args.report = strategy
            args.report_file = None
        else:
            # Show deprecation message pointing to new interface
            print(colorize("  --complete is deprecated. Use --stage commit --report \"...\"", "yellow"))
            return
    
    # --- Reset stages ---
    if getattr(args, "reset_stages", False):
        plan = load_plan()
        meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
        meta["synthesis_stages"] = {}
        save_plan(plan)
        print(colorize("  All synthesis stages reset.", "green"))
        return
    
    # --- Stage submission ---
    stage = getattr(args, "stage", None)
    if stage is not None:
        _cmd_submit_stage(args)
        return
    
    # --- Dry run (existing behavior) ---
    dry_run = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
    plan = load_plan()
    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, state)
    
    if dry_run:
        prompt = build_synthesis_prompt(si)
        print(colorize("  Epic synthesis — dry run", "bold"))
        print(colorize("  " + "-" * 60, "dim"))
        print(f"  Open review findings: {len(si.open_findings)}")
        print(f"  Existing epics: {len(si.existing_epics)}")
        print(prompt)
        return
    
    # --- Default: show findings + stage status ---
    total = len(si.open_findings)
    abbreviated = total <= _ABBREVIATED_THRESHOLD
    
    print(colorize("  Epic synthesis — multi-stage planning", "bold"))
    print(colorize("  " + "-" * 60, "dim"))
    
    # Stage progress
    _print_stage_progress(plan)
    current = _current_stage(plan)
    
    print(f"\n  Open review findings: {total}")
    if abbreviated:
        print(colorize("  (abbreviated requirements — 3 or fewer findings)", "dim"))
    
    # Show the enriched finding display
    _render_findings_by_file(si.open_findings)
    
    # Current progress
    _print_progress(plan, si.open_findings)
    
    # Stage-specific instructions
    print(colorize(f"\n  Current stage: {current}", "yellow"))
    
    if current == "observe":
        print("  Read all findings above. Then submit your observations:")
        coverage_pct = int((_ABBREVIATED_OBSERVE_COVERAGE if abbreviated else _MIN_OBSERVE_COVERAGE) * 100)
        min_len = (_ABBREVIATED_MIN_LENGTH if abbreviated else _MIN_REPORT_LENGTH)["observe"]
        print(f"    - Reference >= {coverage_pct}% of findings by ID or hash suffix")
        print(f"    - Minimum {min_len} characters")
        print(f"    - Note tensions, redundancies, and root causes")
        print()
        print(colorize(
            '  Submit: `desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "..."`',
            "cyan",
        ))
        print(colorize(
            "  (or: `--report-file observations.md` for longer reports)",
            "dim",
        ))
    elif current == "organize":
        print("  Group findings into clusters and assign all findings:")
        print("    1. `desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description \"...\"`")
        print("    2. `desloppify plan cluster add <name> <pattern> [...]`")
        print("    3. `desloppify plan cluster move <name> top`")
        print("    4. `desloppify plan focus <name>`")
        print()
        print("  Then submit your organization rationale:")
        min_len = (_ABBREVIATED_MIN_LENGTH if abbreviated else _MIN_REPORT_LENGTH)["organize"]
        print(f"    - At least 1 cluster with findings must exist")
        print(f"    - Minimum {min_len} characters explaining grouping logic")
        print()
        print(colorize(
            '  Submit: `desloppify plan synthesize --stage organize --report "..."`',
            "cyan",
        ))
    elif current == "commit":
        print("  Write the execution strategy. Must mention every cluster name:")
        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
        non_empty = [name for name, c in clusters.items() if c.get("finding_ids")]
        if non_empty:
            for name in non_empty:
                print(f"    - {name}")
        min_len = (_ABBREVIATED_MIN_LENGTH if abbreviated else _MIN_REPORT_LENGTH)["commit"]
        print(f"    - Minimum {min_len} characters")
        print()
        print(colorize(
            '  Submit: `desloppify plan synthesize --stage commit --report "..."`',
            "cyan",
        ))
    elif current == "done":
        print(colorize("  All stages complete! Run `desloppify next` to start.", "green"))
```

**Complexity**: High but contained. This is the bulk of the implementation. The file is self-contained -- all the logic lives here, calling into existing plan operations and schema types.
