### File 4: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py`

**What changes**: Modify `sync_synthesis_needed` to reset synthesis stages when the finding landscape changes. Add dampening to prevent reset loops.

**Modified function** -- `sync_synthesis_needed`:

```python
def sync_synthesis_needed(
    plan: PlanModel,
    state: StateModel,
) -> SynthesisSyncResult:
    """Inject/prune synthesis::pending based on review finding drift.
    
    When findings change and synthesis stages exist, stages are reset
    with a dampening mechanism: only reset if the hash actually differs
    from the hash recorded at the most recently completed stage.
    """
    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    result = SynthesisSyncResult()
    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
    has_synthesis = SYNTHESIS_ID in order

    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
        # Need synthesis — inject if not present
        if not has_synthesis:
            order.insert(0, SYNTHESIS_ID)
            result.injected = True
        
        # Invalidate completed stages if the landscape changed
        # Dampening: compare against the most recent stage's snapshot hash,
        # not the overall meta hash, to avoid resetting when stages were
        # completed against the current landscape
        stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
        if stages:
            # Find the most recently completed stage's hash
            latest_stage_hash = ""
            for stage_name in reversed(SYNTHESIS_STAGES):
                stage_data = stages.get(stage_name, {})
                if stage_data.get("completed_at"):
                    latest_stage_hash = stage_data.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
                    break
            
            if latest_stage_hash and latest_stage_hash != current_hash:
                # Landscape genuinely changed since stages were completed
                meta["synthesis_stages"] = {}
                # result could track this: result.stages_reset = True
    else:
        # Synthesis up to date or no review findings — prune if present
        if has_synthesis:
            order.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
            result.pruned = True

    return result
```

This needs `SYNTHESIS_STAGES` imported from schema:
```python
from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults, SYNTHESIS_STAGES
```

**Edge case -- dampening**: The key insight is comparing against the *stage's recorded hash*, not the meta-level hash. If an agent completes the observe stage with hash X, then a scan resolves one finding (hash changes to Y), the stages reset. But if the agent then re-does observe with hash Y and submits organize with hash Y, a second scan that doesn't change findings (hash still Y) won't reset. This prevents infinite reset loops.

**Complexity**: Low-medium. The dampening logic is ~15 lines added to an existing function.

```python
def synthesis_phase_banner(plan: dict) -> str | None:
    """Return a warning banner if synthesis::pending is blocking, else None."""
    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
        return None
    
    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
    
    completed = [s for s in SYNTHESIS_STAGES if stages.get(s, {}).get("completed_at")]
    current_idx = len(completed)
    
    if current_idx >= len(SYNTHESIS_STAGES):
        # All stages done but synthesis::pending still in queue (shouldn't happen)
        return None
    
    current_stage = SYNTHESIS_STAGES[current_idx]
    progress = f"Stage {current_idx}/{len(SYNTHESIS_STAGES)}: {current_stage}"
    
    return (
        f"PLANNING PHASE — synthesis::pending is blocking the queue.\n"
        f"  {progress}\n"
        f"  Run `desloppify plan synthesize` to see status.\n"
        f"  Submit: `desloppify plan synthesize --stage {current_stage} --report \"...\"`\n"
        f"  Do NOT fix code, resolve findings, or skip items until planning is done."
    )
```

**Complexity**: Low.

def _render_synthesis_item(item: dict) -> None:
    """Render the synthesis::pending card with stage progress."""
    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
    total = detail.get("total_review_findings", 0)
    new = detail.get("new_since_last", 0)
    resolved = detail.get("resolved_since_last", 0)
    trigger = detail.get("trigger", "unknown")
    
    # NEW: stage progress from detail
    stage_progress = detail.get("stage_progress", {})
    completed_stages = stage_progress.get("completed", [])
    current_stage = stage_progress.get("current", "observe")
    total_stages = stage_progress.get("total", 3)

    print(colorize("  PLAN — do not proceed until complete", "bold"))
    print(colorize("  " + "-" * 60, "dim"))
    print(f"  {total} review findings need integration into the meta-plan.")
    print(f"  Trigger: {trigger}")
    
    # Stage progress bar
    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import SYNTHESIS_STAGES
    parts: list[str] = []
    for stage_name in SYNTHESIS_STAGES:
        if stage_name in completed_stages:
            parts.append(f"[x] {stage_name}")
        elif stage_name == current_stage:
            parts.append(f"[>] {stage_name}")
        else:
            parts.append(f"[ ] {stage_name}")
    print(colorize(f"\n  Progress: {' -> '.join(parts)}", "cyan"))
    print(f"  ({len(completed_stages)}/{total_stages} stages complete)")
    
    # ... rest of existing output (new/resolved counts, etc.) ...
    print()
    if new:
        print(f"  New findings since last synthesis: {new}")
    if resolved:
        print(f"  Resolved since last synthesis: {resolved}")
    print()
    print(colorize("  This is a planning step. Do NOT start fixing code until this is done.", "yellow"))
    print()
    print(f"  Current stage: {current_stage}")
    print(f"    `desloppify plan synthesize`  -- see findings and stage instructions")
    print(f'    `desloppify plan synthesize --stage {current_stage} --report "..."`  -- submit')
    print()
    print(colorize("  Forbidden during this phase:", "red"))
    print("    - desloppify fix / resolve / editing code / plan skip")

### File 7: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/cluster_handlers.py`

**What changes**: Update `_print_synthesis_progress` to show stage progress alongside cluster coverage.

```python
def _print_synthesis_progress(plan: dict) -> None:
    """If synthesis is pending, print stage progress and cluster coverage."""
    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
        return
    
    # Stage progress
    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import SYNTHESIS_STAGES
    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
    completed = [s for s in SYNTHESIS_STAGES if stages.get(s, {}).get("completed_at")]
    current_idx = len(completed)
    current = SYNTHESIS_STAGES[current_idx] if current_idx < len(SYNTHESIS_STAGES) else "done"
    
    parts = []
    for s in SYNTHESIS_STAGES:
        if s in [c for c in completed]:
            parts.append(f"[x] {s}")
        elif s == current:
            parts.append(f"[>] {s}")
        else:
            parts.append(f"[ ] {s}")
    print(colorize(f"  Stages: {' -> '.join(parts)}", "cyan"))
    
    # Existing cluster coverage
    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
    for c in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
        all_cluster_ids.update(c.get("finding_ids", []))
    queue_ids = [fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", []) if fid != SYNTHESIS_ID]
    organized = sum(1 for fid in queue_ids if fid in all_cluster_ids)
    total = len(queue_ids)
    print(colorize(
        f"  Coverage: {organized}/{total} findings organized into clusters.",
        "cyan",
    ))
    
    if current == "organize" or (current == "commit" and organized < total):
        print(colorize("  Continue organizing, or submit the current stage.", "dim"))
    elif current == "done":
        print(colorize("  All stages complete!", "green"))
```

**Complexity**: Low.

---

### File 8: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/docs/SKILL.md`

**What changes**: Rewrite the "Planning phase -- synthesis" section.

Replace lines 52-69 with:

```markdown
### Planning phase — synthesis

When `desloppify next` shows **"PLAN — do not proceed until complete"**, you are in
the **planning phase**. This blocks all other work. Complete all 3 stages in order.

**Stage 1 — OBSERVE** (read everything):
1. `desloppify plan synthesize` — see all findings grouped by file, with tensions highlighted
2. Read every finding. Note root causes, redundancies, and contradictions.
3. Optionally: `desloppify plan note <id> "observation"` for per-finding notes (surfaces in `next` cards)
4. Submit: `desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "your observations..."`
   - Must cite ≥80% of findings by ID or hash suffix
   - Use `--report-file obs.md` for longer reports

**Stage 2 — ORGANIZE** (decide everything):
1. Create clusters: `desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."`
2. Add findings: `desloppify plan cluster add <name> <pattern> [...]`
3. Prioritize: `desloppify plan cluster move <name> top`
4. Focus first arc: `desloppify plan focus <name>`
5. Submit: `desloppify plan synthesize --stage organize --report "grouping rationale..."`
   - At least 1 cluster with findings must exist

**Stage 3 — COMMIT** (own it):
1. Submit: `desloppify plan synthesize --stage commit --report "strategy..."`
   - Must mention every cluster name
   - This completes synthesis and unblocks the queue

**Do NOT** edit code, run `fix`, run `resolve`, or skip findings during this phase.
```

**Complexity**: Low.

---

### Edge Cases

**0-2 findings**: When `total_findings <= _ABBREVIATED_THRESHOLD` (3), all length minimums and coverage requirements are halved. With 0 findings, the observe stage accepts any report (0% of 0 = coverage met), and the organize stage doesn't require clusters (no findings to cluster). With 1-2 findings, the agent still goes through the stages but with reduced requirements (50 chars minimum observe, 50% ID coverage).

**Landscape change mid-stage**: The dampening mechanism in `sync_synthesis_needed` compares the current finding hash against the hash recorded in the most recently completed stage. If they differ, all stages are reset. This means:
- Agent completes observe, new findings arrive via scan -> stages reset, must re-observe
- Agent completes observe + organize, no finding changes -> commit proceeds normally  
- Reset only happens on actual hash changes, not on every sync call (prevents loops)

**Voluntary stage reset**: `--reset-stages` clears `meta["synthesis_stages"]` to `{}`. Does NOT remove `synthesis::pending` from the queue. The agent restarts from observe.

**Backwards compatibility**: The `--complete --strategy "..."` interface is mapped to `--stage commit --report "..."` transparently. The `--strategy "same"` shortcut is handled by reading the existing strategy and passing it through.

**Mid-flow cluster operations**: During the organize stage, the agent uses existing `plan cluster create/add/move/focus` commands. These already work and print synthesis progress hints (via `_print_synthesis_progress`). The stage report is submitted AFTER the clusters are set up -- the validation checks the plan state at submission time, not during cluster operations.

**Report stored as strategy**: The commit stage's report IS the strategy summary. It is stored in `meta["strategy_summary"]` (same field the old `--strategy` populated) so all existing code that reads `strategy_summary` works without changes.

---

### Critical Files for Implementation

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/synthesize_handlers.py` - Core logic: stage routing, validation, enriched display, report submission (most code changes)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py` - Data model: SynthesisStageReport TypedDict, SYNTHESIS_STAGES constant, ensure_plan_defaults update
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py` - Stage invalidation on landscape change with dampening logic
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py` - Stage progress data in build_synthesis_item detail dict (feeds render.py)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py` - Synthesis card rendering with stage progress display
